  "content": "1: # Snippets (aka Jetbrains \"Live Templates\")\n2: \n3: User-defined code snippets with trigger word expansion and tabstop navigation\n4: \n5: > **Status:** Planning\n6: > **Priority:** P2\n7: > **Effort:** L\n8: > **Created:** 2025-12-19\n9: > **Milestone:** 6 - Productivity\n10: > **Feature ID:** F-180\n11: \n12: ---\n13: \n14: ## Table of Contents\n15: \n16: 1. [Overview](#overview)\n17: 2. [Architecture](#architecture)\n18: 3. [Data Structures](#data-structures)\n19: 4. [Snippet Syntax](#snippet-syntax)\n20: 5. [Expansion Engine](#expansion-engine)\n21: 6. [Keybindings](#keybindings)\n22: 7. [Implementation Plan](#implementation-plan)\n23: 8. [Testing Strategy](#testing-strategy)\n24: 9. [References](#references)\n25: \n26: ---\n27: \n28: ## Overview\n29: \n30: ### Current State\n31: \n32: The editor currently has:\n33: \n34: - Multi-cursor support with synchronized editing\n35: - Configurable keybindings via YAML\n36: - Language detection from file extension\n37: - Tab/indent handling\n38: - Undo/redo with atomic multi-cursor operations\n39: \n40: However, there is no snippet system for expanding trigger words into templates with placeholders.\n41: \n42: ### Goals\n43: \n44: 1. **Trigger word expansion** - Type a prefix + Tab to expand to full snippet\n45: 2. **Tabstops** - Navigate between placeholders with Tab/Shift+Tab\n46: 3. **Placeholder text** - Default values shown and replaced on typing\n47: 4. **Mirror tabstops** - Same variable in multiple places updates together\n48: 5. **Language-scoped snippets** - Snippets active only for specific file types\n49: 6. **User configuration** - Snippets defined in YAML files\n50: 7. **Built-in snippets** - Common patterns for supported languages\n51: \n52: ### Non-Goals (This Phase)\n53: \n54: - Visual snippet picker/palette (use command palette instead)\n55: - Snippet variables like `$TM_FILENAME`, `$CURRENT_DATE` (future)\n56: - Nested snippets (snippet within snippet)\n57: - Choice/dropdown placeholders\n58: - Regex transformations on placeholders\n59: - Importing VS Code snippet format\n60: \n61: ---\n62: \n63: ## Architecture\n64: \n65: ```\n66: ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n67: │                         Snippet Expansion Flow                              │\n68: │                                                                             │\n69: │  ┌──────────────┐    ┌──────────────┐    ┌──────────────────────────────┐  │\n70: │  │  User types  │───▶│  Check for   │───▶│  Found: Expand snippet       │  │\n71: │  │  \"fn\" + Tab  │    │  trigger     │    │  Not found: Insert tab       │  │\n72: │  └──────────────┘    └──────────────┘    └───────────────┬──────────────┘  │\n73: │                                                           │                 │\n74: │                                                           ▼                 │\n75: │                           ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │\n76: │                           │             SnippetSession                    │ │\n77: │                           │  - Active tabstops                            │ │\n78: │                           │  - Current tabstop index                      │ │\n79: │                           │  - Mirror groups                              │ │\n80: │                           │  - Original cursor positions                  │ │\n81: │                  │\n84: │  │  Tab: Next   │◀───│  User edits  │◀────────┘                            │\n85: │  │  tabstop     │    │  placeholder │                                      │\n86: │  └──────────────┘    └──────────────┘                                      │\n87: │         │                   │                                               │\n88: │         │                   ▼ \n126: ### Integration with Multi-Cursor\n127: \n128: Snippets work with multi-cursor mode:\n129: \n130: ```\n131: ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n132: │  Before: Multiple cursors, each with \"fn\" before it                         │\n133: │                                                                              │\n134: │    fn|                  fn|                  fn|                             │\n135: │                                                                              │\n136: │  After Tab (expand at each cursor):                                          │\n137: │                                                                              │\n138: │    fn [name]() {        fn [name]() {        fn [name]() {                   │\n139: │        |                    |                    |                           │\n140: │    }                    }                    }                               │\n141: │                                                                              │\n142: │  [name] placeholders are synchronized across all expansions                  │\n143: └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n144: ```\n145: \n146: ---\n147: \n148: ## Data Structures\n149: \n150: ### Snippet Definition\n151: \n152: ```rust\n153: // src/snippets/mod.rs\n154: \n155: use std::collections::HashMap;\n156: use crate::syntax::LanguageId;\n157: \n158: /// A single snippet definition\n159: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n160: pub struct Snippet {\n161:     /// Unique identifier (e.g., \"fn\", \"for\", \"impl\")\n162:     pub prefix: String,\n163: \n164:     /// Display name shown in UI\n165:     pub name: String,\n166: \n167:     /// Description for documentation\n168:     pub description: Option<String>,\n169: \n170:     /// The snippet body (with tabstops)\n171:     pub body: SnippetBody,\n172: \n173:     /// Language scope (None = global)\n174:     pub scope: Option<LanguageId>,\n175: \n176:     /// Source of this snippet\n177:     pub source: SnippetSource,\n178: }\n179: \n180: /// Where the snippet was defined\n181: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]\n182: pub enum SnippetSource {\n183:     /// Built-in snippet embedded in binary\n184:     Builtin,\n185:     /// User-defined in config directory\n186:     User,\n187: }\n188: \n189: /// Parsed snippet body ready for expansion\n190: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n191: pub struct SnippetBody {\n192:     /// Body parts in order\n193:     pub parts: Vec<SnippetPart>,\n194:     /// All tabstop indices used (for validation)\n195:     pub tabstop_indices: Vec<u32>,\n196:     /// Whether this snippet has a final tabstop ($0)\n197:     pub has_final_tabstop: bool,\n198: }\n199: \n200: /// A part of a snippet body\n201: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n202: pub enum SnippetPart {\n203:     /// Literal text (no placeholders)\n204:     Text(String),\n205: \n206:     /// Simple tabstop: $1, $2, etc.\n207:     Tabstop {\n208:         index: u32,\n209:     },\n210: \n211:     /// Tabstop with placeholder: ${1:default}\n212:     Placeholder {\n213:         index: u32,\n214:         default: String,\n215:     },\n216: \n217:     /// Mirror of another tabstop (same index, no default)\n218:     Mirror {\n219:         index: u32,\n220:     },\n221: }\n222: \n223: impl SnippetBody {\n224:     /// Get all unique tabstop indices in order\n225:     pub fn tabstop_order(&self) -> Vec<u32> {\n226:         let mut indices: Vec<u32> = self.parts\n227:             .iter()\n228:             .filter_map(|p| match p {\n229:                 SnippetPart::Tabstop { index } => Some(*index),\n230:                 SnippetPart::Placeholder { index, .. } => Some(*index),\n231:                 SnippetPart::Mirror { index } => Some(*index),\n232:                 SnippetPart::Text(_) => None,\n233:             })\n234:             .collect();\n235: \n236:         indices.sort_unstable();\n237:         indices.dedup();\n238: \n239:         // Move 0 to the end (final tabstop)\n240:         if let Some(pos) = indices.iter().position(|&i| i == 0) {\n241:             indices.remove(pos);\n242:             indices.push(0);\n243:         }\n244: \n245:         indices\n246:     }\n247: }\n248: ```\n249: \n250: ### Snippet Registry\n251: \n252: ```rust\n253: // src/snippets/registry.rs\n254: \n255: use std::collections::HashMap;\n256: use std::path::PathBuf;\n257: use crate::syntax::LanguageId;\n258: \n259: /// Registry of all available snippets\n260: #[derive(Debug, Default)]\n261: pub struct SnippetRegistry {\n262:     /// Snippets indexed by (language, prefix)\n263:     /// None language = global snippets\n264:     snippets: HashMap<(Option<LanguageId>, String), Snippet>,\n265: \n266:     /// All prefixes for quick lookup during typing\n267:     /// Maps language → set of prefixes\n268:     prefix_cache: HashMap<Option<LanguageId>, Vec<String>>,\n269: }\n270: \n271: impl SnippetRegistry {\n272:     pub fn new() -> Self {\n273:         Self::default()\n274:     }\n275: \n276:     /// Load built-in snippets\n277:     pub fn load_builtins(&mut self) {\n278:         for snippet in builtin::rust_snippets() {\n279:             self.register(snippet);\n280:         }\n281:         for snippet in builtin::javascript_snippets() {\n282:             self.register(snippet);\n283:         }\n284:         for snippet in builtin::global_snippets() {\n285:             self.register(snippet);\n286:         }\n287:     }\n288: \n289:     /// Load user snippets from config directory\n290:     pub fn load_user_snippets(&mut self) -> Result<(), String> {\n291:         let snippets_dir = crate::config_paths::snippets_dir()\n292:             .ok_or(\"Could not find config directory\")?;\n293: \n294:         if !snippets_dir.exists() {\n295:             return Ok(());\n296:         }\n297: \n298:         for entry in std::fs::read_dir(&snippets_dir)\n299:             .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?\n300:             .filter_map(|e| e.ok())\n301:         {\n302:             let path = entry.path();\n303:             if path.extension().is_some_and(|ext| ext == \"yaml\" || ext == \"yml\") {\n304:                 self.load_snippet_file(&path)?;\n305:             }\n306:         }\n307: \n308:         Ok(())\n309:     }\n310: \n311:     fn load_snippet_file(&mut self, path: &PathBuf) -> Result<(), String> {\n312:         let content = std::fs::read_to_string(path)\n313:             .map_err(|e| format!(\"Failed to read {}: {}\", path.display(), e))?;\n314: \n315:         let snippets: Vec<SnippetDef> = serde_yaml::from_str(&content)\n316:             .map_err(|e| format!(\"Failed to parse {}: {}\", path.display(), e))?;\n317: \n318:         // Determine language scope from filename\n319:         let language = path.file_stem()\n320:             .and_then(|s| s.to_str())\n321:             .and_then(|name| {\n322:                 if name == \"global\" {\n323:                     None\n324:                 } else {\n325:                     LanguageId::from_name(name)\n326:                 }\n327:             });\n328: \n329:         for def in snippets {\n330:             let snippet = def.into_snippet(language)?;\n331:             self.register(snippet);\n332:         }\n333: \n334:         Ok(())\n335:     }\n336: \n337:     /// Register a snippet\n338:     pub fn register(&mut self, snippet: Snippet) {\n339:         let key = (snippet.scope, snippet.prefix.clone());\n340: \n341:         // Update prefix cache\n342:         self.prefix_cache\n343:             .entry(snippet.scope)\n344:             .or_default()\n345:             .push(snippet.prefix.clone());\n346: \n347:         self.snippets.insert(key, snippet);\n348:     }\n349: \n350:     /// Find snippet by prefix for a language\n351:     /// Checks language-specific first, then global\n352:     pub fn find(&self, prefix: &str, language: LanguageId) -> Option<&Snippet> {\n353:         // Try language-specific first\n354:         if let Some(snippet) = self.snippets.get(&(Some(language), prefix.to_string())) {\n355:             return Some(snippet);\n356:         }\n357: \n358:         // Fall back to global\n359:         self.snippets.get(&(None, prefix.to_string()))\n360:     }\n361: \n362:     /// Check if a prefix could match any snippet for a language\n363:     /// Used for optimizing when to check for expansion\n364:     pub fn has_prefix(&self, prefix: &str, language: LanguageId) -> bool {\n365:         let check = |lang: Option<LanguageId>| {\n366:             self.prefix_cache\n367:                 .get(&lang)\n368:                 .is_some_and(|prefixes| prefixes.iter().any(|p| p.starts_with(prefix)))\n369:         };\n370: \n371:         check(Some(language)) || check(None)\n372:     }\n373: \n374:     /// Get all snippets for a language (for command palette)\n375:     pub fn snippets_for_language(&self, language: LanguageId) -> Vec<&Snippet> {\n376:         let mut result: Vec<&Snippet> = self.snippets\n377:             .iter()\n378:             .filter(|((scope, _), _)| {\n379:                 scope.is_none() || *scope == Some(language)\n380:             })\n381:             .map(|(_, snippet)| snippet)\n382:             .collect();\n383: \n384:         result.sort_by(|a, b| a.prefix.cmp(&b.prefix));\n385:         result\n386:     }\n387: }\n388: ```\n389: \n390: ### Snippet Session (Active Expansion)\n391: \n392: ```rust\n393: // src/snippets/session.rs\n394: \n395: use std::collections::HashMap;\n396: use }\n469: \n470:     /// Adjust position after text insertion/deletion\n471:     pub fn adjust(&mut self, edit_line: usize, edit_col: usize, delta: isize) {\n472:         if self.line == edit_line   "content": "1: # Roadmap\n2: \n3: Planned features and improvements for rust-editor.\n4: \n5: For completed work, see [CHANGELOG.md](CHANGELOG.md).\n6: \n7: ---\n8: \n9: ## Recently Completed\n10: \n11: ### Workspace Management Improvements ✅\n12: \n13: **Design:** [feature/workspace-management.md](feature/workspace-management.md) | **Completed:** 2025-12-17 (v0.3.7)\n14: \n15: Sidebar resize, file tree keyboard navigation, and focus management:\n16: \n17: - **Sidebar resize drag** - Click-and-drag to resize sidebar width with proper cursor feedback\n18: - **File tree keyboard navigation:**\n19:   - Arrow Up/Down to navigate between items\n20:   - Arrow Right expands folders or moves to next item\n21:   - Arrow Left collapses folders or jumps to parent folder\n22:   - Enter opens files or toggles folders\n23:   - Space toggles folder expansion\n24:   - Escape returns focus to editor\n25: - **Focus management system:**\n26:   - New `FocusTarget` enum: `Editor`, `Sidebar`, `Modal`\n27:   - Explicit focus tracking in `UiState.focus`\n28:   - Click transfers focus appropriately; modals capture/release on open/close\n29:   - Global shortcuts (Cmd+Shift+A, Cmd+S, etc.) work regardless of focus state\n30: - **Cursor icon cleanup** - I-beam only over editable text, default pointer elsewhere\n31: - **Workspace root display** - Root folder shown as first item, auto-expanded on open\n32: - **New commands:** `FileTreeSelectPrevious`, `FileTreeSelectNext`, `FileTreeOpenOrToggle`, `FileTreeRefresh`\n33: - **New messages:** `WorkspaceMsg::SelectParent` for parent folder navigation\n34: \n35: Remaining: Phase 7 (file system watching), Phase 8 (tab integration, preview tabs, open file highlighting)\n36: \n37: ### CSV Viewer/Editor Phases 1-2 ✅\n38: \n39: **Design:** [feature/csv-editor.md](feature/csv-editor.md) | **Completed:** 2025-12-16 (v0.3.6)\n40: \n41: Spreadsheet-like view for CSV/TSV/PSV files with grid rendering, cell navigation, and editing:\n42: \n43: **Phase 1 (Read-Only):**\n44: - **Module structure:** `src/csv/` with model.rs, parser.rs, viewport.rs, navigation.rs, render.rs\n45: - **Data model:** `CsvData` with memory-efficient row storage (0xFA delimiter), `CsvState`, `CellPosition`\n46: - **Parsing:** RFC 4180 compliant via `csv` crate, auto-detect delimiter from content or extension\n47: - **ViewMode enum:** Added to `EditorState` for switching between Text and Csv modes\n48: - **Grid rendering:** Row numbers, column headers (A, B, C...), cell grid, selected cell highlight\n49: - **Navigation:** Arrow keys, Tab/Shift+Tab, Page Up/Down, Home/End, Cmd+Home/End\n50: - **Theme support:** `CsvTheme` with header, grid lines, selection, and number colors\n51: - **Command integration:** \"Toggle CSV View\" in command palette\n52: \n53: **Phase 2 (Cell Editing):**\n54: - **Cell editing:** Enter or typing starts editing the selected cell\n55: - **Edit buffer:** `CellEditState` tracks buffer, cursor position, original value\n56: - **Cursor navigation:** Left/Right arrows, Home/End within cell editor\n57: - **Edit operations:** Insert characters, Backspace, Delete\n58: - **Confirm/Cancel:** Enter confirms and moves down, Tab moves to next cell, Escape cancels\n59: - **Document sync:** Edits update underlying text buffer with proper CSV escaping (RFC 4180)\n60: - **Quoted fields:** Values with delimiters, quotes, or newlines properly escaped\n61: \n62: Remaining:\n63: - **Phase 3:** Copy support, virtual scrolling for large files, row/column insertion/deletion\n64: \n65: ### HiDPI Display Switching Fixes ✅\n66: \n67: **Design:** [archived/UI-SCALING-REVIEW.md](archived/UI-SCALING-REVIEW.md) | **Completed:** 2025-12-16 (v0.3.4)\n68: \n69: Fixed critical issues when switching between displays with different DPI/scale factors:\n70: \n71: - **Surface resize on display change** - The softbuffer Surface is now explicitly resized after creation\n72: - **Buffer bounds checking** - `Frame::new` validates buffer size matches dimensions, preventing panics\n73: - **Dynamic tab bar height** - Computed from glyph metrics (`line_height + padding * 2`) instead of hardcoded\n74: - **Scaled metrics throughout** - `resize()` and `set_char_width()` use properly scaled metrics\n75: - **Viewport calculation** - Now accounts for tab bar height when computing visible lines\n76: \n77: Key implementation:\n78: - `ScaledMetrics` struct with all DPI-aware layout constants\n79: - `recompute_tab_bar_height_from_line_height()` for font-metric-based sizing\n80: - `Renderer::with_scale_factor()` explicitly resizes Surface after creation\n81: - `ScaleFactorChanged` triggers `Cmd::Batch([ReinitializeRenderer, Redraw])`\n82: \n83: ### Benchmark Suite Improvements ✅\n84: \n85: **Design:** [feature/benchmark-improvements.md](feature/benchmark-improvements.md) | **Completed:** 2025-12-15\n86: \n87: Comprehensive audit and improvement of the benchmark suite in `benches/`:\n88: \n89: - **Fixed inaccurate benchmarks:**\n90:   - Rewrote `glyph_cache.rs` to use actual fontdue rasterization\n91:   - Created shared `token::rendering::blend_pixel_u8` function\n92:   - Removed duplicated blend code from `rendering.rs` and `support.rs`\n93: - **Added multi-cursor benchmarks:** Setup, insert, delete, move, select word (10-500 cursors)\n94: - **Added large file scaling:** 100k, 500k, 1M line tests in `rope_operations.rs`\n95: - **New `benches/search.rs`:** Literal search, case-insensitive, whole word, visible range\n96: - **New `benches/layout.rs`:** Line width, visible lines, char position, viewport layout\n97: - **New Makefile targets:** `bench-loop`, `bench-search`, `bench-layout`, `bench-multicursor`, `bench-large`\n98: \n99: Remaining: Phase 3 (criterion throughput metrics for CI), syntax highlighting benchmarks (when feature ready).\n100: \n101: ### Syntax Highlighting MVP ✅\n102: \n103: **Design:** [feature/syntax-highlighting.md](feature/syntax-highlighting.md) | **Completed:** 2025-12-15\n104: \n105: Tree-sitter based syntax highlighting with async background parsing:\n106: \n107: - **Core data structures:** `HighlightToken`, `LineHighlights`, `SyntaxHighlights`, `LanguageId`\n108: - **Async parser:** Background worker thread with mpsc channels\n109: - **Debouncing:** 30ms timer prevents parsing on every keystroke\n110: - **Revision tracking:** Staleness checks discard outdated parse results\n111: - **Languages:** 17 languages supported:\n112:   - Phase 1: YAML, Markdown, Rust\n113:   - Phase 2: HTML, CSS, JavaScript\n114:   - Phase 3: TypeScript, TSX, JSON, TOML\n115:   - Phase 4: Python, Go, PHP\n116:   - Phase 5: C, C++, Java, Bash\n117: - **Theme integration:** `SyntaxTheme` struct with VS Code-like default colors\n118: - **Rendering:** Highlighted text rendering with proper tab expansion\n119: - **Auto-trigger:** Parsing on document load and content changes\n120: - **No FOUC:** Old highlights preserved until new ones arrive\n121: \n122: **v0.3.3 - Phase 3-5 languages:**\n123: - Added TypeScript/TSX with `tree-sitter-typescript` (0.23)\n124: - Added JSON with `tree-sitter-json` (0.24)\n125: - Added TOML with `tree-sitter-toml-ng` (0.7)\n126: - Added Python with `tree-sitter-python` (0.25) using built-in queries\n127: - Added Go with `tree-sitter-go` (0.25) using built-in queries\n128: - Added PHP with `tree-sitter-php` (0.24) using built-in queries\n129: - Added C with `tree-sitter-c` (0.24) using built-in queries\n130: - Added C++ with `tree-sitter-cpp` (0.23) using built-in queries\n131: - Added Java with `tree-sitter-java` (0.23) using built-in queries\n132: - Added Bash with `tree-sitter-bash` (0.25) using built-in queries\n133: - Upgraded tree-sitter core from 0.24 to 0.25 (ABI compatibility)\n134: - 671 total tests passing\n135: \n136: **Performance improvements (v0.3.2):**\n137: - Implemented proper incremental parsing with `tree.edit()` and tree caching\n138: - `ParserState` caches trees per document for incremental updates\n139: - `compute_incremental_edit()` diffs old/new source to generate `InputEdit`\n140: - Added comprehensive benchmark suite (`benches/syntax.rs`)\n141: - Parse times: 67µs (100 lines) to 7.5ms (10000 lines)\n142: \n143: **Bug fixes (v0.3.1):**\n144: - Fixed tree-sitter incremental parsing bug causing misaligned highlights\n145: - Fixed flash of unstyled text during re-parsing\n146: - Fixed tab expansion in highlight token rendering\n147: \n148: Future phases: Language injection for PHP/HTML/Vue, semantic highlighting via LSP.\n149: \n150: ### File Operations – Phases 1-5 Complete ✅\n151: \n152: **Design:** [feature/file-operations.md](feature/file-operations.md) | **Completed:** 2025-12-15\n153: \n154: Comprehensive file handling with dialogs, CLI arguments, and drag-drop feedback:\n155: \n156: - **Phase 1:** Refactored `AppModel::new()` with `ViewportGeometry`, `load_config_and_theme()`, `create_initial_session()` helpers\n157: - **Phase 2:** Native file dialogs via `rfd` crate (⌘O Open File, ⇧⌘O Open Folder, ⇧⌘S Save As)\n158: - **Phase 3:** Visual feedback for file drag-hover (`DropState`, overlay rendering)\n159: - **Phase 4:** File validation (`FileOpenError`, binary detection, 50MB size limit)\n160: - **Phase 5:** CLI arguments with `clap` (`--new`, `--wait`, `--line`, `--column`)\n161: - **Duplicate file detection:** Already-open files focus existing tab\n162: - Added `workspace_root: Option<PathBuf>` to `AppModel`\n163: - 703 total tests passing\n164: \n165: ### Centralized Config Paths ✅\n166: \n167: **Completed:** 2025-12-15\n168: \n169: Single source of truth for configuration directories:\n170: \n171: - New `src/config_paths.rs` module centralizing all path logic\n172: - XDG-compliant paths on Unix (`~/.config/token-editor/`)\n173: - Windows support (`%APPDATA%\\token-editor\\`)\n174: - Moved inline tests to `tests/config.rs`\n175: - Fixed command palette navigation clamping bug\n176: - 597 total tests passing\n177: \n178: ### User Theme Configuration ✅\n179: \n180: **Completed:** 2025-12-15\n181: \n182: Theme loading from user config directories with persistence:\n183: \n184: - **Layered loading**: User config → embedded builtins\n185:   - User themes at `~/.config/token-editor/themes/*.yaml`\n186:   - 4 built-in themes (default-dark, fleet-dark, github-dark, github-light)\n187: - **Config persistence**: Theme selection saved to `~/.config/token-editor/config.yaml`\n188: - **ThemePicker improvements**: Sectioned list showing User/Builtin themes\n189: - **New APIs**: `load_theme(id)`, `list_available_themes()`, `ThemeInfo`, `ThemeSource`\n190: - **EditorConfig**: New config module for persistent editor settings\n191: - **Fallback handling**: Falls back to default-dark if saved theme not found\n192: - 15 theme tests, 556 total tests passing\n193: \n194: ### Configurable Keymapping System ✅\n195: \n196: **Design:** [archived/KEYMAPPING_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md](archived/KEYMAPPING_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md) | **Completed:** 2025-12-15\n197: \n198: Data-driven keybinding system with YAML configuration:\n199: \n200: - **Core module** (`src/keymap/`): Types, commands, bindings, context, YAML parser\n201: - **74 default bindings** in `keymap.yaml` (embedded at compile time)\n202: - **Platform-aware `cmd` modifier**: Maps to Cmd on macOS, Ctrl elsewhere\n203: - **Context-aware bindings**: `when: [\"has_selection\"]` for conditional activation\n204: - **User configuration**: Layered loading from embedded → user config\n205:   - User keymap at `~/.config/token-editor/keymap.yaml`\n206:   - `merge_bindings()` combines base + user with override semantics\n207:   - `command: Unbound` to disable default bindings\n208: - **Bridge integration**: Keymap tried first, input.rs fallback for complex behaviors\n209: - **Chord infrastructure**: `KeyAction::AwaitMore` for multi-key sequences\n210: - **Cleanup**: input.rs reduced 54% (477→220 lines), removed all redundant match arms\n211: - Tab behavior: Indent with selection, insert tab without\n212: - Escape cascade: Multi-cursor → selection → nothing\n213: - Option double-tap preserved for multi-cursor gestures\n214: - 74 keymap tests, 546 total tests passing\n215: \n216: ### GUI Phase 1 – Frame/Painter Abstraction ✅\n217: \n218: **Design:** [GUI-CLEANUP.md](archived/GUI-CLEANUP.md) | **Completed:** 2025-12-08\n219: \n220: Centralized drawing primitives for cleaner rendering code:\n221: \n222: - `Frame` struct wraps pixel buffer with safe drawing methods\n223: - `TextPainter` struct wraps fontdue + glyph cache for text\n224: - All render functions migrated (`render_*_static`, `render_perf_overlay`)\n225: - Removed legacy `draw_text()` and `draw_sparkline()` functions\n226: - Next: Phase 2 (Widget Extraction) or Phase 3 (Modal/Focus System)\n227: \n228: ### Debug Tracing & Instrumentation ✅\n229: \n230: **Design:** [tracing-instrumentation.md](archived/tracing-instrumentation.md) | **Completed:** 2025-12-08\n231: \n232: Debug instrumentation for multi-cursor state transitions:\n233: \n234: - `tracing` crate replaces `log`/`env_logger`\n235: - `CursorSnapshot` captures before/after state with diffing\n236: - `update_traced()` wrapper logs message flow and cursor changes\n237: - `assert_invariants_with_context()` for contextual assertion failures\n238: - F8 toggle for in-editor debug overlay\n239: - `make trace` runs with `RUST_LOG=debug`\n240: - Human-readable message names (e.g., `Editor::MoveCursor(Up)` instead of discriminants)\n241: \n242: ### Multi-Cursor Line Operations ✅\n243: \n244: **Completed:** 2025-12-07\n245: \n246: Fixed line-based operations to work with all cursors:\n247: \n248: - **IndentLines** - now indents lines at all cursor positions\n249: - **UnindentLines** - now unindents lines at all cursor positions\n250: - **DeleteLine** - now deletes lines at all cursor positions\n251: - **AddCursorAbove/Below** - now expands from edge cursors, not primary\n252: - Uses `lines_covered_by_all_cursors()` helper for unique line collection\n253: - 10 new tests in `tests/multi_cursor.rs`\n254: \n255: ### Test Extraction ✅\n256: \n257: **Completed:** 2025-12-07\n258: \n259: Extracted inline tests from production code to `tests/` folder:\n260: \n261: - `tests/editor_area.rs` - 7 tests (Rect, layout, hit testing)\n262: - `tests/overlay.rs` - 7 tests (anchor positioning, alpha blending)\n263: - `tests/theme.rs` - 10 tests (Color parsing, YAML themes, builtins)\n264: - Tests in `src/main.rs` (33) remain - they test binary-only `handle_key()`\n265: \n266: ### Codebase Organization ✅\n267: \n268: **Design:** [archived/ORGANIZATION-CODEBASE.md](archived/ORGANIZATION-CODEBASE.md) | **Completed:** 2025-12-06\n269: \n270: Restructured large files for maintainability:\n271: \n272: - Converted `update.rs` (2900 lines) → `update/` module directory with 5 submodules\n273: - Extracted from `main.rs`: `view.rs`, `app.rs`, `input.rs`, `perf.rs`\n274: - `main.rs` now ~20 lines (entry point) + tests\n275: - `update/mod.rs` is a pure 36-line dispatcher\n276: \n277: ### Multi-Cursor Selection Gaps ✅\n278: \n279: **Design:** [archived/MULTI_CURSOR_SELECTION_GAPS.md](archived/MULTI_CURSOR_SELECTION_GAPS.md) | **Completed:** 2025-12-06\n280: \n281: Fixed remaining selection operations to work with multiple cursors:\n282: \n283: - `merge_overlapping_selections()` - merge overlapping/touching selections\n284: - `SelectWord` - select word at each cursor with automatic merge\n285: - `SelectLine` - select line at each cursor with automatic merge\n286: - `SelectAll` - properly collapses to single full-document selection\n287: - `ExtendSelectionToPosition` - collapses multi-cursor first, then extends\n288: \n289: ### Expand/Shrink Selection ✅\n290: \n291: **Design:** [archived/TEXT-SHRINK-EXPAND-SELECTION.md](archived/TEXT-SHRINK-EXPAND-SELECTION.md) | **Completed:** 2025-12-06\n292: \n293: Progressive selection expansion with history stack:\n294: \n295: - Option+Up: Expand (cursor → word → line → all)\n296: - Option+Down: Shrink (restore previous from history)\n297: - 18 tests in `tests/expand_shrink_selection.rs`\n298: \n299: ### Multi-Cursor Movement ✅\n300: \n301: **Design:** [archived/MULTI_CURSOR_MOVEMENT.md](archived/MULTI_CURSOR_MOVEMENT.md) | **Completed:** 2025-12-06\n302: \n303: All cursor movement operations now work with multiple cursors:\n304: \n305: - Arrow keys, Home/End, Word navigation, Page Up/Down move ALL cursors\n306: - Shift+movement extends selection for ALL cursors\n307: - Cursor deduplication when cursors collide\n308: - Each cursor preserves its own `desired_column`\n309: - 10 new tests in `tests/cursor_movement.rs`\n310: \n311: ---\n312: \n313: ## Planned Features\n314: \n315: ### Workspace Management - Remaining Phases\n316: \n317: **Design:** [feature/workspace-management.md](feature/workspace-management.md)\n318: \n319: Phases 0-6 complete. permanent)\n330: - Highlight open files in tree\n331: - Sync tree selection with active tab\n332: - Support opening files in new split pane\n333: \n334: ### Command Palette & Modal System\n335: \n336: VS Code-style command palette and modal overlays:\n337: \n338: - `Cmd+Shift+P` opens command palette\n339: - Fuzzy search over all available commands\n340: - Go to Line, Find/Replace as modal dialogs\n341: - Focus capture for modal key routing\n342: - Builds on existing overlay system\n343: \n344: ### GUI Architecture Improvements\n345: \n346: **Design:** rendering ✅\n352: - Widget extraction (tab bar, gutter, text area, status bar)\n353: - Centralized coordinate space conversions\n354: - Keep existing winit/softbuffer/fontdue stack\n355: \n356: ### Undo Coalescing\n357: \n358: Group rapid consecutive edits into single undo entries:\n359: \n360: - Time-based grouping (e.g., 300ms threshold)\n361: - Coalesce consecutive character insertions\n362: - Break on cursor movement or pause\n363: - Improves undo ergonomics for normal typing\n364: \n365: ---\n366: \n367: ## Future Enhancements\n368: \n369: | Feature | Design Doc |\n370: recorder\n\n2. **During Recording:**\n   - Commands flow through `update()` normally\n   - `MacroRecorder::record()` intercepts recordable commands\n   - Non-recordable commands (file dialogs, macros) recursion depth)\n    pub player: Option<MacroPlayer>,\n\n    /// Stored macros\n    pub registry: MacroRegistry,\n}\n\nimpl MacroState {\n    /// Check if currently recording\n    pub fn MacroRecorder\n\n```rust\n// src/macros/recorder.rs\n\nuse super::types::{MacroAction, MacroId};\nuse crate::keymap::command::Command;\n\n/// Active macro recording session\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct MacroRecorder {\n    /// Target slot for this recording\n    pub target_slot: MacroId,\n\n    /// Recorded actions so far\n    pub actions: Vec<MacroAction>,\n\n    /// Buffer for batching consecutive text insertions\n    text_buffer: String,\n\n    /// Actions recorded (for status display)\n    pub action_count: usize,\n}\n\nimpl MacroRecorder {\n    /// Start a new recording session\n    pub fn new(target_slot: MacroId) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            target_slot,\n            actions: Vec::new(),\n            text_buffer: String::new(),\n            action_count: 0,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Record a command (returns true if recorded, false if filtered)\n    pub fn record(&mut self, command: &Command) -> bool {\n        // Filter non-recordable commands\n        if !is_recordable(command) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        // Batch consecutive text insertions\n        if let Command::InsertChar(c) = command {\n            self.text_buffer.push(*c);\n            self.action_count += 1;\n            return true;\n        }\n\n        // Flush text buffer before recording other commands\n        self.flush_text_buffer();\n\n        self.actions.push(MacroAction::Command(command.clone()));\n        self.action_count += 1;\n        true\n    }\n\n    /// Flush any buffered text insertions\n    fn flush_text_buffer(&mut self) {\n        if !self.text_buffer.is_empty() {\n            let text = std::mem::take(&mut self.text_buffer);\n            self.actions.push(MacroAction::InsertText(text));\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Finalize recording and return the macro\n    pub fn finalize(mut self) -> Macro {\n        self.flush_text_buffer();\n        Macro::new(self.target_slot, self.actions)\n    }\n}\n\n/// Check if a command should be recorded\nfn is_recordable(command: &Command) -> bool {\n    use Command::*;\n\n    match command {\n        // Macro commands are never recorded (prevent recursion issues)\n        RecordMacro | RecordMacroToSlot(_) | PlayMacro | PlayMacroFromSlot(_) |\n        StopMacro | SaveMacroToSlot(_) => false,\n\n        // File dialogs are not recordable (user interaction)\n        OpenFile | SaveFileAs | OpenFolder => false,\n\n        // Window management not recordable\n        Quit | ForceQuit => false,\n\n        // Theme/config changes not recordable\n        OpenThemePicker | OpenCommandPalette => false,\n\n        // Everything else is recordable\n        _ => true,\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### MacroPlayer\n\n```rust\n// src/macros/player.rs\n\nuse super::types::{Macro, MacroAction, MacroId};\nuse crate::messages::Msg;\n\n/// Maximum recursion depth for macro playback\npub const MAX_RECURSION_DEPTH: u32 = 100;\n\n/// Active macro playback session\n#[derive(Debug)]\npub struct MacroPlayer {\n    /// The macro being played\n    pub macro_ref: Macro,\n\n    /// Current action index\n    pub current_index: usize,\n\n    /// Recursion depth (for nested macro calls)\n    pub recursion_depth: u32,\n\n    /// Whether playback should stop\n    pub cancelled: bool,\n}\n\nimpl MacroPlayer {\n    /// Start playing a macro\n    pub fn new(macro_ref: Macro, recursion_depth: u32) -> Result<Self, MacroPlayError> {\n        if recursion_depth >= MAX_RECURSION_DEPTH {\n            return Err(MacroPlayError::RecursionLimit);\n        }\n\n        if macro_ref.is_empty() {\n            return Err(MacroPlayError::EmptyMacro);\n        }\n\n        Ok(Self {\n            macro_ref,\n            current_index: 0,\n            recursion_depth,\n            cancelled: false,\n        })\n    }\n\n    /// Get the next action to execute\n    pub fn next_action(&mut self) -> Option<&MacroAction> {\n        if self.cancelled {\n            return None;\n        }\n\n        if self.current_index >= self.macro_ref.actions.len() {\n            return None;\n        }\n\n        let action = &self.macro_ref.actions[self.current_index];\n        self.current_index += 1;\n        Some(action)\n    }\n\n    /// Cancel playback\n    pub fn cancel(&mut self) {\n        self.cancelled = true;\n    }\n\n    /// Check if playback is complete\n    pub fn is_complete(&self) -> bool {\n        self.cancelled || self.current_index >= self.macro_ref.actions.len()\n    }\n\n    /// Progress as percentage\n    pub fn progress(&self) -> f32 {\n        if self.macro_ref.actions.is_empty() {\n            return 100.0;\n        }\n        (self.current_index as f32 / self.macro_ref.actions.len() as f32) * 100.0\n    }\n}\n\n/// Errors that can occur during macro playback\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub enum MacroPlayError {\n    /// Macro not found in registry\n    NotFound(MacroId),\n\n    /// Recursion limit exceeded\n    RecursionLimit,\n\n    /// Macro has no actions\n    EmptyMacro,\n\n    /// Playback was cancelled by user\n    Cancelled,\n}\n```\n\n### MacroRegistry\n\n```rust\n// src/macros/registry.rs\n\nuse std::collections::HashMap;\nuse std::path::PathBuf;\nuse super::types::{Macro, MacroId};\n\n/// Storage for all macros\n#[derive(Debug, Default, serde::Serialize, serde::Deserialize)]\npub struct MacroRegistry {\n    /// Stored macros by ID\n    #[serde(flatten)]\n    macros: HashMap<MacroId, Macro>,\n}\n\nimpl MacroRegistry {\n    pub fn new() -> Self {\n        Self::default()\n    }\n\n    /// Store a macro (overwrites existing)\n    pub fn store(&mut self, macro_data: Macro) {\n        self.macros.insert(macro_data.id, macro_data);\n    }\n\n    /// Get a macro by ID\n    pub fn get(&self, id: MacroId) -> Option<&Macro> {\n        self.macros.get(&id)\n    }\n\n    /// Get a mutable reference to a macro\n    pub fn get_mut(&mut self, id: MacroId) -> Option<&mut Macro> {\n        self.macros.get_mut(&id)\n    }\n\n    /// Check if a slot has a macro\n    pub fn has(&self, id: MacroId) -> bool {\n        self.macros.contains_key(&id)\n    }\n\n    /// Get all stored macros\n    pub fn all(&self) -> impl Iterator<Item = &Macro> {\n        self.macros.values()\n    }\n\n    /// Clear a specific slot\n    pub fn clear(&mut self, id: MacroId) {\n        self.macros.remove(&id);\n    }\n\n    /// Clear all macros\n    pub fn clear_all(&mut self) {\n        self.macros.clear();\n    }\n\n    /// Load from config file\n    pub fn load() -> Result<Self, String> {\n        let path = Self::config_path()\n            .ok_or(\"Could not find config directory\")?;\n\n        if !path.exists() {\n            return Ok(Self::default());\n        }\n\n        let content = std::fs::read_to_string(&path)\n            .map_err(|e| format!(\"Failed to read macros: {}\", e))?;\n\n        serde_yaml::from_str(&content)\n            .map_err(|e| format!(\"Failed to parse macros: {}\", e))\n    }\n\n    /// Save to config file\n    pub fn save(&self) -> Result<(), String> {\n        let path = Self::config_path()\n            .ok_or(\"Could not find config directory\")?;\n\n        // Create parent directory if needed\n        if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {\n            std::fs::create_dir_all(parent)\n                .map_err(|e| format!(\"Failed to create config directory: {}\", e))?;\n        }\n\n        let content = serde_yaml::to_string(self)\n            .map_err(|e| format!(\"Failed to serialize macros: {}\", e))?;\n\n        std::fs::write(&path, content)\n            .map_err(|e| format!(\"Failed to write macros: {}\", e))\n    }\n\n    fn config_path() -> Option<PathBuf> {\n        crate::config_paths::config_dir()\n            .map(|dir| dir.join(\"macros.yaml\"))\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### MacroMsg\n\n```rust\n// src/messages.rs (additions)\n\n/// Messages for the macro system\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub enum MacroMsg {\n    /// Toggle recording (start if not recording, stop if recording)\n    ToggleRecord,\n\n    /// Start recording to a specific slot\n    RecordToSlot(MacroId),\n\n    /// Stop recording and save\n    StopRecord,\n\n    /// Play the last recorded macro\n    PlayLast,\n\n    /// Play macro from a specific slot\n    Play(MacroId),\n\n    /// Play macro N times\n    PlayRepeat { slot: MacroId, count: u32 },\n\n    /// Cancel current playback\n    CancelPlayback,\n\n    /// Save last recorded to a numbered slot\n    SaveToSlot(u8),\n\n    /// Clear a macro slot\n    Clear(MacroId),\n\n    /// Clear all macros\n    ClearAll,\n\n    /// Internal: Execute next action during playback\n    ExecuteNext,\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Recording Engine\n\n### Command Interception\n\nRecording happens at the `update()` layer, after keymap resolution:\n\n```rust\n// src/update/macros.rs\n\npub fn update_macro(model: &mut AppModel, msg: MacroMsg) -> Cmd {\n    match msg {\n        MacroMsg::ToggleRecord => {\n            if model.macro_state.is_recording() {\n                stop_recording(model)\n            } else {\n                start_recording(model, MacroId::Last)\n            }\n        }\n\n        MacroMsg::RecordToSlot(slot) => {\n            if model.macro_state.is_recording() {\n                // Already recording - ignore or switch slots?\n                Cmd::None\n            } else {\n                start_recording(model, slot)\n            }\n        }\n\n        MacroMsg::StopRecord => stop_recording(model),\n\n        MacroMsg::PlayLast => play_macro(model, MacroId::Last),\n\n        MacroMsg::Play(slot) => play_macro(model, slot),\n\n        MacroMsg::PlayRepeat { slot, count } => {\n            play_macro_repeat(model, slot, count)\n        }\n\n        MacroMsg::CancelPlayback => {\n            if let Some(player) = &mut model.macro_state.player {\n                player.cancel();\n            }\n            model.macro_state.player = None;\n            Cmd::Redraw\n        }\n\n        MacroMsg::SaveToSlot(n) => {\n            if let Some(last) = model.macro_state.registry.get(MacroId::Last).cloned() {\n                let mut saved = last;\n                saved.id = MacroId::Slot(n);\n                model.macro_state.registry.store(saved);\n                let _ = model.macro_state.registry.save();\n            }\n            Cmd::None\n        }\n\n        MacroMsg::ExecuteNext => execute_next_action(model),\n\n        _ => Cmd::None,\n    }\n}\n\nfn start_recording(model: &mut AppModel, slot: MacroId) -> Cmd {\n    model.macro_state.recorder = Some(MacroRecorder::new(slot));\n    Cmd::Redraw // Update status bar\n}\n\nfn stop_recording(model: &mut AppModel) -> Cmd {\n    if let Some(recorder) = model.macro_state.recorder.take() {\n        let macro_data = recorder.finalize();\n        if !macro_data.is_empty() {\n            model.macro_state.registry.store(macro_data);\n            let _ = model.macro_state.registry.save();\n        }\n    }\n    Cmd::Redraw\n}\n```\n\n### Recording During Normal Updates\n\n```rust\n// src/update/mod.rs (modification)\n\npub fn update(model: &mut AppModel, msg: Msg) -> Cmd {\n    // Record the command if we're recording\n    if let Some(recorder) = &mut model.macro_state.recorder {\n        if let Some(command) = msg.as_command() {\n            recorder.record(&command);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Normal update processing\n    match msg {\n        Msg::Macro(macro_msg) => macros::update_macro(model, macro_msg),\n        Msg::Editor(editor_msg) => editor::update_editor(model, editor_msg),\n        // ... etc\n    }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Playback Engine\n\n### Sequential Execution\n\n```rust\n// src/update/macros.rs\n\nfn play_macro(model: &mut AppModel, slot: MacroId) -> Cmd {\n    let recursion_depth = model.macro_state.player\n        .as_ref()\n        .map(|p| p.recursion_depth + 1)\n        .unwrap_or(0);\n\n    let Some(macro_data) = model.macro_state.registry.get(slot).cloned() else {\n        return Cmd::None; // Macro not found\n    };\n\n    match MacroPlayer::new(macro_data, recursion_depth) {\n        Ok(player) => {\n            model.macro_state.player = Some(player);\n            // Start execution chain\n            Cmd::Batch(vec![\n                Cmd::Dispatch(Msg::Macro(MacroMsg::ExecuteNext)),\n            ])\n        }\n        Err(MacroPlayError::RecursionLimit) => {\n            // Show error in status bar\n            Cmd::Redraw\n        }\n        Err(_) => Cmd::None,\n    }\n}\n\nfn execute_next_action(model: &mut AppModel) -> Cmd {\n    let Some(player) = &mut model.macro_state.player else {\n        return Cmd::None;\n    };\n\n    let Some(action) = player.next_action().cloned() else {\n        // Playback complete\n        if let Some(mut player) = model.macro_state.player.take() {\n            // Update play count\n            if let Some(macro_data) = model.macro_state.registry.get_mut(player.macro_ref.id) {\n                macro_data.play_count += 1;\n            }\n        }\n        return Cmd::Redraw;\n    };\n\n    // Convert action to commands\n    let cmd = match action {\n        MacroAction::Command(command) => {\n            // Convert command to messages\n            let msgs = command.to_msgs();\n            Cmd::Batch(msgs.into_iter().map(Cmd::Dispatch).collect())\n        }\n\n        MacroAction::InsertText(text) => {\n            // Insert each character\n            let msgs: Vec<Cmd> = text\n                .chars()\n                .map(|c| Cmd::Dispatch(Msg::Document(DocumentMsg::InsertChar(c))))\n                .collect();\n            Cmd::Batch(msgs)\n        }\n\n        MacroAction::Delay(duration) => {\n            // Future: implement delay for visualization\n            Cmd::None\n        }\n    };\n\n    // Chain to next action\n    Cmd::Batch(vec![\n        cmd,\n        Cmd::Dispatch(Msg::Macro(MacroMsg::ExecuteNext)),\n    ])\n}\n\nfn play_macro_repeat(model: &mut AppModel, slot: MacroId, count: u32) -> Cmd {\n    // Queue N playbacks\n    let msgs: Vec<Cmd> = (0..count)\n        .map(|_| Cmd::Dispatch(Msg::Macro(MacroMsg::Play(slot))))\n        .collect();\n    Cmd::Batch(msgs)\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Keybindings\n\n### Default Bindings\n\n| Action | Mac | Windows/Linux | Context |\n|--------|-----|---------------|---------|\n| Toggle record | `Cmd+Shift+R` | `Ctrl+Shift+R` | always |\n| Play last macro | `Cmd+Shift+E` | `Ctrl+Shift+E` | always |\n| Play macro N times | `Cmd+Shift+E, N` | `Ctrl+Shift+E, N` | always |\n| Play from slot 1-9 | `Cmd+Ctrl+1-9` | `Ctrl+Alt+1-9` | always |\n| Save to slot 1-9 | `Cmd+Shift+1-9` | `Ctrl+Shift+1-9` | always |\n| Cancel playback | `Escape` | `Escape` | macro_playing |\n\n### Keymap Configuration\n\n```yaml\n# keymap.yaml additions\n\n- key: \"cmd+shift+r\"\n  command: RecordMacro\n  when: [\"in_editor\"]\n\n- key: \"cmd+shift+e\"\n  command: PlayMacro\n  when: [\"in_editor\"]\n\n- key: \"cmd+ctrl+1\"\n  command: PlayMacroFromSlot\n  args: { slot: 1 }\n  when: [\"in_editor\"]\n\n# ... slots 2-9 ...\n\n- key: \"cmd+shift+1\"\n  command: SaveMacroToSlot\n  args: { slot: 1 }\n  when: [\"in_editor\"]\n\n# ... slots 2-9 ...\n\n- key: \"escape\"\n  command: CancelMacro\n  when: [\"macro_playing\"]\n```\n\n### Status Bar Integration\n\n```rust\n// Status bar shows recording state\n\nfn macro_status_segment(macro_state: &MacroState) -> Option<StatusSegment> {\n    if let Some(recorder) = &macro_state.recorder {\n        Some(StatusSegment::new(\n            format!(\"● REC ({})\", recorder.action_count),\n            StatusStyle::Error, // Red for recording\n        ))\n    } else if let Some(player) = &macro_state.player {\n        Some(StatusSegment::new(\n            format!(\"▶ MACRO {:.0}%\", player.progress()),\n            StatusStyle::Info,\n        ))\n    } else {\n        None\n    }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Implementation Plan\n\n### Phase 1: Core Types & Recording\n\n**Effort:** M (3-5 days)\n\n- [ ] Create `src/macros/` module structure\n- [ ] Implement `MacroAction`, `Macro`, `MacroId` types\n- [ ] Implement `MacroRecorder` with command filtering\n- [ ] Add `MacroState` to `AppModel`\n- [ ] Add `MacroMsg` to `messages.rs`\n- [ ] Implement start/stop recording in `update/macros.rs`\n- [ ] Wire up `Cmd+Shift+R` keybinding\n- [ ] Add \"● REC\" indicator to status bar\n\n**Test:** Record typing + cursor movements, verify actions captured.\n\n### Phase 2: Playback Engine\n\n**Effort:** M (3-5 days)\n\n- [ ] Implement `MacroPlayer` with action iteration\n- [ ] Implement `execute_next_action()` with command dispatch\n- [ ] Add recursion depth tracking\n- [ ] Handle `InsertText` batched playback\n- [ ] Wire up `Cmd+Shift+E` for playback\n- [ ] Add escape cancellation during playback\n- [ ] Add playback progress to status bar\n\n**Test:** Record 5 actions, play back, verify document matches manual execution.\n\n### Phase 3: Registry & Persistence\n\n**Effort:** S (1-2 days)\n\n- [ ] Implement `MacroRegistry` with HashMap storage\n- [ ] Add YAML serialization for macros\n- [ ] Load/save from `~/.config/token-editor/macros.yaml`\n- [ ] Load registry on startup\n- [ ] Save on recording stop\n\n**Test:** Record macro, quit editor, relaunch, play macro successfully.\n\n### Phase 4: Numbered Slots\n\n**Effort:** S (1-2 days)\n\n- [ ] Implement `SaveMacroToSlot` command\n- [ ] Implement `PlayMacroFromSlot` command\n- [ ] Add keybindings for slots 1-9\n- [ ] Command palette entries for macro operations\n\n**Test:** Save macro to slot 3, clear last macro, play from slot 3.\n\n### Phase 5: Multi-Cursor & Edge Cases\n\n**Effort:** M (3-5 days)\n\n- [ ] Test recording with multiple cursors\n- [ ] Test playback with multiple cursors\n- [ ] Handle edge cases (empty document, end of file)\n- [ ] Handle errors gracefully (show in status bar)\n- [ ] Prevent recording during playback\n- [ ] Add repeat count support (play N times)\n\n**Test:** Record multi-cursor edit, play back, verify all cursor positions edited.\n\n### Phase 6: Polish\n\n**Effort:** S (1-2 days)\n\n- [ ] Visual feedback improvements (color, animation?)\n- [ ] Performance optimization for long macros\n- [ ] Documentation\n- [ ] Integration tests\n\n**Test:** Record 100+ action macro, play without lag.\n\n---\n\n## Testing Strategy\n\n### Unit Tests\n\n```rust\n// tests/macros.rs\n\n#[test]\nfn test_recorder_filters_macro_commands() {\n    let mut recorder = MacroRecorder::new(MacroId::Last);\n\n    // Regular commands are recorded\n    assert!(recorder.record(&Command::MoveCursorUp));\n    assert!(recorder.record(&Command::InsertChar('a')));\n\n    // Macro commands are filtered\n    assert!(!recorder.record(&Command::RecordMacro));\n    assert!(!recorder.record(&Command::PlayMacro));\n\n    assert_eq!(recorder.action_count, 2);\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn test_recorder_batches_text() {\n    let mut recorder = MacroRecorder::new(MacroId::Last);\n\n    recorder.record(&Command::InsertChar('h'));\n    recorder.record(&Command::InsertChar('e'));\n    recorder.record(&Command::InsertChar('l'));\n    recorder.record(&Command::InsertChar('l'));\n    recorder.record(&Command::InsertChar('o'));\n\n    let macro_data = recorder.finalize();\n\n    // Should batch into single InsertText\n    assert_eq!(macro_data.actions.len(), 1);\n    assert!(matches!(\n        &macro_data.actions[0],\n        MacroAction::InsertText(s) if s == \"hello\"\n    ));\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn test_recorder_flushes_on_non_text() {\n    let mut recorder = MacroRecorder::new(MacroId::Last);\n\n    recorder.record(&Command::InsertChar('a'));\n    recorder.record(&Command::InsertChar('b'));\n    recorder.record(&Command::MoveCursorDown);\n    recorder.record(&Command::InsertChar('c'));\n\n    let macro_data = recorder.finalize();\n\n    assert_eq!(macro_data.actions.len(), 3);\n    // InsertText(\"ab\"), MoveCursorDown, InsertText(\"c\")\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn test_player_respects_recursion_limit() {\n    let macro_data = Macro::new(MacroId::Last, vec![\n        MacroAction::Command(Command::MoveCursorDown),\n    ]);\n\n    // Should fail at recursion limit\n    let result = MacroPlayer::new(macro_data.clone(), MAX_RECURSION_DEPTH);\n    assert!(matches!(result, Err(MacroPlayError::RecursionLimit)));\n\n    // Should succeed below limit\n    let result = MacroPlayer::new(macro_data, MAX_RECURSION_DEPTH - 1);\n    assert!(result.is_ok());\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn test_registry_persistence() {\n    let mut registry = MacroRegistry::new();\n\n    let macro_data = Macro::new(MacroId::Slot(1), vec![\n        MacroAction::Command(Command::MoveCursorUp),\n    ]);\n    registry.store(macro_data);\n\n    // Serialize and deserialize\n    let yaml = serde_yaml::to_string(&registry).unwrap();\n    let restored: MacroRegistry = serde_yaml::from_str(&yaml).unwrap();\n\n    assert!(restored.has(MacroId::Slot(1)));\n    assert_eq!(restored.get(MacroId::Slot(1)).unwrap().actions.len(), 1);\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn test_empty_macro_rejected() {\n    let macro_data = Macro::new(MacroId::Last, vec![]);\n\n    let result = MacroPlayer::new(macro_data, 0);\n    assert!(matches!(result, Err(MacroPlayError::EmptyMacro)));\n}\n```\n\n### Integration Tests\n\n```rust\n// tests/macro_integration.rs\n\n#[test]\nfn test_record_and_playback() {\n    let mut model = test_model_with_content(\"hello\");\n    position_cursor(&mut model, 0, 5); // End of \"hello\"\n\n    // Start recording\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Macro(MacroMsg::ToggleRecord));\n    assert!(model.macro_state.is_recording());\n\n    // Type \" world\"\n    for c in \" world\".chars() {\n        update(&mut model, Msg::Document(DocumentMsg::InsertChar(c)));\n    }\n\n    // Stop recording\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Macro(MacroMsg::ToggleRecord));\n    assert!(!model.macro_state.is_recording());\n\n    // Clear and reset\n    set_content(&mut model, \"foo\");\n    position_cursor(&mut model, 0, 3);\n\n    // Play macro\n    run_until_complete(&mut model, Msg::Macro(MacroMsg::PlayLast));\n\n    assert_eq!(get_content(&model), \"foo world\");\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn test_macro_with_cursor_movement() {\n    let mut model = test_model_with_content(\"line1\\nline2\\nline3\");\n    position_cursor(&mut model, 0, 0);\n\n    // Record: go down, insert \">> \"\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Macro(MacroMsg::ToggleRecord));\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::MoveCursor(Direction::Down)));\n    for c in \">> \".chars() {\n        update(&mut model, Msg::Document(DocumentMsg::InsertChar(c)));\n    }\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Macro(MacroMsg::ToggleRecord));\n\n    // Reset cursor\n    position_cursor(&mut model, 0, 0);\n\n    // Play twice\n    run_until_complete(&mut model, Msg::Macro(MacroMsg::PlayRepeat {\n        slot: MacroId::Last,\n        count: 2,\n    }));\n\n    let content = get_content(&model);\n    assert!(content.contains(\">> line2\"));\n    assert!(content.contains(\">> >> line3\")); // Second play adds another prefix\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn test_escape_cancels_playback() {\n    let mut model = test_model_with_content(\"test\");\n\n    // Create a long macro\n    let actions: Vec<MacroAction> = (0..100)\n        .map(|_| MacroAction::Command(Command::MoveCursorRight))\n        .collect();\n    model.macro_state.registry.store(Macro::new(MacroId::Last, actions));\n\n    // Start playing\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Macro(MacroMsg::PlayLast));\n    assert!(model.macro_state.is_playing());\n\n    // Execute a few steps\n    for _ in 0..5 {\n        update(&mut model, Msg::Macro(MacroMsg::ExecuteNext));\n    }\n\n    // Cancel\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Macro(MacroMsg::CancelPlayback));\n    assert!(!model.macro_state.is_playing());\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn test_multi_cursor_macro() {\n    let mut model = test_model_with_content(\"aaa\\nbbb\\nccc\");\n\n    // Add cursors on each line\n    position_cursor(&mut model, 0, 0);\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::AddCursorBelow));\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::AddCursorBelow));\n\n    // Record: move to end, insert \"!\"\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Macro(MacroMsg::ToggleRecord));\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::MoveToLineEnd));\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Document(DocumentMsg::InsertChar('!')));\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Macro(MacroMsg::ToggleRecord));\n\n    // Reset\n    set_content(&mut model, \"xxx\\nyyy\\nzzz\");\n    position_cursor(&mut model, 0, 0);\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::AddCursorBelow));\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::AddCursorBelow));\n\n    // Play\n    run_until_complete(&mut model, Msg::Macro(MacroMsg::PlayLast));\n\n    assert_eq!(get_content(&model), \"xxx!\\nyyy!\\nzzz!\");\n}\n```\n\n### Manual Testing Checklist\n\n- [ ] Record simple typing macro\n- [ ] Record cursor movement + editing macro\n- [ ] Play back immediately after recording\n- [ ] Play back after editor restart\n- [ ] Save to numbered slot\n- [ ] Play from numbered slot\n- [ ] Record with multi-cursor active\n- [ ] Play with multi-cursor active\n- [ ] Escape cancels recording\n- [ ] Escape cancels playback\n- [ ] Status bar shows recording indicator\n- [ ] Status bar shows playback progress\n- [ ] Empty macro not saved\n- [ ] Rapid key repeat during playback\n\n---\n\n## References\n\n### Internal Docs\n\n- [Feature: Snippets](./snippets.md) - Similar expansion pattern\n- [Keymapping System](../archived/KEYMAPPING_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md) - Command enum integration\n- [Message Architecture](../dev/contracts-update.md) - Msg/Cmd pattern\n\n### External Resources\n\n- [Vim Macros](https://vim.fandom.com/wiki/Macros) - Classic macro UX\n- [Emacs Keyboard Macros](https://www.gnu.org/software/emacs/manual/html_node/emacs/Keyboard-Macros.html)\n- [VS Code Macro Extension](https://marketplace.visualstudio.com/items?itemName=geddski.macros)\n\n---\n\n## Open Questions\n\n1. **Should macros record at the command level or keystroke level?**\n   - Command level is cleaner but loses some fidelity\n   - Decision: Command level (matches existing architecture)\n\n2. **How to handle commands that fail during playback?**\n   - Option A: Stop playback on first error\n   - Option B: Continue, log errors\n   - Recommendation: Option A for safety\n\n3. **Should there be a \"step through\" debug mode for macros?**\n   - Useful for debugging but adds complexity\n   - Decision: Defer to future phase\n\n---\n\n## Appendix\n\n### Design Decisions\n\n| Decision | Options Considered | Chosen | Rationale |\n|----------|-------------------|--------|-----------|\n| Storage format | Binary, JSON, YAML | YAML | Human-readable, consistent with other config |\n| Recording layer | Keystroke, Command, Message | Command | Clean abstraction, works with keymap system |\n| Slot system | Named only, Numbered only, Both | Numbered + last | Simple UX, vim-like familiarity |\n| Execution model | Immediate all, Async chunked | Async chunked | Allows cancellation, visual feedback |\n\n### Changelog\n\n| Date | Change |\n|------|--------|\n| 2025-12-20 | Initial draft |\n"
}

It's the behavioral heart of an editor—everything that makes cursor movement \"feel right.\"\n11: \n12: Token already has this implicitly spread across `model/editor.rs`, `update/editor.rs`, and the tests. This document explores what a clean separation would look like.\n13: \n14: ---\n15: \n16: ## What \"Text Physics\" Includes\n17: \n18: ### State\n19: \n20: ```rust\n21: pub struct TextViewState {\n22:     // Multi-cursor support\n23:     pub cursors: Vec<Cursor>,\n24:     pub selections: Vec<Selection>,\n25:     pub active_cursor: usize,\n26:     \n27:     // Viewport (in line/column space, not pixels)\n28:     pub viewport: Viewport,  // top_line, left_column, visible_lines, visible_columns\n29:     \n30:     // Behavioral state\n31:     pub scroll_mode: ScrollMode,  // CursorLocked, FreeBrowse, RevealPending\n32:     pub rectangle_selection: Option<RectangleSelectionState>,\n33:     pub selection_history: Vec<SelectionSnapshot>,  // for expand/shrink\n34: }\n35: ```\n36: \n37: ### Invariants (Must Always Hold)\n38: \n39: ```rust\n40: // CRITICAL: Parallel array invariant\n41: assert!(cursors.len() == selections.len());\n42: \n43: // CRITICAL: Cursor/selection head correspondence  \n44: for i in 0..cursors.len() {\n45:     assert!(cursors[i].to_position() == selections[i].head);\n46: }\n47: \n48: // Viewport: cursor should be in safe zone after any movement\n49: assert!(cursor_in_safe_zone(primary_cursor, viewport, margins));\n50: \n51: // Selection normalization: start <= end\n52: for sel in &selections {\n53:     assert!(sel.start() <= sel.end());\n54: }\n55: ```\n56: \n57: ### Operations (Commands)\n58: \n59: ```rust\n60: pub enum Motion {\n61:     CharLeft, CharRight,\n62:     LineUp, LineDown,\n63:     WordLeft, WordRight,\n64:     LineStart, LineEnd,      // Home/End\n65:     LineStartSmart,          // Toggle between column 0 and first non-whitespace\n66:     PageUp, PageDown,\n67:     DocumentStart, DocumentEnd,\n68:     // With wrapping: VisualLineUp, VisualLineDown\n69: }\n70: \n71: pub enum SelectionMode {\n72:     Move,       // Clear selection, move cursor\n73:     Extend,     // Shift+movement: extend selection\n74:     AddCursor,  // Add new cursor (Cmd+Click, Option+Option+Arrow)\n75: }\n76: \n77: pub enum SelectionCommand {\n78:     SelectWord,\n79:     SelectLine,\n80:     SelectAll,\n81:     ExpandSelection,   // word → brackets → block → all\n82:     ShrinkSelection,   // reverse via history stack\n83:     SelectNextOccurrence,\n84:     MergeOverlappingSelections,\n85: }\n86: ```\n87: \n88: ---\n89: \n90: ## What's Outside the Physics Layer\n91: \n92: | Layer | Responsibility | Example |\n93: |-------|---------------|---------|\n94: | **Buffer** | Text storage, edits, undo/redo | `Document`, ropey `Rope` |\n95: | **Input** | Raw events → commands | winit → `Motion` + `SelectionMode` |\n96: | **Render** | State → pixels | Glyphs, themes, GPU |\n97: | **App** | Tabs, splits, files | `EditorArea`, workspace |\n98: \n99: ---\n100: \n101: ## The Buffer Abstraction\n102: \n103: The physics layer needs to query the buffer but shouldn't know its implementation:\n104: \n105: ```rust\n106: pub trait TextBuffer {\n107:     fn line_count(&self) -> usize;\n108:     fn line_length(&self, line: usize) -> usize;\n109:     \n110:     // For word movement\n111:     fn char_type_at(&self, line: usize, column: usize) -> Option<CharType>;\n112:     \n113:     // For smart home\n114:     fn first_non_whitespace_column(&self, line: usize) -> usize;\n115:     \n116:     // Optional: for advanced features\n117:     fn line_text(&self, line: usize) -> Option<&str>;\n118: }\n119: ```\n120: \n121: Token's `Document` would implement this trait.\n122: \n123: ---\n124: \n125: ## The Physics API\n126: \n127: ```rust\n128: impl TextViewState {\n129:     /// Apply a motion command, updating cursors, selections, and viewport\n130:     pub fn apply_motion<B: TextBuffer>(\n131:         &mut self,\n132:         buffer: &B,\n133:         motion: Motion,\n134:         selection_mode: SelectionMode,\n135:         reveal_mode: ScrollRevealMode,\n136:         metrics: &ViewMetrics,\n137:     ) {\n138:         // 1. Move each cursor according to motion\n139:         // 2. Update selections according to selection_mode\n140:         // 3. Deduplicate colliding cursors\n141:         // 4. Scroll viewport to keep primary cursor visible\n142:     }\n143:     \n144:     /// Handle mouse click (single, double, triple)\n145:     pub fn handle_click<B: TextBuffer>(\n146:         &mut self,\n147:         buffer: &B,\n148:         position: Position,\n149:         click_count: u8,\n150:         modifiers: Modifiers,\n151:     );\n152:     \n153:     /// Handle mouse drag for selection extension\n154:     pub fn handle_drag<B: TextBuffer>(\n155:         &mut self,\n156:         buffer: &B,\n157:         position: Position,\n158:         metrics: &ViewMetrics,\n159:     );\n160:     \n161:     /// Free scroll (mouse wheel, scrollbar)\n162:     pub fn scroll(&mut self, delta_lines: isize, delta_columns: isize);\n163:     \n164:     /// Compute visible line range for rendering\n165:     pub fn visible_lines(&self) -> Range<usize>;\n166: }\n167: ```\n168: \n169: ---\n170: \n171: ## Current Token Implementation\n172: \n173: Token already has most of this, but distributed:\n174: \n175: | Concept | Current Location |\n176: |---------|-----------------|\n177: | Cursor/Selection state | `model/editor.rs` |\n178: | Viewport state | `model/editor.rs` (embedded in `EditorState`) |\n179: | Movement primitives | `model/editor.rs` (`move_cursor_*_at`) |\n180: | All-cursors wrappers | `model/editor.rs` (`move_all_cursors_*`) |\n181: | Selection commands | `update/editor.rs` |\n182: | Scroll logic | `model/editor.rs` (`ensure_cursor_visible_*`) |\n183: | Invariant spec | `EDITOR_UI_REFERENCE.md` (chapters 4, 5) |\n184: | Conformance tests | `tests/cursor_movement.rs`, `tests/selection.rs` |\n185: \n186: ---\n187: \n188: ## When to Formalize This Separation\n189: \n190: Consider extracting a true \"text physics\" crate if:\n191: \n192: 1. **Multiple frontends** — TUI version, web version, or embedding in other apps\n193: 2. **Reuse requests** — Others want Token's cursor \"feel\" in their projects  \n194: 3. **Complexity growth** — BiDi, complex folding, or structural selections that benefit from isolation\n195: 4. **Testing friction** — Physics tests need too much app scaffolding\n196: \n197: For now, the conceptual separation (knowing what belongs where) is sufficient.\n198: \n199: ---\n200: \n201: ## Appendix: Proposed Addition to EDITOR_UI_REFERENCE.md\n202: \n203: Consider adding a new chapter:\n204: \n205: ### Chapter 15: System Invariants and Constraints\n206: \n207: **15.1 Cursor-Selection Invariants**\n208: ```\n209: cursors.len() == selections.len()\n210: cursors[i].to_position() == selections[i].head\n211: ```\n212: \n213: **15.2 Selection Normalization**\n214: - `start()` always returns the earlier position\n215: - `end()` always returns the later position  \n216: - `is_reversed()` indicates if anchor > head\n217: \n218: **15.3 Viewport Safety Zone**\n219: - After any cursor movement, primary cursor must be within `(margins.top, viewport.height - margins.bottom)`\n220: - Horizontal equivalent for horizontal scrolling\n221: \n222: **15.4 Multi-Cursor Deduplication**  \n223: - After any operation, no two cursors may occupy the same `(line, column)`\n224: - When cursors collide, keep the one with lower index\n225: \n226: **15.5 Selection Merging**\n227: - Overlapping selections `[a, b)` and `[c, d)` where `c <= b` merge to `[a, max(b, d))`\n228: - Adjacent selections (touching but not overlapping) also merge",
  "absolutePath": "~/code/rust-editor/docs/feature/TEXT_PHYSICS_LAYER.md"
}
