  "content": "1: # Markdown Preview\n2: \n3: Split view with source on left, rendered markdown on right with synchronized scrolling\n4: \n5: > **Status:** Planning\n6: > **Priority:** P2\n7: > **Effort:** L\n8: > **Created:** 2025-12-19\n9: > **Milestone:** 5 - Insight Tools\n10: > **Feature ID:** F-170\n11: \n12: ---\n13: \n14: ## Table of Contents\n15: \n16: 1. [Overview](#overview)\n17: 2. [Architecture](#architecture)\n18: 3. [Data Structures](#data-structures)\n19: 4. [Rendering](#rendering)\n20: 5. [Keybindings](#keybindings)\n21: 6. [Implementation Plan](#implementation-plan)\n22: 7. [Testing Strategy](#testing-strategy)\n23: 8. [References](#references)\n24: \n25: ---\n26: \n27: ## Overview\n28: \n29: ### Current State\n30: \n31: The editor currently has:\n32: \n33: - Syntax highlighting for Markdown files (tree-sitter-md)\n34: - Split view infrastructure (`EditorArea`, `LayoutNode`, `SplitContainer`)\n35: - CPU-based rendering with fontdue + softbuffer\n36: - Theme system with customizable colors\n37: \n38: However, there is no rendered preview of Markdown content. Users see only the raw Markdown source with syntax highlighting.\n39: \n40: ### Goals\n41: \n42: 1. **Live preview** - Render Markdown as formatted text in a preview pane\n43: 2. **Split view integration** - Preview appears as a special split alongside source\n44: 3. **Synchronized scrolling** - Scroll position synced between source and preview\n45: 4. **Basic formatting** - Headers, bold, italic, code, lists, blockquotes, links\n46: 5. **Theme integration** - Preview respects current editor theme colors\n47: 6. **Live updates** - Preview updates as user types (debounced)\n48: \n49: ### Non-Goals (This Phase)\n50: \n51: - Image rendering (show placeholder or alt text)\n52: - External link handling (no browser opening)\n53: - Table rendering (show as plain text)\n54: - LaTeX/math rendering\n55: - Mermaid/diagram rendering\n56: - Custom CSS styling\n57: - Print/export to PDF\n58: \n59: ---\n60: \n61: ## Architecture\n62: \n63: ```\n64: ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n65: │                        Markdown Preview Architecture                        │\n66: │                                                                             │\n67: │  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐  │\n68: │  │                         EditorArea Layout                             │  │\n69: │  │  ┌────────────────────────┬────────────────────────┐                 │  │\n70: │  │  │    Source Editor       │    Preview Pane        │                 │  │\n71: │  │  │   (EditorGroup)        │   (PreviewPane)        │                 │  │\n72: │  │  │                        │                        │                 │  │\n73: │  │  │  # Heading             │  Heading               │                 │  │\n74: │  │  │  Some **bold** text    │  Some bold text        │                 │  │\n75: │  │  │                        │                        │                 │  │\n76: │  │  │  - Item 1              │  - Item 1              │                 │  │\n77: │  │  │  - Item 2              │  - Item 2              │                 │  │\n78: │  │  │                        │                        │                 │  │\n79: │  │  └────────────────────────┴────────────────────────┘                 │  │\n80: │  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘  │\n81: │                                                                             │\n82: │  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐  │\n83: │  │                         Data Flow                                     │  │\n84: │  │                                                                       │  │\n85: │  │  Document.buffer ───▶ MarkdownParser ───▶ MarkdownAst ───▶ Renderer  │  │\n86: │  │       │                    │                   │              │       │  │\n87: │  │       │                    │                   │              ▼       │  │\n88: │  │  (on edit)            (debounced)         (cached)        Preview    │  │\n89: │  │                                                            Pane      │  │\n90: │  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘  │\n91: └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n92: ```\n93: \n94: ### Module Structure\n95: \n96: ```\n97: src/\n98: ├── markdown/                    # NEW MODULE\n99: │   ├── mod.rs                   # Public exports\n100: │   ├── parser.rs                # Markdown → AST conversion (pulldown-cmark)\n101: │   ├── ast.rs                   # MarkdownAst, MarkdownNode types\n102: │   ├── layout.rs                # AST → LayoutLines conversion\n103: │   └── render.rs                # Rendering formatted text\n104: ├── model/\n105: │   ├── editor.rs                # + ViewMode::MarkdownPreview variant\n106: │   └── preview.rs               # NEW: PreviewPane, PreviewState\n107: ├── update/\n108: │   └── preview.rs               # NEW: Preview update handler\n109: └── view/\n110:     └── preview.rs               # NEW: Preview pane rendering\n111: ```\n112: \n113: ### Scroll Synchronization\n114: \n115: ```\n116: Source Editor                    Preview Pane\n117: ┌──────────────┐                ┌──────────────┐\n118: │              │                │              │\n119: │   Line 1     │ ◄───────────► │   Para 1     │  (mapped via AST positions)\n120: │   Line 2     │                │              │\n121: │   Line 3     │                │   Para 2     │\n122: │   ──────────►│ scroll         │   ──────────►│\n123: │   Line 4     │                │   Para 3     │\n124: │   Line 5     │                │              │\n125: │              │                │              │\n126: └──────────────┘                └──────────────┘\n127: \n128: Mapping: Source line 3 (heading) → Preview element \"Para 2\" (rendered heading)\n129: ```\n130: \n131: ---\n132: \n133: ## Data Structures\n134: \n135: ### Markdown AST\n136: \n137: ```rust\n138: // src/markdown/ast.rs\n139: \n140: /// A node in the parsed Markdown AST\n141: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n142: pub enum MarkdownNode {\n143:     /// Document root\n144:     Document(Vec<MarkdownNode>),\n145: \n146:     /// Heading with level (1-6) and inline content\n147:     Heading {\n148:         level: u8,\n149:         content: Vec<InlineNode>,\n150:         source_line: usize,\n151:     },\n152: \n153:     /// Paragraph with inline content\n154:     Paragraph {\n155:         content: Vec<InlineNode>,\n156:         source_line: usize,\n157:     },\n158: \n159:     /// Code block with optional language\n160:     CodeBlock {\n161:         language: Option<String>,\n162:         content: String,\n163:         source_line: usize,\n164:     },\n165: \n166:     /// Blockquote containing nested nodes\n167:     Blockquote {\n168:         content: Vec<MarkdownNode>,\n169:         source_line: usize,\n170:     },\n171: \n172:     /// Unordered list\n173:     UnorderedList {\n174:         items: Vec<ListItem>,\n175:         source_line: usize,\n176:     },\n177: \n178:     /// Ordered list\n179:     OrderedList {\n180:         start: usize,\n181:         items: Vec<ListItem>,\n182:         source_line: usize,\n183:     },\n184: \n185:     /// Horizontal rule\n186:     HorizontalRule {\n187:         source_line: usize,\n188:     },\n189: \n190:     /// Thematic break (blank lines, used for spacing)\n191:     ThematicBreak,\n192: }\n193: \n194: /// A list item containing block-level content\n195: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n196: pub struct ListItem {\n197:     pub content: Vec<MarkdownNode>,\n198:     pub source_line: usize,\n199: }\n200: \n201: /// Inline content within paragraphs and headings\n202: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n203: pub enum InlineNode {\n204:     /// Plain text\n205:     Text(String),\n206:     /// Bold text\n207:     Strong(Vec<InlineNode>),\n208:     /// Italic text\n209:     Emphasis(Vec<InlineNode>),\n210:     /// Inline code\n211:     Code(String),\n212:     /// Link with text and URL\n213:     Link { text: Vec<InlineNode>, url: String },\n214:     /// Soft break (single newline in source)\n215:     SoftBreak,\n216:     /// Hard break (double newline or trailing spaces)\n217:     HardBreak,\n218: }\n219: \n220: impl MarkdownNode {\n221:     /// Get the source line this node starts on\n222:     pub fn source_line(&self) -> Option<usize> {\n223:         match self {\n224:             MarkdownNode::Document(_) => None,\n225:             MarkdownNode::Heading { source_line, .. } => Some(*source_line),\n226:             MarkdownNode::Paragraph { source_line, .. } => Some(*source_line),\n227:             MarkdownNode::CodeBlock { source_line, .. } => Some(*source_line),\n228:             MarkdownNode::Blockquote { source_line, .. } => Some(*source_line),\n229:             MarkdownNode::UnorderedList { source_line, .. } => Some(*source_line),\n230:             MarkdownNode::OrderedList { source_line, .. } => Some(*source_line),\n231:             MarkdownNode::HorizontalRule { source_line } => Some(*source_line),\n232:             MarkdownNode::ThematicBreak => None,\n233:         }\n234:     }\n235: }\n236: ```\n237: \n238: ### Markdown AST Container\n239: \n240: ```rust\n241: // src/markdown/ast.rs\n242: \n243: /// Parsed markdown document with source position mapping\n244: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n245: pub struct MarkdownAst {\n246:     /// Root nodes of the document\n247:     pub nodes: Vec<MarkdownNode>,\n248:     /// Revision of source document this was parsed from\n249:     pub source_revision: u64,\n250: }\n251: \n252: impl MarkdownAst {\n253:     /// Build line mapping from source lines to preview elements\n254:     pub fn build_line_map(&self) -> SourcePreviewMap {\n255:         let mut map = SourcePreviewMap::new();\n256:         let mut preview_y = 0;\n257: \n258:         self.map_nodes(&self.nodes, &mut map, &mut preview_y);\n259:         map\n260:     }\n261: \n262:     fn map_nodes(\n263:         &self,\n264:         nodes: &[MarkdownNode],\n265:         map: &mut SourcePreviewMap,\n266:         preview_y: &mut usize,\n267:     ) {\n268:         for node in nodes {\n269:             if let Some(source_line) = node.source_line() {\n270:                 map.add(source_line, *preview_y);\n271:             }\n272:             *preview_y += self.node_height(node);\n273:         }\n274:     }\n275: \n276:     /// Estimate rendered height of a node (in lines)\n277:     fn node_height(&self, node: &MarkdownNode) -> usize {\n278:         match node {\n279:             MarkdownNode::Heading { .. } => 2, // Heading + spacing\n280:             MarkdownNode::Paragraph { .. } => 2, // Paragraph + spacing\n281:             MarkdownNode::CodeBlock { content, .. } => {\n282:                 content.lines().count() + 2 // Code + borders\n283:             }\n284:             MarkdownNode::Blockquote { content, .. } => {\n285:                 content.iter().map(|n| self.node_height(n)).sum::<usize>() + 1\n286:             }\n287:             MarkdownNode::UnorderedList { items, .. }\n288:             | MarkdownNode::OrderedList { items, .. } => {\n289:                 items.len() + 1 // Items + spacing\n290:             }\n291:             MarkdownNode::HorizontalRule { .. } => 1,\n292:             MarkdownNode::ThematicBreak => 1,\n293:             MarkdownNode::Document(nodes) => {\n294:                 nodes.iter().map(|n| self.node_height(n)).sum()\n295:             }\n296:         }\n297:     }\n298: }\n299: \n300: /// Bidirectional mapping between source lines and preview positions\n301: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]\n302: pub struct SourcePreviewMap {\n303:     /// source_line → preview_y\n304:     source_to_preview: Vec<(usize, usize)>,\n305: }\n306: \n307: impl SourcePreviewMap {\n308:     pub fn new() -> Self {\n309:         Self::default()\n310:     }\n311: \n312:     pub fn add(&mut self, source_line: usize, preview_y: usize) {\n313:         self.source_to_preview.push((source_line, preview_y));\n314:     }\n315: \n316:     /// Find preview Y position for a source line\n317:     pub fn preview_y_for_source(&self, source_line: usize) -> Option<usize> {\n318:         // Find the closest mapped line at or before source_line\n319:         self.source_to_preview\n320:             .iter()\n321:             .filter(|(src, _)| *src <= source_line)\n322:             .max_by_key(|(src, _)| *src)\n323:             .map(|(_, preview_y)| *preview_y)\n324:     }\n325: \n326:     /// Find source line for a preview Y position\n327:     pub fn source_line_for_preview(&self, preview_y: usize) -> Option<usize> {\n328:         self.source_to_preview\n329:             .iter()\n330:             .filter(|(_, py)| *py <= preview_y)\n331:             .max_by_key(|(_, py)| *py)\n332:             .map(|(src, _)| *src)\n333:     }\n334: }\n335: ```\n336: \n337: ### Preview Pane State\n338: \n339: ```rust\n340: // src/model/preview.rs\n341: \n342: use crate::markdown::{MarkdownAst, SourcePreviewMap};\n343: use crate::model::editor_area::{DocumentId, GroupId, Rect};\n344: \n345: /// State for a markdown preview pane\n346: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n347: pub struct PreviewPane {\n348:     /// Document being previewed\n349:     pub document_id: DocumentId,\n350: \n351:     /// Group ID of the source editor (for sync)\n352:     pub source_group_id: GroupId,\n353: \n354:     /// Parsed AST (cached, updated on document change)\n355:     pub ast: Option<MarkdownAst>,\n356: \n357:     /// Source ↔ Preview position mapping\n358:     pub line_map: SourcePreviewMap,\n359: \n360:     /// Current scroll offset in preview (in pixels)\n361:     pub scroll_y: f32,\n362: \n363:     /// Whether to sync scroll with source\n364:     pub sync_scroll: bool,\n365: \n366:     /// Layout rectangle for rendering\n367:     pub rect: Rect,\n368: \n369:     /// Computed layout lines (cached for rendering)\n370:     pub layout_lines: Vec<PreviewLayoutLine>,\n371: }\n372: \n373: /// A single line in the preview layout\n374: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n375: pub struct PreviewLayoutLine {\n376:     /// Y position in preview space\n377:     pub y: f32,\n378:     /// Height of this line\n379:     pub height: f32,\n380:     /// Content segments with formatting\n381:     pub segments: Vec<PreviewSegment>,\n382:     /// Source line this corresponds to (for scroll sync)\n383:     pub source_line: Option<usize>,\n384: }\n385: \n386: /// A formatted segment within a preview line\n387: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n388: pub struct PreviewSegment {\n389:     /// Text content\n390:     pub text: String,\n391:     /// Formatting style\n392:     pub style: PreviewStyle,\n393:     /// X offset from line start\n394:     pub x_offset: f32,\n395: }\n396: \n397: /// Text styling for preview rendering\n398: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Default)]\n399: pub struct PreviewStyle {\n400:     pub bold: bool,\n401:     pub italic: bool,\n402:     pub code: bool,\n403:     pub heading_level: Option<u8>,\n404:     pub is_link: bool,\n405:     pub is_blockquote: bool,\n406:     pub list_indent: usize,\n407: }\n408: \n409: impl PreviewPane {\n410:     pub fn new(document_id: DocumentId, source_group_id: GroupId) -> Self {\n411:         Self {\n412:             document_id,\n413:             source_group_id,\n414: layout_lines: Vec::new(),\n420:         }\n421:     }\n422: \n423:     /// Update scroll position based on source editor scroll\n424:     pub fn sync_from_source(&mut self, source_scroll_line: usize, line_height: f32) {\n425:         if !self.sync_scroll {\n426:             return;\n427:         }\n428: \n429:         if let Some(preview_line) = self.line_map.preview_y_for_source(source_scroll_line) {\n430:             self.scroll_y = preview_line as f32 * line_height;\n431:         }\n432:     }\n433: }\n434: ```\n435: \n436: ### Layout Extension\n437: \n438: ```rust\n439: // In src/model/editor_area.rs\n440: \n441: /// A node in the layout tree - either a group, split container, or preview\n442: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n443: pub x_offset: 0.0,\n900:                 }],\n901:                 source_line: Some(*source_line),\n902:             });\n903: \n904:             *y += 8.0 + line_height * 0.5;\n905:         }\n906: \n907:         _ => {}\n908:     }\n909: }\n910: \n911: fn layout_inline_content(nodes: &[InlineNode], base_style: PreviewStyle) -> Vec<PreviewSegment> {\n912:     let mut segments = Vec::new();\n913:     let mut x_offset = 0.0;\n914: \n915:     for node in nodes {\n916:         match node {\n917:             InlineNode::Text(text) => {\n918:                 segments.push(PreviewSegment {\n919:                     text: text.clone(),\n920:                     style: base_style,\n921:                     x_offset,\n922:                 });\n923:                 x_offset += text.len() as f32 * 8.0; // Approximate\n924:             }\n925:             InlineNode::Strong(children) => {\n926:                 let mut bold_style = base_style;\n927:                 bold_style.bold = true;\n928:                 let child_segments = layout_inline_content(children, bold_style);\n929:                 for mut seg in child_segments {\n930:                     seg.x_offset += x_offset;\n931:                     x_offset += seg.text.len() as f32 * 8.0;\n932:                     segments.push(seg);\n933:                 }\n934:             }\n935:             InlineNode::Emphasis(children) => {\n936:                 let mut italic_style = base_style;\n937:                 italic_style.italic = true;\n938:                 let child_segments = layout_inline_content(children, italic_style);\n939:                 for mut seg in child_segments {\n940:                     seg.x_offset += x_offset;\n941:                     x_offset += seg.text.len() as f32 * 8.0;\n942:                     segments.push(seg);\n943:                 }\n944:             }\n945:             InlineNode::Code(code) => {\n946:                 let mut code_style = base_style;\n947:                 code_style.code = true;\n948:                 segments.push(PreviewSegment {\n949:                     text: code.clone(),\n950:                     style: code_style,\n951:                     x_offset,\n952:                 });\n953:                 x_offset += code.len() as f32 * 8.0;\n954:             }\n955:             InlineNode::Link { text, .. } => {\n956:                 let mut link_style = base_style;\n957:                 link_style.is_link = true;\n958:                 let child_segments = layout_inline_content(text, link_style);\n959:                 for mut seg in child_segments {\n960:                     seg.x_offset += x_offset;\n961:                     x_offset += seg.text.len() as f32 * 8.0;\n962:                     segments.push(seg);\n963:                 }\n964:             }\n965:             InlineNode::SoftBreak => {\n966:                 segments.push(PreviewSegment {\n967:                     text: \" \".to_string(),\n968:                     style: base_style,\n969:                     x_offset,\n970:                 });\n971:                 x_offset += 8.0;\n972:             }\n973:             InlineNode::HardBreak => {\n974:                 // Start new line - handled by caller\n975:             }\n976:         }\n977:     }\n978: \n979:     segments\n980: }\n981: \n982: fn heading_scale(level: u8) -> f32 {\n983:     match level {\n984:         1 => 1.8,\n985:         2 => 1.5,\n986:         3 => 1.3,\n987:         4 => 1.1,\n988:         _ => 1.0,\n989:     }\n990: }\n991: ```\n992: \n993: ---\n994: \n995: ## Keybindings\n996: \n997: | Action | Mac | Windows/Linux | Command |\n998: |--------|-----|---------------|---------|\n999: | Toggle preview | `Cmd+Shift+V` | `Ctrl+Shift+V` | `MarkdownTogglePreview` |\n1000: | Open preview to side | `Cmd+K V` | `Ctrl+K V` | `MarkdownOpenPreviewToSide` |\n1001: | Toggle scroll sync | `Cmd+Shift+S` | `Ctrl+Shift+S` | `MarkdownToggleScrollSync` |\n1002: \n1003: ### debounce, preview should update\n1212:     // (verify by checking AST revision matches document revision)\n1213: }\n1214: \n1215: #[test]\n1216: fn test_scroll_sync() {\n1217:     let mut model = test_model_with_markdown_and_preview(LONG_MARKDOWN);\n1218: \n1219:     // Scroll source to line 50\n1220:     let editor = model.focused_editor_mut().unwrap();\n1221:     editor.viewport.scroll_offset = 50;\n1222: \n1223:     update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::Scroll(50)));\n1224: \n1225:     // Preview should have scrolled proportionally\n1226:     let preview = model.preview_panes.values().next().unwrap();\n1227:     assert!(preview.scroll_y > 0.0);\n1228: }\n1229: ```\n1230: \n1231: ---\n1232: \n1233: ## Dependencies\n1234: \n1235: ```toml\n1236: # Cargo.toml additions\n1237: \n1238: [dependencies]\n1239: pulldown-cmark = \"0.10\"  # Markdown parser (CommonMark compliant)\n1240: ```\n1241: \n1242: ### Why `pulldown-cmark`?\n1243: \n1244: - Fast, streaming parser\n1245: - CommonMark compliant\n1246: - Source position tracking\n1247: - Well-maintained, widely used\n1248: - No unsafe code\n1249: - Pure Rust\n1250: \n1251: ---\n1252: \n1253: ## 150;\n1261: ```\n1262: \n1263: ### Large File Handling\n1264: \n1265: For files > 5000 lines:\n1266: \n1267: 1. Parse only visible portion + buffer\n1268: 2. Use virtual scrolling for layout\n1269: 3. Cache rendered line bitmaps\n1270: \n1271: ### Memory\n1272: \n1273: - AST is relatively lightweight (string slices into source)\n1274: - Layout lines can grow large - consider streaming layout\n1275: - Preview pane caches layout; clear on document change\n1276: \n1277: ---\n1278: \n1279: ## Future Enhancements\n1280: \n1281: ### Phase 2: Enhanced Rendering\n1282: \n1283: - Syntax highlighting in code blocks (reuse tree-sitter)\n1284: - Image placeholders with dimensions\n1285: - Table rendering (basic grid)\n1286: - Checkbox rendering for task lists\n1287: \n1288: ### Phase 3: Interactivity\n1289: \n1290: - Click links to open in browser\n1291: - Click headings to jump in source\n1292: - Copy code blocks to clipboard\n1293: - Hover tooltips for links\n1294: \n1295: ### Phase 4: Export\n1296: \n1297: - Export to HTML\n1298: - Print preview\n1299: - PDF generation (via external tool)\n1300: \n1301: ---\n1302: \n1303: ## References\n1304: \n1305: - [pulldown-cmark](https://crates.io/crates/pulldown-cmark) - Markdown parser\n1306: - [CommonMark Spec](https://spec.commonmark.org/) - Markdown standard\n1307: - [VS Code Markdown Preview](https://code.visualstudio.com/docs/languages/markdown) - Visual reference\n1308: - [Feature: Split View](../archived/SPLIT_VIEW.md) - Layout system reference\n1309: - [Feature: Syntax Highlighting](syntax-highlighting.md) - For code block highlighting",

  "content": "1: # Diff Gutter\n2: \n3: Mini diff markers in the gutter showing changed/added/deleted lines vs disk\n4: \n5: > **Status:** Planning\n6: > **Priority:** P2\n7: > **Effort:** M\n8: > **Created:** 2025-12-19\n9: > **Milestone:** 5 - Insight Tools\n10: > **Feature ID:** F-160\n11: \n12: ---\n13: \n14: ## Table of Contents\n15: \n16: 1. [Overview](#overview)\n17: 2. [Architecture](#architecture)\n18: 3. [Data Structures](#data-structures)\n19: 4. [Rendering](#rendering)\n20: 5. [Keybindings](#keybindings)\n21: 6. [Implementation Plan](#implementation-plan)\n22: 7. [Testing Strategy](#testing-strategy)\n23: 8. [References](#references)\n24: \n25: ---\n26: \n27: ## Overview\n28: \n29: ### Current State\n30: \n31: The editor currently has:\n32: \n33: - Line number gutter rendered alongside the text area\n34: - File modification tracking (`Document.is_modified` flag)\n35: - File system watcher for external changes (`src/fs_watcher.rs`)\n36: - Rope-based buffer with efficient line access\n37: \n38: However, there is no visual indication of *which* lines have changed since the file was last saved or loaded. Users must rely on the modification indicator in the tab title or status bar.\n39: \n40: ### Goals\n41: \n42: 1. **Visual diff indicators** - Show colored markers in the gutter for:\n43:    - Added lines (green)\n44:    - Modified lines (yellow/orange)\n45:    - Deleted lines (red triangle/marker)\n46: \n47: 2. **Efficient diffing** - Compute diff incrementally when possible, avoid full diff on every keystroke\n48: \n49: 3. **Disk-based comparison** - Compare current buffer against last saved/loaded content\n50: \n51: 4. **Navigation** - Jump to next/previous change\n52: \n53: 5. **Inline diff preview** - Option to show what changed on hover or via command\n54: \n55: ### Non-Goals (This Phase)\n56: \n57: - Git integration (comparing against HEAD, branches, etc.)\n58: - Blame annotations\n59: - Multi-file diff views\n60: - Merge conflict resolution UI\n61: - External diff tool integration\n62: \n63: ---\n64: \n65: ## Architecture\n66: \n67: ```\n68: ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n69: │                           Diff Gutter Flow                                  │\n70: │                                                                             │\n71: │  ┌──────────────┐    ┌──────────────┐    ┌──────────────────────────────┐  │\n72: │  │   Document   │───▶│  DiffEngine  │───▶│       LineDiffState          │  │\n73: │  │   Changed    │    │  (on edit)   │    │  Vec<LineChange> per doc     │  │\n74: │  └──────────────┘    └──────────────┘    └───────────────┬──────────────┘  │\n75: │         │                   │                             │                 │\n76: │         │                   │                             │                 │\n77: │         ▼                   ▼                             ▼                 │\n78: │  ┌──────────────┐    ┌──────────────┐    ┌──────────────────────────────┐  │\n79: │  │ Save/Load    │───▶│  Baseline    │    │        Renderer              │  │\n80: │  │ Operation    │    │  Snapshot    │    │  (draws gutter markers)      │  │\n81: │  └──────────────┘    └──────────────┘    └──────────────────────────────┘  │\n82: │                                                                             │\n83: └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n84: \n85:      Baseline Update              Incremental Diff               Render\n86:         on Save                    on Each Edit                 Each Frame\n87: ```\n88: \n89: ### Module Structure\n90: \n91: ```\n92: src/\n93: ├── diff/                        # NEW MODULE\n94: │   ├── mod.rs                   # Public exports\n95: │   ├── engine.rs                # DiffEngine, Myers diff algorithm wrapper\n96: │   ├── state.rs                 # LineDiffState, per-document diff cache\n97: │   └── gutter.rs                # Gutter marker types, rendering helpers\n98: ├── model/\n99: │   └── document.rs              # + baseline_content: Option<String>\n100: ├── update/\n101: │   └── document.rs              # + trigger diff recompute after edits\n102: └── view/\n103:     └── mod.rs                   # + render_diff_gutter()\n104: ```\n105: \n106: ### Message Flow\n107: \n108: ```\n109:                          ┌──────────────────┐\n110:                          │   User Types     │\n111:                          └────────┬─────────┘\n112:                                   │\n113:                                   ▼\n114:                          ┌──────────────────┐\n115:                          │ DocumentMsg::    │\n116:                          │ InsertChar(ch)   │\n117:                          └────────┬─────────┘\n118:                                   │\n119:                                   ▼\n120:                     ┌─────────────────────────────┐\n121:                     │  update_document()          │\n122:                     │  - Apply edit to buffer     │\n123:                     │  - Increment revision       │\n124:                     └─────────────┬───────────────┘\n125:                                   │\n126:                                   ▼\n127:                     ┌─────────────────────────────┐\n128:                     │  Cmd::DebouncedDiffCompute  │\n129:                     │  { doc_id, revision, 100ms }│\n130:                     └─────────────┬───────────────┘\n131:                                   │\n132:                                   ▼ (after debounce)\n133:                     ┌─────────────────────────────┐\n134:                     │  Cmd::RunDiffCompute        │\n135:                     │  { doc_id, revision,        │\n136:                     │    current, baseline }      │\n137:                     └─────────────┬───────────────┘\n138:                                   │\n139:                                   ▼ (background thread)\n140:                     ┌─────────────────────────────┐\n141:                     │  Msg::DiffComputed          │\n142:                     │  { doc_id, revision,        │\n143:                     │    line_changes }           │\n144:                     └─────────────┬───────────────┘\n145:                                   │\n146:                                   ▼\n147:                     ┌─────────────────────────────┐\n148:                     │  Store in Document.         │\n149:                     │  diff_state                 │\n150:                     │  Cmd::Redraw                │\n151:                     └─────────────────────────────┘\n152: ```\n153: \n154: ---\n155: \n156: ## Data Structures\n157: \n158: ### Core Types\n159: \n160: ```rust\n161: // src/diff/mod.rs\n162: \n163: /// Type of change for a line relative to baseline\n164: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]\n165: pub enum LineChangeKind {\n166:     /// Line was added (not in baseline)\n167:     Added,\n168:     /// Line content was modified\n169:     Modified,\n170:     /// Line(s) were deleted at this position\n171:     /// The count indicates how many baseline lines were removed\n172:     Deleted { count: usize },\n173: }\n174: \n175: /// A single line change marker\n176: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n177: pub struct LineChange {\n178:     /// Line number in current buffer (0-indexed)\n179:     pub line: usize,\n180:     /// Type of change\n181:     pub kind: LineChangeKind,\n182: }\n183: \n184: /// Diff state for a single document\n185: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]\n186: pub struct LineDiffState {\n187:     /// All line changes relative to baseline\n188:     pub changes: Vec<LineChange>,\n189:     /// Revision this diff corresponds to\n190:     pub revision: u64,\n191:     /// Whether diff is currently being computed\n192:     pub computing: bool,\n193: }\n194: \n195: impl LineDiffState {\n196:     /// Get the change kind for a specific line, if any\n197:     pub fn change_at(&self, line: usize) -> Option<LineChangeKind> {\n198:         self.changes\n199:             .iter()\n200:             .find(|c| c.line == line)\n201:             .map(|c| c.kind)\n202:     }\n203: \n204:     /// Get indices of all changed lines for navigation\n205:     pub fn changed_lines(&self) -> Vec<usize> {\n206:         self.changes.iter().map(|c| c.line).collect()\n207:     }\n208: \n209:     /// Find next change after given line (wraps around)\n210:     pub fn next_change(&self, after_line: usize) -> Option<usize> {\n211:         let lines = self.changed_lines();\n212:         if lines.is_empty() {\n213:             return None;\n214:         }\n215: \n216:         // Find first change after current line\n217:         if let Some(&line) = lines.iter().find(|&&l| l > after_line) {\n218:             return Some(line);\n219:         }\n220: \n221:         // Wrap to first change\n222:         Some(lines[0])\n223:     }\n224: \n225:     /// Find previous change before given line (wraps around)\n226:     pub fn prev_change(&self, before_line: usize) -> Option<usize> {\n227:         let lines = self.changed_lines();\n228:         if lines.is_empty() {\n229:             return None;\n230:         }\n231: \n232:         // Find last change before current line\n233:         if let Some(&line) = lines.iter().rev().find(|&&l| l < before_line) {\n234:             return Some(line);\n235:         }\n236: \n237:         // Wrap to last change\n238:         lines.last().copied()\n239:     }\n240: }\n241: ```\n242: \n243: ### Document Extension\n244: \n245: ```rust\n246: // In src/model/document.rs\n247: \n248: pub struct Document {\n249:     // ... existing fields ...\n250: \n251:     /// Baseline content for diff comparison\n252:     /// Set on load/save, cleared on close\n253:     pub baseline_content: Option<String>,\n254: \n255:     /// Current diff state (computed asynchronously)\n256:     pub diff_state: LineDiffState,\n257: }\n258: \n259: impl Document {\n260:     /// Update baseline to current buffer content\n261:     /// Called after successful save\n262:     pub fn update_baseline(&mut self) {\n263:         self.baseline_content = Some(self.buffer.to_string());\n264:         self.diff_state = LineDiffState::default(); // No changes after save\n265:     }\n266: \n267:     /// Set baseline from loaded content\n268:     /// Called when file is loaded from disk\n269:     pub fn set_baseline(&mut self, content: String) {\n270:         self.baseline_content = Some(content);\n271:         self.diff_state = LineDiffState::default();\n272:     }\n273: \n274:     /// Check if we have a baseline to diff against\n275:     pub fn has_baseline(&self) -> bool {\n276:         self.baseline_content.is_some()\n277:     }\n278: }\n279: ```\n280: \n281: ### Diff Engine\n282: \n283: ```rust\n284: // src/diff/engine.rs\n285: \n286: use similar::{ChangeTag, TextDiff};\n287: \n288: /// Compute line-level diff between baseline and current content\n289: pub fn compute_line_diff(baseline: &str, current: &str) -> Vec<LineChange> {\n290:     let diff = TextDiff::from_lines(baseline, current);\n291:     let mut changes = Vec::new();\n292:     let mut current_line = 0;\n293:     let mut pending_deletes = 0;\n294: \n295:     for change in diff.iter_all_changes() {\n296:         match change.tag() {\n297:             ChangeTag::Equal => {\n298:                 // Flush any pending deletes at this position\n299:                 if pending_deletes > 0 {\n300:                     changes.push(LineChange {\n301:                         line: current_line,\n302:                         kind: LineChangeKind::Deleted { count: pending_deletes },\n303:                     });\n304:                     pending_deletes = 0;\n305:                 }\n306:                 current_line += 1;\n307:             }\n308:             ChangeTag::Delete => {\n309:                 // Accumulate consecutive deletes\n310:                 pending_deletes += 1;\n311:             }\n312:             ChangeTag::Insert => {\n313:                 // Check if this replaces deleted lines (modification)\n314:                 if pending_deletes > 0 {\n315:                     changes.push(LineChange {\n316:                         line: current_line,\n317:                         kind: LineChangeKind::Modified,\n318:                     });\n319:                     pending_deletes -= 1;\n320:                 } else {\n321:                     changes.push(LineChange {\n322:                         line: current_line,\n323:                         kind: LineChangeKind::Added,\n324:                     });\n325:                 }\n326:                 current_line += 1;\n327:             }\n328:         }\n329:     }\n330: \n331:     // Handle trailing deletes (at end of file)\n332:     if pending_deletes > 0 {\n333:         changes.push(LineChange {\n334:             line: current_line.saturating_sub(1),\n335:             kind: LineChangeKind::Deleted { count: pending_deletes },\n336:         });\n337:     }\n338: \n339:     changes\n340: }\n341: \n342: /// Background diff computation task\n343: pub fn compute_diff_async(\n344:     baseline: String,\n345:     current: String,\n346:     doc_id: DocumentId,\n347:     revision: u64,\n348:     tx: std::sync::mpsc::Sender<Msg>,\n349: ) {\n350:     std::thread::spawn(move || {\n351:         let changes = compute_line_diff(&baseline, &current);\n352:         let _ = tx.send(Msg::Diff(DiffMsg::Computed {\n353:             document_id: doc_id,\n354:             revision,\n355:             changes,\n356:         }));\n357:     });\n358: }\n359: ```\n360: \n361: ### Messages and Commands\n362: \n363: ```rust\n364: // In src/messages.rs\n365: \n366: /// Diff-related messages\n367: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n368: pub enum DiffMsg {\n369:     /// Diff computation completed\n370:     Computed {\n371:         document_id: DocumentId,\n372:         revision: u64,\n373:         changes: Vec<LineChange>,\n374:     },\n375:     /// Navigate to next change\n376:     GotoNextChange,\n377:     /// Navigate to previous change\n378:     GotoPrevChange,\n379:     /// Show inline diff for current line\n380:     ShowLineDiff,\n381:     /// Hide inline diff\n382:     HideLineDiff,\n383: }\n384: \n385: // Add to Msg enum:\n386: pub enum Msg {\n387:     // ... existing variants ...\n388:     Diff(DiffMsg),\n389: }\n390: ```\n391: \n392: ```rust\n393: // In src/commands.rs\n394: \n395: pub enum Cmd {\n396:     // ... existing variants ...\n397: \n398:     /// Start debounce timer for diff computation\n399:     DebouncedDiffCompute {\n400:         document_id: DocumentId,\n401:         revision: u64,\n402:         delay_ms: u64,\n403:     },\n404: \n405:     /// Run diff computation in background\n406:     RunDiffCompute {\n407:         document_id: DocumentId,\n408:         revision: u64,\n409:         baseline: String,\n410:         current: String,\n411:     },\n412: }\n413: ```\n414: \n415: ### Theme Extension\n416: \n417: ```rust\n418: // In src/theme.rs\n419: \n420: pub struct EditorTheme {\n421:     // ... existing fields ...\n422: \n423:     /// Diff gutter colors\n424:     pub diff_gutter: DiffGutterTheme,\n425: }\n426: \n427: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize)]\n428: pub struct DiffGutterTheme {\n429:     /// Color for added lines (default: green)\n430:     pub added: Color,\n431:     /// Color for modified lines (default: yellow/orange)\n432:     pub modified: Color,\n433:     /// Color for deleted line markers (default: red)\n434:     pub deleted: Color,\n435:     /// Width of the diff marker in pixels (default: 3)\n436:     pub marker_width: u32,\n437: }\n438: \n439: impl Default for DiffGutterTheme {\n440:     fn default() -> Self {\n441:         Self {\n442:             added: Color::rgb(0x28, 0xA7, 0x45),      // GitHub green\n443:             modified: Color::rgb(0xD2, 0x9D, 0x22),    // Yellow/gold\n444:             deleted: Color::rgb(0xCB, 0x24, 0x31),     // Red\n445:             marker_width: 3,\n446:         }\n447:     }\n448: }\n449: ```\n450: \n451: ---\n452: \n453: ## Rendering\n454: \n455: ### Gutter Marker Rendering\n456: \n457: ```\n458: ┌───┬────┬────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n459: │ ▌ │  1 │ fn main() {                                │  <- Modified line (yellow bar)\n460: │   │  2 │     let x = 1;                             │  <- Unchanged\n461: │ ▌ │  3 │     let y = 2;  // new comment             │  <- Modified line (yellow bar)\n462: │ █ │  4 │     let z = 3;                             │  <- Added line (green bar)\n463: │ █ │  5 │     let w = 4;                             │  <- Added line (green bar)\n464: │   │  6 │     println!(\"{}\", x + y);                 │  <- Unchanged\n465: │ ◀ │  7 │ }                                          │  <- Deleted after this (red triangle)\n466: └───┴────┴────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n467:   │    │\n468:   │    └── Line number gutter (existing)\n469:   └─────── Diff marker gutter (new, 3-4px wide)\n470: ```\n471: \n472: ### Rendering Logic\n473: \n474: ```rust\n475: // src/diff/gutter.rs\n476: \n477: use crate::view::Frame;\n478: use crate::theme::DiffGutterTheme;\n479: \n480: /// Render diff markers for visible lines\n481: pub fn render_diff_gutter(\n482:     frame: &mut Frame,\n483:     diff_state: &LineDiffState,\n484:     visible_start_line: usize,\n485:     visible_end_line: usize,\n486:     gutter_x: usize,           // X position of diff gutter\n487:     line_height: usize,\n488:     y_offset: usize,           // Y offset (after tab bar)\n489:     theme: &DiffGutterTheme,\n490: ) {\n491:     let marker_width = theme.marker_width as usize;\n492: \n493:     for line in visible_start_line..=visible_end_line {\n494:         let Some(change_kind) = diff_state.change_at(line) else {\n495:             continue;\n496:         };\n497: \n498:         let line_y = y_offset + (line - visible_start_line) * line_height;\n499: \n500:         match change_kind {\n501:             LineChangeKind::Added => {\n502:                 // Green vertical bar\n503:                 frame.fill_rect(\n504:                     gutter_x,\n505:                     line_y,\n506:                     marker_width,\n507:                     line_height,\n508:                     theme.added.to_argb_u32(),\n509:                 );\n510:             }\n511:             LineChangeKind::Modified => {\n512:                 // Yellow/orange vertical bar\n513:                 frame.fill_rect(\n514:                     gutter_x,\n515:                     line_y,\n516:                     marker_width,\n517:                     line_height,\n518:                     theme.modified.to_argb_u32(),\n519:                 );\n520:             }\n521:             LineChangeKind::Deleted { count } => {\n522:                 // Red triangle/arrow pointing left\n523:                 // Indicates lines were deleted at this position\n524:                 render_delete_marker(\n525:                     frame,\n526:                     gutter_x,\n527:                     line_y,\n528:                     marker_width,\n529:                     line_height,\n530:                     count,\n531:                     theme.deleted.to_argb_u32(),\n532:                 );\n533:             }\n534:         }\n535:     }\n536: }\n537: \n538: /// Render a small triangle marker for deleted lines\n539: fn render_delete_marker(\n540:     frame: &mut Frame,\n541:     x: usize,\n542:     y: usize,\n543:     width: usize,\n544:     height: usize,\n545:     _count: usize,\n546:     color: u32,\n547: ) {\n548:     // Draw a small left-pointing triangle at the line boundary\n549:     // Positioned at the bottom of the line to indicate \"deleted below\"\n550:     let triangle_height = height.min(8);\n551:     let triangle_y = y + height - triangle_height;\n552: \n553:     for dy in 0..triangle_height {\n554:         let row_width = (width * (triangle_height - dy)) / triangle_height;\n555:         if row_width > 0 {\n556:             frame.fill_rect(\n557:                 x,\n558:                 triangle_y + dy,\n559:                 row_width,\n560:                 1,\n561:                 color,\n562:             );\n563:         }\n564:     }\n565: }\n566: ```\n567: \n568: ---\n569: \n570: ## Keybindings\n571: \n572: | Action | Mac | Windows/Linux | Command |\n573: |--------|-----|---------------|---------|\n574: | Next change | `Option+]` | `Alt+]` | `DiffGotoNext` |\n575: | Previous change | `Option+[` | `Alt+[` | `DiffGotoPrev` |\n576: | Show line diff | `Option+D` | `Alt+D` | `DiffShowLine` |\n577: | Revert line | `Option+Shift+D` | `Alt+Shift+D` | `DiffRevertLine` |\n578: \n796: ## Dependencies\n797: \n798: ```toml\n799: # Cargo.toml additions\n800: \n801: [dependencies]\n802: similar = \"2.4\"  # Text diffing library (fast, pure Rust)\n803: ```\n804: \n805: ### Why `similar`?\n806: \n807: - Pure Rust implementation (no C dependencies)\n808: - Fast Myers diff algorithm\n809: - Line-level and character-level diffing\n810: - Well-maintained, used by insta (snapshot testing)\n811: - Small footprint (~50KB)\n812: \n813: ---\n814: \n815: ## Performance Considerations\n816: \n817: ### Debouncing\n818: \n819: Diff computation is debounced (100ms default) to avoid recomputing on every keystroke:\n820: \n821: ```rust\n822: const DIFF_DEBOUNCE_MS: u64 = 100;\n823: ```\n824: \n825: ### Large File Handling\n826: \n827: For files > 10,000 lines:\n828: \n829: 1. Consider chunked diffing (diff visible region + context)\n830: 2. Increase debounce time\n831: 3. Show \"computing...\" indicator\n832: \n833: ### Memory\n834: \n835: Baseline content doubles memory usage per document. For very large files:\n836: \n837: - Consider storing baseline as compressed chunks\n838: - Or compute baseline hash and re-read from disk on demand\n839: \n840: ---\n841: \n842: ## Future Enhancements\n843: \n844: ### Phase 2: Git Integration\n845: \n846: - Compare against last commit instead of disk\n847: - Show branch/commit in status\n848: - Stage/unstage individual lines\n849: \n850: ### Phase 3: Inline Diff\n851: \n852: - Show deleted content inline (strikethrough)\n853: - Character-level highlighting within changed lines\n854: - Mini diff tooltip on hover\n855: \n856: ### Phase 4: Revert Operations\n857: \n858: - Revert single line to baseline\n859: - Revert selection to baseline\n860: - Undo revert\n861: \n862: ---\n863: \n864: ## References\n865: \n866: - [similar crate](https://crates.io/crates/similar) - Diffing library\n867: - [VS Code SCM Decorations](https://code.visualstudio.com/docs/sourcecontrol/overview#_scm-decorations) - Visual reference\n868: - [Myers Diff Algorithm](https://blog.jcoglan.com/2017/02/12/the-myers-diff-algorithm-part-1/) - Algorithm explanation\n869: - [Feature: Workspace Management](workspace-management.md) - File system watching dependency",

  "content": "1: # Code Folding (F-150)\n2: \n3: Collapse and expand regions of code for better navigation and focus.\n4: \n5: > **Status:** 📋 Planned\n6: > **Priority:** P3 (Nice-to-have)\n7: > **Effort:** XL (2+ weeks)\n8: > **Created:** 2025-12-19\n9: > **Updated:** 2025-12-19\n10: > **Milestone:** 4 - Hard Problems\n11: \n12: ---\n13: \n14: ## Overview\n15: \n16: Code folding allows users to collapse code blocks (functions, classes, comments, etc.) to hide their contents and show only a summary. This improves navigation in large files and helps focus on relevant code sections.\n17: \n18: ---\n19: \n20: ## Features\n21: \n22: ### Core Capabilities\n23: \n24: | Feature | Description |\n25: |---------|-------------|\n26: | Fold/Unfold | Collapse/expand individual regions |\n27: | Fold All | Collapse all foldable regions |\n28: | Unfold All | Expand all folded regions |\n29: | Fold Level N | Fold to specific nesting depth |\n30: | Fold Selection | Create custom fold from selection |\n31: | Persistent Folds | Remember fold state across sessions |\n32: \n33: ### Fold Sources\n34: \n35: | Source | Description | Priority |\n36: |--------|-------------|----------|\n37: | Syntax-based | Language constructs (functions, classes) | P1 |\n38: | Indentation-based | Indent levels (Python, YAML) | P1 |\n39: | Region markers | `// #region` / `// #endregion` | P2 |\n40: | Manual | User-created folds | P3 |\n41: \n42: ---\n43: \n44: ## Data Structures\n45: \n46: ### FoldRegion\n47: \n48: ```rust\n49: /// A single foldable region in the document\n50: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n51: pub struct FoldRegion {\n52:     /// Starting line (inclusive)\n53:     pub start_line: usize,\n54:     /// Ending line (inclusive)\n55:     pub end_line: usize,\n56:     /// Current fold state\n57:     pub state: FoldState,\n58:     /// Source of fold detection\n59:     pub source: FoldSource,\n60:     /// Summary text to show when folded\n61:     pub summary: Option<String>,\n62:     /// Nesting level (0 = top level)\n63:     pub level: usize,\n64: }\n65: \n66: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]\n67: pub enum FoldState {\n68:     Expanded,\n69:     Collapsed,\n70: }\n71: \n72: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]\n73: pub enum FoldSource {\n74:     /// From syntax tree (functions, classes, etc.)\n75:     Syntax,\n76:     /// From indentation changes\n77:     Indentation,\n78:     /// From region markers\n79:     RegionMarker,\n80:     /// User-created fold\n81:     Manual,\n82: }\n83: ```\n84: \n85: ### FoldingState\n86: \n87: ```rust\n88: /// Folding state for a document\n89: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]\n90: pub struct FoldingState {\n91:     /// All detected fold regions\n92:     pub regions: Vec<FoldRegion>,\n93:     /// Collapsed regions (by start line)\n94:     pub collapsed: HashSet<usize>,\n95:     /// Document revision when folds were computed\n96:     pub revision: u64,\n97: }\n98: \n99: impl FoldingState {\n100:     /// Check if a line is inside a collapsed fold\n101:     pub fn is_hidden(&self, line: usize) -> bool {\n102:         self.regions.iter().any(|r| {\n103:             r.state == FoldState::Collapsed\n104:                 && line > r.start_line\n105:                 && line <= r.end_line\n106:         })\n107:     }\n108: \n109:     /// Get visible line count (total - hidden)\n110:     pub fn visible_line_count(&self, total_lines: usize) -> usize {\n111:         let hidden = self.regions.iter()\n112:             .filter(|r| r.state == FoldState::Collapsed)\n113:             .map(|r| r.end_line - r.start_line)\n114:             .sum::<usize>();\n115:         total_lines - hidden\n116:     }\n117: \n118:     /// Map visual line to document line\n119:     pub fn visual_to_document_line(&self, visual: usize) -> usize {\n120:         // Account for collapsed regions above this line\n121:         let mut doc_line = 0;\n122:         let mut vis_line = 0;\n123: \n124:         while vis_line < visual {\n125:             if !self.is_hidden(doc_line) {\n126:                 vis_line += 1;\n127:             }\n128:             doc_line += 1;\n129:         }\n130:         doc_line\n131:     }\n132: }\n133: ```\n134: \n135: ---\n136: \n137: ## Fold Detection\n138: \n139: ### Syntax-Based Detection\n140: \n141: Uses tree-sitter syntax tree to find foldable constructs.\n142: \n143: ```rust\n144: /// Detect fold regions from syntax tree\n145: fn detect_syntax_folds(\n146:     tree: &tree_sitter::Tree,\n147:     source: &str,\n148:     language: LanguageId,\n149: ) -> Vec<FoldRegion> {\n150:     let mut regions = Vec::new();\n151:     let mut cursor = tree.walk();\n152: \n153:     // Language-specific node types that are foldable\n154:     let foldable_types = match language {\n155:         LanguageId::Rust => &[\n156:             \"function_item\", \"impl_item\", \"struct_item\",\n157:             \"enum_item\", \"mod_item\", \"match_expression\"\n158:         ],\n159:         LanguageId::JavaScript => &[\n160:             \"function_declaration\", \"class_declaration\",\n161:             \"method_definition\", \"if_statement\", \"for_statement\"\n162:         ],\n163:         // ... other languages\n164:     };\n165: \n166:     visit_tree(&mut cursor, |node| {\n167:         if foldable_types.contains(&node.kind()) {\n168:             let start = node.start_position().row;\n169:             let end = node.end_position().row;\n170: \n171:             // Only fold if multi-line\n172:             if end > start {\n173:                 regions.push(FoldRegion {\n174:                     start_line: start,\n175:                     end_line: end,\n176:                     state: FoldState::Expanded,\n177:                     source: FoldSource::Syntax,\n178:                     summary: extract_summary(node, source),\n179:                     level: compute_nesting_level(node),\n180:                 });\n181:             }\n182:         }\n183:     });\n184: \n185:     regions\n186: }\n187: ```\n188: \n189: ### Indentation-Based Detection\n190: \n191: For languages like Python where indentation defines blocks.\n192: \n193: ```rust\n194: fn detect_indentation_folds(lines: &[&str]) -> Vec<FoldRegion> {\n195:     let mut regions = Vec::new();\n196:     let mut stack: Vec<(usize, usize)> = vec![]; // (start_line, indent_level)\n197: \n198:     for (line_num, line) in lines.iter().enumerate() {\n199:         let indent = line.chars().take_while(|c| c.is_whitespace()).count();\n200: \n201:         // Close any regions with higher indent\n202:         while let Some(&(start, level)) = stack.last() {\n203:             if indent <= level && line.trim().len() > 0 {\n204:                 regions.push(FoldRegion {\n205:                     start_line: start,\n206:                     end_line: line_num - 1,\n207:                     // ...\n208:                 });\n209:                 stack.pop();\n210:             } else {\n211:                 break;\n212:             }\n213:         }\n214: \n215:         // Start new region if line ends with colon (Python) or similar\n216:         if should_start_fold(line) {\n217:             stack.push((line_num, indent));\n218:         }\n219:     }\n220: \n221:     regions\n222: }\n223: ```\n224: \n225: ### Region Marker Detection\n226: \n227: ```rust\n228: fn detect_region_markers(lines: &[&str]) -> Vec<FoldRegion> {\n229:     let region_patterns = [\n230:         (r\"//\\s*#region\\s*(.*)\", r\"//\\s*#endregion\"),   // C-style\n231:         (r\"<!--\\s*region\\s*(.*)\", r\"<!--\\s*endregion\"), // HTML\n232:         (r\"#\\s*region\\s*(.*)\", r\"#\\s*endregion\"),      // Python/Ruby\n233:     ];\n234: \n235:     // Match start/end markers and create regions\n236: }\n237: ```\n238: \n239: ---\n240: \n241: ## Messages\n242: \n243: ```rust\n244: pub enum EditorMsg {\n245:     /// Toggle fold at cursor line\n246:     ToggleFold,\n247:     /// Fold the region at cursor\n248:     Fold,\n249:     /// Unfold the region at cursor\n250:     Unfold,\n251:     /// Fold all regions\n252:     FoldAll,\n253:     /// Unfold all regions\n254:     UnfoldAll,\n255:     /// Fold to specific level (0 = all, 1 = top-level only, etc.)\n256:     FoldLevel(usize),\n257:     /// Fold selection into manual region\n258:     FoldSelection,\n259:     /// Go to parent fold\n260:     GoToParentFold,\n261:     /// Go to next fold\n262:     GoToNextFold,\n263:     /// Go to previous fold\n264:     GoToPrevFold,\n265: }\n266: ```\n267: \n268: ---\n269: \n270: ## Key Bindings\n271: \n272: | Action | Mac | Standard |\n273: |--------|-----|----------|\n274: | Toggle Fold | Cmd+Option+[ | Ctrl+Shift+[ |\n275: | Fold | Cmd+Option+[ | Ctrl+Shift+[ |\n276: | Unfold | Cmd+Option+] | Ctrl+Shift+] |\n277: | Fold All | Cmd+K Cmd+0 | Ctrl+K Ctrl+0 |\n278: | Unfold All | Cmd+K Cmd+J | Ctrl+K Ctrl+J |\n279: | Fold Level 1 | Cmd+K Cmd+1 | Ctrl+K Ctrl+1 |\n280: | Fold Level 2 | Cmd+K Cmd+2 | Ctrl+K Ctrl+2 |\n281: \n282: ---\n283: \n284: ## Rendering\n285: \n286: ### Fold Indicators in Gutter\n287: \n288: ```rust\n289: fn render_fold_indicators(\n290:     frame: &mut Frame,\n291:     gutter_x: usize,\n292:     folding: &FoldingState,\n293:     visible_lines: Range<usize>,\n294:     theme: &Theme,\n295: ) {\n296:     for region in &folding.regions {\n297:         if !visible_lines.contains(&region.start_line) {\n298:             continue;\n299:         }\n300: \n301:         let y = line_to_y(region.start_line);\n302:         let icon = match region.state {\n303:             FoldState::Expanded => \"▼\",   // or \"⌄\"\n304:             FoldState::Collapsed => \"▶\",  // or \"›\"\n305:         };\n306: \n307:         frame.draw_text(gutter_x, y, icon, theme.fold_indicator);\n308:     }\n309: }\n310: ```\n311: \n312: ### Collapsed Region Display\n313: \n314: When a region is collapsed, show:\n315: 1. First line of the region\n316: 2. Fold indicator (`...` or `⋯`)\n317: 3. Optional summary (e.g., `{ 15 lines }`)\n318: \n319: ```rust\n320: fn render_collapsed_fold(\n321:     frame: &mut Frame,\n322:     line_num: usize,\n323:     region: &FoldRegion,\n324:     theme: &Theme,\n325: ) {\n326:     // Render first line normally\n327:     render_line(frame, line_num);\n328: \n329:     // Add fold placeholder at end\n330:     let placeholder = format!(\n331:         \" ⋯ {} lines\",\n332:         region.end_line - region.start_line\n333:     );\n334:     frame.draw_text(\n335:         line_end_x,\n336:         line_y,\n337:         &placeholder,\n338:         theme.fold_placeholder,\n339:     );\n340: }\n341: ```\n342: \n343: ---\n344: \n345: ## Integration with Other Features\n346: \n347: ### Soft-Wrap (F-140)\n348: \n349: Folding must coordinate with soft-wrap for visual line calculations:\n350: \n351: ```rust\n352: // Visual line = document line accounting for:\n353: // 1. Collapsed folds (subtract hidden lines)\n354: // 2. Soft-wrapped lines (add extra visual lines)\n355: \n356: fn document_to_visual_line(\n357:     doc_line: usize,\n358:     folding: &FoldingState,\n359:     wrap_info: &WrapInfo,\n360: ) -> usize {\n361:     let mut visual = 0;\n362: \n363:     for line in 0..doc_line {\n364:         if !folding.is_hidden(line) {\n365:             visual += wrap_info.wrapped_line_count(line);\n366:         }\n367:     }\n368: \n369:     visual\n370: }\n371: ```\n372: \n373: ### Syntax Highlighting\n374: \n375: Reuse existing tree-sitter infrastructure:\n376: \n377: ```rust\n378: // src/syntax/worker.rs already parses documents\n379: // Add fold detection to parse results\n380: \n381: struct ParseResult {\n382:     highlights: SyntaxHighlights,\n383:     fold_regions: Vec<FoldRegion>,  // NEW\n384: }\n385: ```\n386: \n387: ### Find/Replace\n388: \n389: When searching:\n390: - Auto-unfold regions containing matches\n391: - Or show match count in collapsed regions\n392: \n393: ### Multi-Cursor\n394: \n395: When a cursor moves into a collapsed region:\n396: - Auto-unfold that region\n397: - Or move cursor to region boundary\n398: \n399: ---\n400: \n401: ## Persistence\n402: \n403: Save fold state per file:\n404: \n405: ```rust\n406: // ~/.config/token-editor/fold-state.json\n407: {\n408:     \"/path/to/file.rs\": {\n409:         \"collapsed\": [10, 45, 120],  // Start lines of collapsed regions\n410:         \"manual\": [                   // User-created folds\n411:             {\"start\": 50, \"end\": 55}\n412:         ]\n413:     }\n414: }\n415: ```\n416: \n417: ---\n418: \n419: ## Performance Considerations\n420: \n421: ### Lazy Detection\n422: \n423: Don't detect all folds upfront for large files:\n424: \n425: ```rust\n426: struct LazyFoldingState {\n427:     /// Fully computed regions\n428:     computed_ranges: Vec<Range<usize>>,\n429:     /// Ranges not yet computed\n430:     pending_ranges: Vec<Range<usize>>,\n431: }\n432: \n433: impl LazyFoldingState {\n434:     fn ensure_computed(&mut self, lines: Range<usize>) {\n435:         // Only compute folds in viewport + buffer\n436:     }\n437: }\n438: ```\n439: \n440: ### Incremental Updates\n441: \n442: When document changes:\n443: - Invalidate fold regions that overlap with edit\n444: - Shift line numbers for regions after edit\n445: - Recompute only affected regions\n446: \n447: ```rust\n448: fn update_folds_after_edit(\n449:     folding: &mut FoldingState,\n450:     edit_line: usize,\n451:     lines_delta: isize,\n452: ) {\n453:     // Remove regions that overlap with edit\n454:     folding.regions.retain(|r| {\n455:         !(r.start_line <= edit_line && r.end_line >= edit_line)\n456:     });\n457: \n458:     // Shift regions after edit\n459:     for region in &mut folding.regions {\n460:         if region.start_line > edit_line {\n461:             region.start_line = (region.start_line as isize + lines_delta) as usize;\n462:             region.end_line = (region.end_line as isize + lines_delta) as usize;\n463:         }\n464:     }\n465: }\n466: ```\n467: \n468: ---\n469: \n470: ## Testing\n471: \n472: ### Unit Tests\n473: \n474: ```rust\n475: #[test]\n476: fn test_detect_rust_function_folds() {\n477:     let source = r#\"\n478: fn main() {\n479:     println!(\"hello\");\n480: }\n481: \"#;\n482:     let folds = detect_syntax_folds(source, LanguageId::Rust);\n483:     assert_eq!(folds.len(), 1);\n484:     assert_eq!(folds[0].start_line, 1);\n485:     assert_eq!(folds[0].end_line, 3);\n486: }\n487: \n488: #[test]\n489: fn test_visual_line_mapping() {\n490:     // Fold lines 5-10 (6 lines hidden)\n491:     // Line 15 in doc should be visual line 9\n492: }\n493: \n494: #[test]\n495: fn test_fold_toggle() {\n496:     // Toggle fold, verify state changes\n497: }\n498: ```\n499: \n500: ### Manual Testing Checklist\n501: \n502: - [ ] Fold indicators appear in gutter\n503: - [ ] Click indicator toggles fold\n504: - [ ] Keyboard shortcuts work\n505: - [ ] Collapsed regions show placeholder\n506: - [ ] Cursor in collapsed region unfolds it\n507: - [ ] Fold state persists across restart\n508: - [ ] Works with soft-wrap enabled\n509: - [ ] Find highlights visible in collapsed regions\n510: \n511: ---\n512: \n513: ## Implementation Plan\n514: \n515: ### Phase 1: Core Infrastructure (1 week)\n516: \n517: 1. Define `FoldRegion`, `FoldingState` types\n518: 2. Integrate fold state into `EditorState`\n519: 3. Implement visual/document line mapping\n520: 4. Basic gutter rendering (indicators)\n521: \n522: ### Phase 2: Fold Detection (1 week)\n523: \n524: 1. Indentation-based detection\n525: 2. Syntax-based detection (extend syntax worker)\n526: 3. Region marker detection\n527: 4. Incremental updates\n528: \n529: ### Phase 3: User Interaction (3 days)\n530: \n531: 1. Message handlers (Toggle, FoldAll, etc.)\n532: 2. Key bindings\n533: 3. Mouse interaction (click gutter)\n534: 4. Command palette integration\n535: \n536: ### Phase 4: Visual Polish (3 days)\n537: \n538: 1. Collapsed region placeholder\n539: 2. Fold summary extraction\n540: 3. Theme support\n541: 4. Animation (optional)\n542: \n543: ### Phase 5: Integration (3 days)\n544: \n545: 1. Soft-wrap coordination\n546: 2. Find integration\n547: 3. Multi-cursor handling\n548: 4. Persistence\n549: \n550: ---\n551: \n552: ## Dependencies\n553: \n554: - **Soft-wrap (F-140)**: Required for visual line mapping\n555: - **Syntax Highlighting**: Reuse tree-sitter infrastructure\n556: - **Gutter Rendering**: Need clickable gutter regions\n557: \n558: ---\n559: \n560: ## References\n561: \n562: - [VS Code Folding](https://code.visualstudio.com/docs/editor/codebasics#_folding)\n563: - [JetBrains Folding](https://www.jetbrains.com/help/idea/code-folding.html)\n564: - [Neovim foldexpr](https://neovim.io/doc/user/fold.html)\n565: - Tree-sitter node traversal for fold detection",

  "content": "1: # Configurable Gesture Bindings\n2: \n3: **Status:** Planned\n4: **Created:** 2025-12-19\n5: **Effort:** M (3-5 sessions)\n6: \n7: ---\n8: \n9: ## Overview\n10: \n11: Enable configurable keybindings for temporal gestures like double-tap modifier keys. Currently, `Option+Option+Arrow` (double-tap Option then Arrow) is hardcoded for adding cursors above/below. This feature makes such gestures configurable through the keymap system.\n12: \n13: ### Goals\n14: \n15: 1. **Configurable double-tap bindings** — Express `alt+alt+up` in keymap.yaml\n16: 2. **Support all modifiers** — Alt, Ctrl, Shift, Cmd can all have double-tap gestures\n17: 3. **Clean syntax** — `key: alt+alt+down` instead of conditions\n18: 4. **Backward compatible** — Existing hardcoded behavior becomes default binding\n19: \n20: ### Non-Goals (This Phase)\n21: \n22: - Configurable timing window (hardcode 300ms)\n23: - Triple-tap detection\n24: - Hold-to-repeat gestures\n25: - Modifier-only bindings (double-tap must end with a key)\n26: \n27: ---\n28: \n29: ## Current Implementation\n30: \n31: ### Detection (`src/runtime/app.rs:404-421`)\n32: \n33: ```rust\n34: let is_option_key = matches!(\n35:     event.physical_key,\n36:     PhysicalKey::Code(KeyCode::AltLeft) | PhysicalKey::Code(KeyCode::AltRight)\n37: );\n38: \n39: if is_option_key {\n40:     if event.state == ElementState::Pressed && !event.repeat {\n41:         let now = Instant::now();\n42:         if let Some(last) = self.last_option_press {\n43:             if now.duration_since(last) < Duration::from_millis(300) {\n44:                 self.option_double_tapped = true;\n45:             }\n46:         }\n47:         self.last_option_press = Some(now);\n48:     } else if event.state == ElementState::Released {\n49:         self.option_double_tapped = false;\n50:     }\n51: }\n52: ```\n53: \n54: ### Handling (`src/runtime/input.rs:70-75`)\n55: \n56: ```rust\n57: Key::Named(NamedKey::ArrowUp) if alt && option_double_tapped => {\n58:     update(model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::AddCursorAbove))\n59: }\n60: Key::Named(NamedKey::ArrowDown) if alt && option_double_tapped => {\n61:     update(model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::AddCursorBelow))\n62: }\n63: ```\n64: \n65: ### Why It Bypasses the Keymap\n66: \n67: The keymap system is declarative and cannot express temporal constraints. The current code explicitly skips the keymap when `option_double_tapped && alt` is true.\n68: \n69: ---\n70: \n71: ## Proposed Design\n72: \n73: ### YAML Syntax\n74: \n75: ```yaml\n76: # Double-tap modifier expressed in key specification\n77: - key: alt+alt+up\n78:   command: AddCursorAbove\n79: \n80: - key: alt+alt+down\n81:   command: AddCursorBelow\n82: \n83: # Works with all modifiers\n84: - key: ctrl+ctrl+d\n85:   command: DuplicateLine\n86: \n87: - key: cmd+cmd+k\n88:   command: ToggleBookmark\n89: ```\n90: \n91: The parser recognizes `modifier+modifier` (same modifier twice) as a double-tap pattern.\n92: \n93: ### Extended Keystroke\n94: \n95: ```rust\n96: // src/keymap/types.rs\n97: \n98: pub enum ModifierKey {\n99:     Alt,\n100:     Ctrl,\n101:     Shift,\n102:     Cmd,\n103: }\n104: \n105: pub struct Keystroke {\n106:     pub key: KeyCode,\n107:     pub mods: Modifiers,\n108:     pub double_tap_mod: Option<ModifierKey>,  // NEW\n109: }\n110: ```\n111: \n112: ### Extended KeyContext\n113: \n114: ```rust\n115: // src/keymap/context.rs\n116: \n117: pub struct KeyContext {\n118:     // Existing...\n119:     pub has_selection: bool,\n120:     pub modal_active: bool,\n121: \n122:     // Gesture states (NEW)\n123:     pub alt_double_tap: bool,\n124:     pub ctrl_double_tap: bool,\n125:     pub shift_double_tap: bool,\n126:     pub cmd_double_tap: bool,\n127: }\n128: ```\n129: \n130: ### Gesture State Tracking\n131: \n132: ```rust\n133: // src/runtime/app.rs\n134: \n135: #[derive(Default)]\n136: struct GestureState {\n137:     last_press: Option<Instant>,\n138:     double_tapped: bool,\n139: }\n140: \n141: struct App {\n142:     // Replace single field with per-modifier tracking\n143:     alt_gesture: GestureState,\n144:     ctrl_gesture: GestureState,\n145:     shift_gesture: GestureState,\n146:     cmd_gesture: GestureState,\n147: }\n148: ```\n149: \n150: ---\n151: \n152: ## Implementation Plan\n153: \n154: ### Phase 1: Generalize Gesture Detection\n155: **File:** `src/runtime/app.rs`\n156: \n157: - Add `GestureState` struct with `last_press` and `double_tapped`\n158: - Track all 4 modifiers: Alt, Ctrl, Shift, Cmd\n159: - Update keyboard event handler to detect double-tap for each\n160: \n161: ### Phase 2: Extend KeyContext\n162: **File:** `src/keymap/context.rs`\n163: \n164: - Add `alt_double_tap`, `ctrl_double_tap`, `shift_double_tap`, `cmd_double_tap` fields\n165: - Wire up from App gesture state when creating context\n166: \n167: ### Phase 3: Extend Keystroke\n168: **File:** `src/keymap/types.rs`\n169: \n170: - Add `ModifierKey` enum\n171: - Add `double_tap_mod: Option<ModifierKey>` to Keystroke\n172: - Update `Keystroke::new()` and related constructors\n173: \n174: ### Phase 4: Update Config Parser\n175: **File:** `src/keymap/config.rs`\n176: \n177: - Parse `alt+alt+key` syntax\n178: - Detect repeated modifier (e.g., `alt+alt`) and set `double_tap_mod`\n179: - Handle all combinations: `ctrl+ctrl`, `cmd+cmd`, `shift+shift`\n180: \n181: ### Phase 5: Update Keymap Matching\n182: **File:** `src/keymap/keymap.rs`\n183: \n184: - When matching, check if `double_tap_mod` requirement is satisfied\n185: - Compare against corresponding `KeyContext` gesture field\n186: \n187: ### Phase 6: Remove Hardcoded Handling\n188: **Files:** `src/runtime/app.rs`, `src/runtime/input.rs`\n189: \n190: - Remove `skip_keymap` check for `option_double_tapped`\n191: - Remove hardcoded ArrowUp/ArrowDown handling in input.rs\n192: - Let keymap handle it naturally\n193: \n194: ### Phase 7: Add Default Bindings\n195: **File:** `keymap.yaml`\n196: \n197: ```yaml\n198: - key: alt+alt+up\n199:   command: AddCursorAbove\n200: - key: alt+alt+down\n201:   command: AddCursorBelow\n202: ```\n203: \n204: ### Phase 8: Testing\n205: \n206: - Unit tests for gesture detection timing\n207: - Unit tests for YAML parsing of double-modifier syntax\n208: - Integration tests for keymap matching with gesture context\n209: - Verify backward compatibility (default behavior unchanged)\n210: \n211: ---\n212: \n213: ## Edge Cases\n214: \n215: | Scenario | Behavior |\n216: |----------|----------|\n217: | Alt+Alt+Alt+Up | Third press within window keeps double_tapped true |\n218: | Alt release before arrow | double_tapped becomes false, binding doesn't match |\n219: | Alt+Ctrl+Up during alt_double_tap | Only matches if binding requires both (unlikely) |\n220: | Very fast typing 301ms+ | Gesture expires, treated as normal Alt+Up |\n221: \n222: ---\n223: \n224: ## Files to Modify\n225: \n226: | File | Changes |\n227: |------|---------|\n228: | `src/runtime/app.rs` | Generalize gesture tracking to all modifiers |\n229: | `src/keymap/types.rs` | Add `ModifierKey`, `double_tap_mod` to Keystroke |\n230: | `src/keymap/context.rs` | Add gesture state fields to KeyContext |\n231: | `src/keymap/config.rs` | Parse `alt+alt+key` syntax |\n232: | `src/keymap/keymap.rs` | Match double-tap bindings against gesture state |\n233: | `src/runtime/input.rs` | Remove hardcoded gesture handling |\n234: | `keymap.yaml` | Add `alt+alt+up/down` default bindings |\n235: \n236: ---\n237: \n238: ## Success Criteria\n239: \n240: 1. `alt+alt+up` and `alt+alt+down` work as before (default bindings)\n241: 2. Users can remap to different modifiers: `ctrl+ctrl+up`\n242: 3. Users can disable by binding to `Unbound`\n243: 4. No timing window configuration needed (300ms hardcoded)\n244: 5. All existing tests pass",
