[Overview](#overview)\n16: 2. [Architecture](#architecture)\n17: 3. [Data Structures](#data-structures)\n18: 4. [Session File Format](#session-file-format)\n19: 5. [Implementation Plan](#implementation-plan)\n20: 6. [Testing Strategy](#testing-strategy)\n21: 7. [References](#references)\n22: \n23: ---\n24: \n25: ## Overview\n26: \n27: ### Current State\n28: \n29: The editor currently:\n30: - Opens files specified on command line\n31: - Has `EditorArea` with split views, tabs, and groups\n32: - Tracks cursor position per editor (`EditorState`)\n33: - Has workspace concept with sidebar state\n34: - Does NOT persist any state between sessions\n35: \n36: ### Goals\n37: \n38: 1. **Persist open files**: Remember which files were open\n39: 2. **Restore split layout**: Recreate horizontal/vertical splits with ratios\n40: 3. **Restore cursor positions**: Return to exact line/column in each file\n41: 4. **Restore scroll positions**: Return to same viewport\n42: 5. **Restore selection state**: Preserve selections and multi-cursors\n43: 6. **Workspace-aware**: Separate sessions per workspace\n44: 7. **Graceful degradation**: Handle missing files, changed files, moved workspaces\n45: \n46: ### Non-Goals\n47: \n48: - Undo history persistence (complex, large, fragile)\n49: - Unsaved file content persistence (\"hot exit\" pattern - separate feature)\n50: - Recent files list across sessions (simpler feature, could be part of this)\n51: - Project-level settings (separate config feature)\n52: \n53: ---\n54: \n55: ## Architecture\n56: \n57: ```\n58: ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n59: │                              Application Lifecycle                           │\n60: │                                                                              │\n61: │  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n62: │  │                         Startup                                       │   │\n63: │  │                                                                       │   │\n64: │  │   1. Parse CLI args                                                   │   │\n65: │  │   2. Determine workspace root (if any)                                │   │\n66: │  │   3. Load session file:                                               │   │\n67: │  │        ~/.config/token-editor/sessions/<workspace-hash>.json          │   │\n68: │  │      OR ~/.config/token-editor/sessions/default.json                  │   │\n69: │  │   4. Validate session (files exist, etc.)                             │   │\n70: │  │   5. Merge CLI args with session (CLI takes precedence)               │   │\n71: │  │   6. Build AppModel with restored state                               │   │\n72: │  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n73: │                                                                              │\n74: │  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n75: │  │                         Shutdown                                      │   │\n76: │  │                                                                       │   │\n77: │  │   1. Collect current session state from AppModel                      │   │\n78: │  │   2. Serialize to SessionData                                         │   │\n79: │  │   3. Write to session file (atomic write with temp file)              │   │\n80: │  │   4. Handle write errors gracefully (log, don't crash)                │   │\n81: │  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n82: │                                                                              │\n83: │  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n84: │  │                      Periodic Autosave (Optional)                     │   │\n85: │  │                                                                       │   │\n86: │  │   Every 60 seconds (configurable), snapshot session state             │   │\n87: │  │   Protects against crashes losing session data                        │   │\n88: │  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n89: │                                                                              │\n90: └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n91: ```\n92: \n93: ### Module Structure\n94: \n95: ```\n96: src/\n97: ├── session.rs                # NEW: SessionData, load/save functions\n98: ├── cli.rs                    # Integrate session loading\n99: ├── main.rs                   # Call session restore\n100: ├── app.rs                    # Save session on close\n101: └── model/\n102:     ├── mod.rs                # AppModel::from_session()\n103:     ├── editor_area.rs        # EditorArea::from_session()\n104:     └── editor.rs             # EditorState::from_session()\n105: \n106: ~/.config/token-editor/\n107: ├── config.yaml               # User configuration\n108: ├── keymap.yaml               # Key bindings\n109: └── sessions/                 # NEW: Session storage\n110:     ├── default.json          # Session for no-workspace mode\n111:     ├── a1b2c3d4.json         # Session for workspace (hashed path)\n112:     └── ...\n113: ```\n114: \n115: ### Session Storage Location\n116: \n117: ```\n118: Workspace mode:\n119:   ~/.config/token-editor/sessions/<hash>.json\n120:   where hash = blake3(canonical_workspace_path)[0..8]\n121: \n122: No workspace mode:\n123:   ~/.config/token-editor/sessions/default.json\n124: \n125: Session filename example:\n126:   /home/user/projects/my-app  →  sessions/a1b2c3d4.json\n127: ```\n128: \n129: ---\n130: \n131: ## Data Structures\n132: \n133: ### SessionData (Persisted)\n134: \n135: ```rust\n136: // In src/session.rs\n137: \n138: use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\n139: use std::path::PathBuf;\n140: \n141: /// Complete session state for persistence\n142: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n143: pub struct SessionData {\n144:     /// Session format version (for migration)\n145:     pub version: u32,\n146: \n147:     /// When this session was last saved\n148:     pub timestamp: chrono::DateTime<chrono::Utc>,\n149: \n150:     /// Workspace root (None for no-workspace mode)\n151:     pub workspace_root: Option<PathBuf>,\n152: \n153:     /// Editor layout\n154:     pub layout: LayoutData,\n155: \n156:     /// All open documents with their state\n157:     pub documents: Vec<DocumentSessionData>,\n158: \n159:     /// Sidebar state\n160:     pub sidebar: Option<SidebarData>,\n161: \n162:     /// Window geometry (optional, for multi-monitor support)\n163:     pub window: Option<WindowData>,\n164: }\n165: \n166: /// Current session format version\n167: pub const SESSION_VERSION: u32 = 1;\n168: \n169: impl SessionData {\n170:     pub fn new() -> Self {\n171:         Self {\n172:             version: SESSION_VERSION,\n173:             timestamp: chrono::Utc::now(),\n174:             workspace_root: None,\n175:             layout: LayoutData::Single { group: GroupData::empty() },\n176:             documents: Vec::new(),\n177:             sidebar: None,\n178:             window: None,\n179:         }\n180:     }\n181: }\n182: \n183: /// Editor layout tree (mirrors LayoutNode)\n184: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n185: #[serde(tag = \"type\", rename_all = \"snake_case\")]\n186: pub enum LayoutData {\n187:     /// Single group (no splits)\n188:     Single { group: GroupData },\n189: \n190:     /// Split container with children\n191:     Split {\n192:         direction: SplitDirectionData,\n193:         children: Vec<LayoutData>,\n194:         ratios: Vec<f32>,\n195:     },\n196: }\n197: \n198: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n199: #[serde(rename_all = \"snake_case\")]\n200: pub enum SplitDirectionData {\n201:     Horizontal,\n202:     Vertical,\n203: }\n204: \n205: /// Editor group (pane with tabs)\n206: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n207: pub struct GroupData {\n208:     /// Document indices (into SessionData.documents)\n209:     pub tab_document_indices: Vec<usize>,\n210: \n211:     /// Active tab index\n212:     pub active_tab: usize,\n213: }\n214: \n215: impl GroupData {\n216:     pub fn empty() -> Self {\n217:         Self {\n218:             tab_document_indices: Vec::new(),\n219:             active_tab: 0,\n220:         }\n221:     }\n222: }\n223: \n224: /// Document state for session\n225: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n226: pub struct DocumentSessionData {\n227:     /// File path (absolute or relative to workspace)\n228:     pub path: PathBuf,\n229: \n230:     /// Whether path is relative to workspace root\n231:     pub path_is_relative: bool,\n232: \n233:     /// Editor state for this document\n234:     pub editor_state: EditorSessionData,\n235: \n236:     /// Whether document had unsaved changes (warning on restore)\n237:     pub was_modified: bool,\n238: }\n239: \n240: /// Editor state for session (view-specific)\n241: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n242: pub struct EditorSessionData {\n243:     /// Primary cursor position\n244:     pub cursor: CursorData,\n245: \n246:     /// Additional cursors (for multi-cursor)\n247:     pub additional_cursors: Vec<CursorData>,\n248: \n249:     /// Selection state (parallel to cursors)\n250:     pub selections: Vec<SelectionData>,\n251: \n252:     /// Viewport scroll position\n253:     pub viewport: ViewportData,\n254: \n255:     /// View mode (text/csv)\n256:     pub view_mode: ViewModeData,\n257: }\n258: \n259: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n260: pub struct CursorData {\n261:     pub line: usize,\n262:     pub column: usize,\n263: }\n264: \n265: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n266: pub struct SelectionData {\n267:     pub anchor_line: usize,\n268:     pub anchor_column: usize,\n269:     pub head_line: usize,\n270:     pub head_column: usize,\n271: }\n272: \n273: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n274: pub struct ViewportData {\n275:     pub top_line: usize,\n276:     pub left_column: usize,\n277: }\n278: \n279: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n280: #[serde(rename_all = \"snake_case\")]\n281: pub enum ViewModeData {\n282:     Text,\n283:     Csv,\n284: }\n285: \n286: /// Sidebar state\n287: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n288: pub struct SidebarData {\n289:     /// Whether sidebar is visible\n290:     pub visible: bool,\n291: \n292:     /// Width in logical pixels\n293:     pub width: f32,\n294: \n295:     /// Expanded folder paths (relative to workspace)\n296:     pub expanded_folders: Vec<PathBuf>,\n297: \n298:     /// Selected item path (relative to workspace)\n299:     pub selected_item: Option<PathBuf>,\n300: \n301:     /// Scroll position\n302:     pub scroll_offset: usize,\n303: }\n304: \n305: /// Window geometry\n306: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n307: pub struct WindowData {\n308:     /// Window position (if available)\n309:     pub position: Option<(i32, i32)>,\n310: \n311:     /// Window size\n312:     pub size: (u32, u32),\n313: \n314:     /// Whether window was maximized\n315:     pub maximized: bool,\n316: }\n317: ```\n318: \n319: ### Session Manager\n320: \n321: ```rust\n322: // In src/session.rs\n323: \n324: use std::fs;\n325: use std::path::PathBuf;\n326: \n327: use crate::config_paths;\n328: \n329: /// Manages session loading and saving\n330: pub struct SessionManager {\n331:     /// Path to the session file\n332:     session_path: PathBuf,\n333: \n334:     /// Last saved session (for dirty checking)\n335:     last_saved: Option<SessionData>,\n336: }\n337: \n338: impl SessionManager {\n339:     /// Create session manager for a workspace\n340:     pub fn for_workspace(workspace_root: Option<&PathBuf>) -> Self {\n341:         let session_path = Self::session_file_path(workspace_root);\n342:         Self {\n343:             session_path,\n344:             last_saved: None,\n345:         }\n346:     }\n347: \n348:     /// Get the session file path for a workspace\n349:     fn session_file_path(workspace_root: Option<&PathBuf>) -> PathBuf {\n350:         let sessions_dir = config_paths::sessions_dir();\n351: \n352:         match workspace_root {\n353:             Some(root) => {\n354:                 let hash = Self::hash_path(root);\n355:                 sessions_dir.join(format!(\"{}.json\", hash))\n356:             }\n357:             None => sessions_dir.join(\"default.json\"),\n358:         }\n359:     }\n360: \n361:     /// Hash a path for use as filename\n362:     fn hash_path(path: &PathBuf) -> String {\n363:         use std::collections::hash_map::DefaultHasher;\n364:         use std::hash::{Hash, Hasher};\n365: \n366:         let canonical = path.canonicalize().unwrap_or_else(|_| path.clone());\n367:         let mut hasher = DefaultHasher::new();\n368:         canonical.hash(&mut hasher);\n369:         format!(\"{:016x}\", hasher.finish())[..8].to_string()\n370:     }\n371: \n372:     /// Load session from disk\n373:     pub fn load(&mut self) -> Result<SessionData, SessionLoadError> {\n374:         if !self.session_path.exists() {\n375:             return Err(SessionLoadError::NotFound);\n376:         }\n377: \n378:         let content = fs::read_to_string(&self.session_path)\n379:             .map_err(SessionLoadError::Io)?;\n380: \n381:         let session: SessionData = serde_json::from_str(&content)\n382:             .map_err(SessionLoadError::Parse)?;\n383: \n384:         // Check version compatibility\n385:         if session.version > SESSION_VERSION {\n386:             return Err(SessionLoadError::FutureVersion {\n387:                 file_version: session.version,\n388:                 supported_version: SESSION_VERSION,\n389:             });\n390:         }\n391: \n392:         // TODO: Migrate older versions if needed\n393: \n394:         self.last_saved = Some(session.clone());\n395:         Ok(session)\n396:     }\n397: \n398:     /// Save session to disk (atomic write)\n399:     pub fn save(&mut self, session: &SessionData) -> Result<(), SessionSaveError> {\n400:         // Ensure sessions directory exists\n401:         let sessions_dir = self.session_path.parent()\n402:             .ok_or_else(|| SessionSaveError::InvalidPath)?;\n403: \n404:         fs::create_dir_all(sessions_dir)\n405:             .map_err(SessionSaveError::Io)?;\n406: \n407:         // Serialize to JSON\n408:         let content = serde_json::to_string_pretty(session)\n409:             .map_err(SessionSaveError::Serialize)?;\n410: \n411:         // Atomic write via temp file\n412:         let temp_path = self.session_path.with_extension(\"json.tmp\");\n413:         fs::write(&temp_path, &content)\n414:             .map_err(SessionSaveError::Io)?;\n415: \n416:         fs::rename(&temp_path, &self.session_path)\n417:             .map_err(SessionSaveError::Io)?;\n418: \n419:         self.last_saved = Some(session.clone());\n420:         Ok(())\n421:     }\n422: \n423:     /// Check if session has changed since last save\n424:     pub fn has_changes(&self, current: &SessionData) -> bool {\n425:         match &self.last_saved {\n426:             Some(saved) => {\n427:                 // Compare relevant fields (ignore timestamp)\n428:                 saved.documents.len() != current.documents.len()\n429:                     || !matches!(&saved.layout, l if format!(\"{:?}\", l) == format!(\"{:?}\", &current.layout))\n430:             }\n431:             None => true,\n432:         }\n433:     }\n434: }\n435: \n436: #[derive(Debug)]\n437: pub enum SessionLoadError {\n438:     NotFound,\n439:     Io(std::io::Error),\n440:     Parse(serde_json::Error),\n441:     FutureVersion { file_version: u32, supported_version: u32 },\n442: }\n443: \n444: #[derive(Debug)]\n445: pub enum SessionSaveError {\n446:     InvalidPath,\n447:     Io(std::io::Error),\n448:     Serialize(serde_json::Error),\n449: }\n450: ```\n451: \n452: ### Conversion Traits\n453: \n454: ```rust\n455: // In src/session.rs\n456: \n457: use crate::model::{\n458:     AppModel, EditorArea, EditorState, EditorGroup, Document,\n459:     Cursor, Selection, Position, Viewport, LayoutNode, SplitDirection,\n460: };\n461: \n462: impl SessionData {\n463:     /// Create session data from current app state\n464:     pub fn from_app_model(model: &AppModel) -> Self {\n465:         let mut session = SessionData::new();\n466: \n467:         session.workspace_root = model.workspace.as_ref().map(|w| w.root.clone());\n468: \n469:         // Collect all documents with their paths\n470:         let mut doc_index_map = std::collections::HashMap::new();\n471:         for (doc_id, doc) in &model.editor_area.documents {\n472:             if let Some(path) = &doc.file_path {\n473:                 let index = session.documents.len();\n474:                 doc_index_map.insert(*doc_id, index);\n475: \n476:                 // Find editor state for this document\n477:                 let editor_state = model.editor_area.editors.values()\n478:                     .find(|e| e.document_id == Some(*doc_id))\n479:                     .map(EditorSessionData::from_editor_state)\n480:                     .unwrap_or_default();\n481: \n482:                 session.documents.push(DocumentSessionData {\n483:                     path: Self::make_path_relative(path, &session.workspace_root),\n484:                     path_is_relative: session.workspace_root.is_some(),\n485:                     editor_state,\n486:                     was_modified: doc.is_modified,\n487:                 });\n488:             }\n489:         }\n490: \n491:         // Convert layout tree\n492:         session.layout = LayoutData::from_layout_node(\n493:             &model.editor_area.layout,\n494:             &model.editor_area,\n495:             &doc_index_map,\n496:         );\n497: \n498:         // Sidebar state\n499:         if let Some(workspace) = &model.workspace {\n500:             session.sidebar = Some(SidebarData {\n[... omitted lines 501 to 508 ...]\n509:         // Window state\n510:         session.window = Some(WindowData {\n511:             position: None, // TODO: Get from window\n512:             size: model.window_size,\n513:             maximized: false, // TODO: Get from window\n514:         });\n515: \n516:         session.timestamp = chrono::Utc::now();\n517:         session\n518:     }\n519: \n520:     fn make_path_relative(path: &PathBuf, workspace: &Option<PathBuf>) -> PathBuf {\n521:         if let Some(root) = workspace {\n522:             if let Ok(relative) = path.strip_prefix(root) {\n523:                 return relative.to_path_buf();\n524:             }\n525:         }\n526:         path.clone()\n527:     }\n528: }\n529: \n530: impl EditorSessionData {\n531:     fn from_editor_state(state: &EditorState) -> Self {\n532:         Self {\n533:             cursor: CursorData {\n534:                 line: state.active_cursor().line,\n535:                 column: state.active_cursor().column,\n536:             },\n537:             additional_cursors: state.cursors.iter()\n538:                 .skip(1)\n539:                 .map(|c| CursorData { line: c.line, column: c.column })\n540:                 .collect(),\n541:             selections: state.selections.iter()\n542:                 .map(|s| SelectionData {\n543:                     anchor_line: s.anchor.line,\n544:                     anchor_column: s.anchor.column,\n545:                     head_line: s.head.line,\n546:                     head_column: s.head.column,\n547:                 })\n548:                 .collect(),\n549:             viewport: ViewportData {\n550:                 top_line: state.viewport.top_line,\n551:                 left_column: state.viewport.left_column,\n552:             },\n553:             view_mode: if state.view_mode.is_csv() {\n554:                 ViewModeData::Csv\n555:             } else {\n556:                 ViewModeData::Text\n557:             },\n558:         }\n559:     }\n560: }\n561: \n562: impl Default for EditorSessionData {\n563:     fn default() -> Self {\n564:         Self {\n565:             cursor: CursorData { line: 0, column: 0 },\n566:             additional_cursors: Vec::new(),\n567:             selections: Vec::new(),\n568:             viewport: ViewportData { top_line: 0, left_column: 0 },\n569:             view_mode: ViewModeData::Text,\n570:         }\n571:     }\n572: }\n573: \n574: impl LayoutData {\n575:     fn from_layout_node(\n576:         node: &LayoutNode,\n577:         area: &EditorArea,\n578:         doc_index_map: &std::collections::HashMap<crate::model::DocumentId, usize>,\n579:     ) -> Self {\n580:         match node {\n581:             LayoutNode::Group(group_id) => {\n582:                 if let Some(group) = area.groups.get(group_id) {\n583:                     LayoutData::Single {\n584:                         group: GroupData::from_group(group, area, doc_index_map),\n585:                     }\n586:                 } else {\n587:                     LayoutData::Single { group: GroupData::empty() }\n588:                 }\n589:             }\n590:             LayoutNode::Split(container) => {\n591:                 LayoutData::Split {\n592:                     direction: match container.direction {\n593:                         SplitDirection::Horizontal => SplitDirectionData::Horizontal,\n594:                         SplitDirection::Vertical => SplitDirectionData::Vertical,\n595:                     },\n596:                     children: container.children.iter()\n597:                         .map(|child| Self::from_layout_node(child, area, doc_index_map))\n598:                         .collect(),\n599:                     ratios: container.ratios.clone(),\n600:                 }\n601:             }\n602:         }\n603:     }\n604: }\n605: \n606: impl GroupData {\n607:     fn from_group(\n608:         group: &EditorGroup,\n609:         area: &EditorArea,\n610:         doc_index_map: &std::collections::HashMap<crate::model::DocumentId, usize>,\n611:     ) -> Self {\n612:         let tab_document_indices: Vec<usize> = group.tabs.iter()\n613:             .filter_map(|tab| {\n614:                 let editor = area.editors.get(&tab.editor_id)?;\n615:                 let doc_id = editor.document_id?;\n616:                 doc_index_map.get(&doc_id).copied()\n617:             })\n618:             .collect();\n619: \n620:         Self {\n621:             tab_document_indices,\n622:             active_tab: group.active_tab_index.min(tab_document_indices.len().saturating_sub(1)),\n623:         }\n624:     }\n625: }\n626: ```\n627: \n628: ---\n629: \n630: ## Session File Format\n631: \n632: ### Example Session File\n633: \n634: ```json\n635: {\n636:   \"version\": 1,\n637:   \"timestamp\": \"2025-12-19T10:30:00Z\",\n638:   \"workspace_root\": \"/home/user/projects/my-app\",\n639:   \"layout\": {\n640:     \"type\": \"split\",\n641:     \"direction\": \"horizontal\",\n642:     \"ratios\": [0.5, 0.5],\n643:     \"children\": [\n644:       {\n645:         \"type\": \"single\",\n646:         \"group\": {\n647:           \"tab_document_indices\": [0, 1],\n648:           \"active_tab\": 0\n649:         }\n650:       },\n651:       {\n652:         \"type\": \"single\",\n653:         \"group\": {\n654:           \"tab_document_indices\": [2],\n655:           \"active_tab\": 0\n656:         }\n657:       }\n658:     ]\n659:   },\n660:   \"documents\": [\n661:     {\n662:       \"path\": \"src/main.rs\",\n663:       \"path_is_relative\": true,\n664:       \"editor_state\": {\n665:         \"cursor\": { \"line\": 42, \"column\": 15 },\n666:         \"additional_cursors\": [],\n667:         \"selections\": [\n668:           {\n669:             \"anchor_line\": 42, \"anchor_column\": 15,\n670:             \"head_line\": 42, \"head_column\": 15\n671:           }\n672:         ],\n673:         \"viewport\": { \"top_line\": 30, \"left_column\": 0 },\n674:         \"view_mode\": \"text\"\n675:       },\n676:       \"was_modified\": false\n677:     },\n678:     {\n679:       \"path\": \"src/lib.rs\",\n680:       \"path_is_relative\": true,\n681:       \"editor_state\": {\n682:         \"cursor\": { \"line\": 0, \"column\": 0 },\n683:         \"additional_cursors\": [],\n684:         \"selections\": [],\n685:         \"viewport\": { \"top_line\": 0, \"left_column\": 0 },\n686:         \"view_mode\": \"text\"\n687:       },\n688:       \"was_modified\": true\n689:     },\n690:     {\n691:       \"path\": \"README.md\",\n692:       \"path_is_relative\": true,\n693:       \"editor_state\": {\n694:         \"cursor\": { \"line\": 10, \"column\": 0 },\n695:         \"additional_cursors\": [],\n696:         \"selections\": [],\n697:         \"viewport\": { \"top_line\": 0, \"left_column\": 0 },\n698:         \"view_mode\": \"text\"\n699:       },\n700:       \"was_modified\": false\n701:     }\n702:   ],\n703:   \"sidebar\": {\n704:     \"visible\": true,\n705:     \"width\": 250.0,\n706:     \"expanded_folders\": [\"src\", \"docs\"],\n707:     \"selected_item\": \"src/main.rs\",\n708:     \"scroll_offset\": 0\n709:   },\n710:   \"window\": {\n711:     \"position\": [100, 50],\n712:     \"size\": [1200, 800],\n713:     \"maximized\": false\n714:   }\n715: }\n716: ```\n717: \n718: ---\n719: \n720: ## Implementation Plan\n721: \n722: ### Phase 1: Session Infrastructure\n723: \n724: **Estimated effort: 2-3 days**\n725: \n726: 1. [ ] Add `chrono` and `serde_json` dependencies\n727: 2. [ ] Create `src/session.rs` with data structures\n728: 3. [ ] Add `sessions_dir()` to `config_paths.rs`\n729: 4. [ ] Implement `SessionManager::load()` and `save()`\n730: 5. #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n812: #[serde(rename_all = \"snake_case\")]\n813: pub struct SessionConfig {\n814:     /// Whether to restore sessions on startup\n815: session files to keep\n827:     #[serde(default = \"default_max_sessions\")]\n828:     pub max_sessions: usize,\n829: }\n830: \n831: fn default_max_sessions() -> usize {\n832:     50\n833: }\n834: ```\n835: \n836: ```yaml\n837: # config.yaml\n838: session:\n839:   restore_session: true\n840:   save_session: true\n841:   auto_save_interval_secs: 60  # 0 to disable\n842:   max_sessions: 50\n843: ```\n844: \n845: ---\n846: \n847: ## Testing Strategy\n848: \n849: ### Unit Tests\n850: \n851: ```rust\n852: #[cfg(test)]\n853: mod tests {\n854:     use super::*;\n855: \n856:     #[test]\n857: assert_eq!(parsed.version, SESSION_VERSION);\n879:         assert_eq!(parsed.documents.len(), 1);\n880:     }\n881: \n882:     #[test]\n883:     fn test_path_hashing_consistent() {\n884:         let hash2);\n888:         assert_eq!(hash1.len(), 8);\n889:     }\n890: \n891:     #[test]\n892:     fn test_path_hashing_different() {\n893:         let path1 = SessionManager::hash_path(&path2);\n897:         assert_ne!(hash1, hash2);\n898:     }\n899: \n900:     #[test]\n901:     fn test_relative_path_conversion() {\n902:         let workspace = &workspace);\n906:         assert_eq!(relative, PathBuf::from(\"src/main.rs\"));\n907:     }\n908: }\n909: ```\n910: \n911: ### Integration Tests\n912: \n913: ```rust\n914: #[test]\n915: fn test_session_save_and_load() {\n916:     let temp_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();\n917:     let workspace = temp_dir.path().to_path_buf();\n918: \n919:     // Create test files\n920:     std::fs::create_dir_all(workspace.join(\"src\")).unwrap();\n921:     std::fs::write(workspace.join(\"src/main.rs\"), \"fn main() {}\").unwrap();\n922: \n923:     // Create app with open files\n924:     let mut app = test_app_with_workspace(&workspace);\n925:     app.open_file(&workspace.join(\"src/main.rs\"));\n926: \n927:     // Save session\n928:     let session = SessionData::from_app_model(&app.model);\n929:     let mut manager = SessionManager::for_workspace(Some(&workspace));\n930:     manager.save(&session).unwrap();\n931: \n932:     // Load session\n933:     let loaded = manager.load().unwrap();\n934: \n935:     assert_eq!(loaded.documents.len(), 1);\n936:     assert_eq!(loaded.documents[0].path, PathBuf::from(\"src/main.rs\"));\n937: }\n938: \n939: #[test]\n940: fn test_session_handles_missing_files() {\n941:     let temp_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();\n942:     let workspace = temp_dir.path().to_path_buf();\n943: \n944:     // Create session referencing non-existent file\n945:     let session = SessionData {\n946:         documents: vec![\n947:             DocumentSessionData {\n948:                 path: PathBuf::from(\"deleted.rs\"),\n949:                 path_is_relative: true,\n950:                 editor_state: EditorSessionData::default(),\n951:                 was_modified: false,\n952:             }\n953:         ],\n954:         ..SessionData::new()\n955:     };\n956: \n957:     // Restore should skip missing file\n958:     let model = AppModel::from_session(session, &workspace, 800, 600, 1.0);\n959: \n960:     assert!(model.editor_area.documents.is_empty()\n961:         || model.editor_area.documents.values().all(|d| d.file_path.is_none()));\n962: }\n963: ```\n964: \n965: ### Manual Testing Checklist\n966: \n967: - [ ] Open multiple files, split view, close editor, reopen\n968: - [ ] Verify cursor positions restored\n969: - [ ] Verify scroll positions restored\n970: - [ ] Verify split layout restored with correct ratios\n971: - [ ] Verify sidebar expanded folders restored\n972: - [ ] Delete file from session, reopen, verify graceful handling\n973: - [ ] `--no-session` flag works\n974: - [ ] CLI files override session\n975: - [ ] Multiple workspaces have separate sessions\n976: - [ ] No-workspace mode has default session\n977: \n978: ---\n979: \n980: ## Edge Cases and Invariants\n981: \n982: ### Edge Cases\n983: \n984: 1. **File moved/renamed**: Path no longer valid; skip file, log warning\n985: 2. **File deleted**: Skip file, potentially warn user\n986: 3. **Workspace moved**: Session hash changes; effectively new workspace\n987: 4. **Permissions changed**: Handle read errors gracefully\n988: 5. **Corrupted session**: Fall back to clean state with warning\n989: 6. **Future version**: Refuse to load, suggest upgrade\n990: 7. **Very old session**: Consider staleness; maybe don't restore\n991: \n992: ### Invariants\n993: \n994: 1. Session file is always valid JSON (or missing)\n995: 2. Session version <= `SESSION_VERSION`\n996: 3. Cursor positions are validated against actual document length\n997: 4. Layout tree always has at least one group\n998: 5. Tab indices are within bounds of tab list\n999: \n1000: ---\n1001: \n1002: ## References\n1003: \n1004: - VS Code workspace state: https://code.visualstudio.com/docs/getstarted/settings#_workspace-settings\n1005: - JetBrains project state: `.idea/` directory structure\n1006: - Existing EditorArea: `src/model/editor_area.rs`\n1007: - CLI handling: `src/cli.rs`\n1008: - Config paths: `src/config_paths.rs`",

  "content": "1: # Soft Wrap\n2: \n3: Word wrapping without modifying the document\n4: \n5: > **Status:** Planned\n6: > **Priority:** P2\n7: > **Effort:** XL\n8: > **Created:** 2025-12-19\n9: > **Milestone:** 4 - Hard Problems\n10: \n11: ---\n12: \n13: ## Table of Contents\n14: \n15: 1. [Overview](#overview)\n16: 2. [Architecture](#architecture)\n17: 3. [Data Structures](#data-structures)\n18: 4. [Keybindings](#keybindings)\n19: 5. [Implementation Plan](#implementation-plan)\n20: 6. [Testing Strategy](#testing-strategy)\n21: 7. [References](#references)\n22: \n23: ---\n24: \n25: ## Overview\n26: \n27: ### Current State\n28: \n29: The editor currently:\n30: - Uses horizontal scrolling for long lines\n31: - Renders one logical line = one visual line\n32: - Cursor positions are `(line, column)` in logical coordinates\n33: - Viewport tracks `top_line` and `left_column` for scrolling\n34: \n35: ### Goals\n36: \n37: 1. **Per-pane soft wrap toggle**: Each editor view can independently enable/disable wrapping\n38: 2. **Logical/visual line mapping**: Cursor operations work on logical lines, display uses visual lines\n39: 3. **Wrap at word boundaries**: Prefer breaking at whitespace\n40: 4. **Preserve cursor position**: Toggle wrap without jumping cursor\n41: 5. **Correct scrolling**: Viewport calculates visible area based on visual lines\n42: 6. **Gutter line numbers**: Show logical line numbers, with continuation indicator for wrapped lines\n43: 7. **Selection across wraps**: Selections that span wrapped lines render correctly\n44: \n45: ### Non-Goals\n46: \n47: - Hard wrap (reformatting document)\n48: - Wrap width configuration (use viewport width)\n49: - Wrap at character level only (always prefer word boundaries)\n50: - Bidirectional text support\n51: \n52: ---\n53: \n54: ## Architecture\n55: \n56: ```\n57: ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n58: │                         Soft Wrap Conceptual Model                           │\n59: │                                                                              │\n60: │  Logical Document:                                                           │\n61: │  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n62: │  │ Line 0: \"This is a very long line that needs to wrap around...\"      │   │\n63: │  │ Line 1: \"Short line\"                                                 │   │\n64: │  │ Line 2: \"Another long line that will require wrapping at the...\"    │   │\n65: │  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n66: │                                                                              │\n67: │  Visual Lines (with wrap width = 30):                                        │\n68: │  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n69: │  │ VLine 0: \"This is a very long line \"      ← Logical Line 0, part 1  │   │\n70: │  │ VLine 1: \"that needs to wrap around...\"   ← Logical Line 0, part 2  │   │\n71: │  │ VLine 2: \"Short line\"                     ← Logical Line 1          │   │\n72: │  │ VLine 3: \"Another long line that \"        ← Logical Line 2, part 1  │   │\n73: │  │ VLine 4: \"will require wrapping at \"      ← Logical Line 2, part 2  │   │\n74: │  │ VLine 5: \"the...\"                         ← Logical Line 2, part 3  │   │\n75: │  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n76: │                                                                              │\n77: │  WrapCache:                                                                  │\n78: │  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n79: │  │ logical_line 0 → [WrapSegment { start: 0, len: 26, visual_line: 0 }, │   │\n80: │  │                   WrapSegment { start: 26, len: 24, visual_line: 1 }]│   │\n81: │  │ logical_line 1 → [WrapSegment { start: 0, len: 10, visual_line: 2 }] │   │\n82: │  │ logical_line 2 → [WrapSegment { start: 0, len: 22, visual_line: 3 }, │   │\n83: │  │                   WrapSegment { start: 22, len: 23, visual_line: 4 },│   │\n84: │  │                   WrapSegment { start: 45, len: 6, visual_line: 5 }] │   │\n85: │  │                                                                       │   │\n86: │  │ total_visual_lines: 6                                                 │   │\n87: │  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n88: │                                                                              │\n89: └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n90: ```\n91: \n92: ### Coordinate Systems\n93: \n94: ```\n95: Logical Coordinates: (line, column)\n96:   - Used by: Cursor, Selection, Document operations\n97:   - Line = index into rope.line(n)\n98:   - Column = character offset within that line\n99: \n100: Visual Coordinates: (visual_line, visual_column)\n101:   - Used by: Rendering, Mouse hit-testing\n102:   - Visual_line = index after wrapping\n103:   - Visual_column = offset within that visual line segment\n104: \n105: Conversion:\n106:   logical_to_visual(line, col) → (visual_line, visual_col)\n107:   visual_to_logical(visual_line, visual_col) → (line, col)\n108: ```\n109: \n110: ### Module Structure\n111: \n112: ```\n113: src/\n114: ├── wrap.rs                   # NEW: WrapCache, wrap computation\n115: ├── model/\n116: │   └── editor.rs             # Add soft_wrap: bool, wrap_cache: WrapCache\n117: ├── view.rs                   # Use wrap cache for rendering\n118: ├── update/\n119: │   └── editor.rs             # Coordinate conversion in cursor movement\n120: └── input.rs                  # Mouse position conversion\n121: ```\n122: \n123: ---\n124: \n125: ## Data Structures\n126: \n127: ### WrapCache\n128: \n129: ```rust\n130: // In src/wrap.rs\n131: \n132: use std::collections::HashMap;\n133: \n134: /// Cache of wrap information for a document\n135: ///\n136: /// This cache is rebuilt when:\n137: /// - Document content changes (edit, load)\n138: /// - Viewport width changes (resize, split)\n139: /// - Soft wrap is toggled on\n140: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n141: pub struct WrapCache {\n142:     /// Wrap segments per logical line\n143:     /// Key: logical line index\n144:     /// Value: list of segments that line wraps into\n145:     lines: HashMap<usize, Vec<WrapSegment>>,\n146: \n147:     /// Total number of visual lines (sum of all segments)\n148:     pub total_visual_lines: usize,\n149: \n150:     /// Width used for wrapping (in characters)\n151:     wrap_width: usize,\n152: \n153:     /// Document revision when cache was built\n154:     revision: u64,\n155: \n156:     /// Whether cache is valid\n157:     valid: bool,\n158: }\n159: \n160: /// A segment of a logical line after wrapping\n161: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]\n162: pub struct WrapSegment {\n163:     /// Character offset within the logical line where this segment starts\n164:     pub start_col: usize,\n165: \n166:     /// Number of characters in this segment\n167:     pub len: usize,\n168: \n169:     /// Visual line index (global, not per-logical-line)\n170:     pub visual_line: usize,\n171: \n172:     /// Whether this is a continuation (not the first segment)\n173:     pub is_continuation: bool,\n174: }\n175: \n176: impl WrapCache {\n177:     pub fn new() -> Self {\n178:         Self {\n179:             lines: HashMap::new(),\n180:             total_visual_lines: 0,\n181:             wrap_width: 80,\n182:             revision: 0,\n183:             valid: false,\n184:         }\n185:     }\n186: \n187:     /// Invalidate the cache (e.g., on document edit)\n188:     pub fn invalidate(&mut self) {\n189:         self.valid = false;\n190:     }\n191: \n192:     /// Check if cache needs rebuilding\n193:     pub fn needs_rebuild(&self, doc_revision: u64, width: usize) -> bool {\n194:         !self.valid || self.revision != doc_revision || self.wrap_width != width\n195:     }\n196: \n197:     /// Rebuild the wrap cache for the entire document\n198:     pub fn rebuild(&mut self, document: &Document, wrap_width: usize, tab_width: usize) {\n199:         self.lines.clear();\n200:         self.wrap_width = wrap_width;\n201:         self.revision = document.revision;\n202: \n203:         let mut visual_line = 0;\n204: \n205:         for logical_line in 0..document.line_count() {\n206:             let line_text = document.get_line(logical_line)\n207:                 .unwrap_or_default()\n208:                 .trim_end_matches('\\n')\n209:                 .to_string();\n210: \n211:             let segments = self.compute_line_wraps(&line_text, wrap_width, tab_width, visual_line);\n212:             let segment_count = segments.len();\n213: \n214:             self.lines.insert(logical_line, segments);\n215:             visual_line += segment_count;\n216:         }\n217: \n218:         self.total_visual_lines = visual_line;\n219:         self.valid = true;\n220:     }\n221: \n222:     /// Compute wrap segments for a single line\n223:     fn compute_line_wraps(\n224:         &self,\n225:         line: &str,\n226:         wrap_width: usize,\n227:         tab_width: usize,\n228:         starting_visual_line: usize,\n229:     ) -> Vec<WrapSegment> {\n230:         if line.is_empty() {\n231:             // Empty line still occupies one visual line\n232:             return vec![WrapSegment {\n233:                 start_col: 0,\n234:                 len: 0,\n235:                 visual_line: starting_visual_line,\n236:                 is_continuation: false,\n237:             }];\n238:         }\n239: \n240:         let mut segments = Vec::new();\n241:         let mut current_start = 0;\n242:         let mut current_visual_width = 0;\n243:         let mut last_break_point = None; // (char_index, visual_width at that point)\n244:         let chars: Vec<char> = line.chars().collect();\n245:         let mut visual_line = starting_visual_line;\n246: \n247:         for (i, &ch) in chars.iter().enumerate() {\n248:             // Calculate visual width of this character\n249:             let char_width = if ch == '\\t' {\n250:                 tab_width - (current_visual_width % tab_width)\n251:             } else {\n252:                 1 // TODO: Handle wide Unicode characters\n253:             };\n254: \n255:             // Check if this character would exceed wrap width\n256:             if current_visual_width + char_width > wrap_width && current_start < i {\n257:                 // Need to wrap\n258:                 let break_at = if let Some((break_idx, _)) = last_break_point {\n259:                     // Wrap at last word boundary\n260:                     break_idx + 1 // Include the space in this segment\n261:                 } else {\n262:                     // No word boundary found, force break at current position\n263:                     i\n264:                 };\n265: \n266:                 segments.push(WrapSegment {\n267:                     start_col: current_start,\n268:                     len: break_at - current_start,\n269:                     visual_line,\n270:                     is_continuation: !segments.is_empty(),\n271:                 });\n272: \n273:                 visual_line += 1;\n274:                 current_start = break_at;\n275:                 current_visual_width = 0;\n276:                 last_break_point = None;\n277: \n278:                 // Re-process characters from break point to current\n279:                 for j in break_at..=i {\n280:                     let ch = chars[j];\n281:                     let w = if ch == '\\t' {\n282:                         tab_width - (current_visual_width % tab_width)\n283:                     } else {\n284:                         1\n285:                     };\n286:                     current_visual_width += w;\n287:                     if ch.is_whitespace() {\n288:                         last_break_point = Some((j, current_visual_width));\n289:                     }\n290:                 }\n291:                 continue;\n292:             }\n293: \n294:             current_visual_width += char_width;\n295: \n296:             // Track potential break points (after whitespace)\n297:             if ch.is_whitespace() {\n298:                 last_break_point = Some((i, current_visual_width));\n299:             }\n300:         }\n301: \n302:         // Final segment\n303:         if current_start < chars.len() || segments.is_empty() {\n304:             segments.push(WrapSegment {\n305:                 start_col: current_start,\n306:                 len: chars.len() - current_start,\n307:                 visual_line,\n308:                 is_continuation: !segments.is_empty(),\n309:             });\n310:         }\n311: \n312:         segments\n313:     }\n314: \n315:     /// Convert logical position to visual position\n316:     pub fn logical_to_visual(&self, line: usize, col: usize) -> (usize, usize) {\n317:         if let Some(segments) = self.lines.get(&line) {\n318:             for seg in segments {\n319:                 if col >= seg.start_col && col < seg.start_col + seg.len {\n320:                     return (seg.visual_line, col - seg.start_col);\n321:                 }\n322:                 if col == seg.start_col + seg.len && seg == segments.last().unwrap() {\n323:                     // Cursor at end of line\n324:                     return (seg.visual_line, seg.len);\n325:                 }\n326:             }\n327:             // Past end of line, return last segment's end\n328:             if let Some(last) = segments.last() {\n329:                 return (last.visual_line, last.len);\n330:             }\n331:         }\n332:         (line, col) // Fallback (unwrapped)\n333:     }\n334: \n335:     /// Convert visual position to logical position\n336:     pub fn visual_to_logical(&self, visual_line: usize, visual_col: usize) -> (usize, usize) {\n337:         for (&logical_line, segments) in &self.lines {\n338:             for seg in segments {\n339:                 if seg.visual_line == visual_line {\n340:                     let col = seg.start_col + visual_col.min(seg.len);\n341:                     return (logical_line, col);\n342:                 }\n343:             }\n344:         }\n345:         (visual_line, visual_col) // Fallback\n346:     }\n347: \n348:     /// Get the visual line for a logical line (first segment)\n349:     pub fn logical_line_to_visual(&self, logical_line: usize) -> usize {\n350:         self.lines\n351:             .get(&logical_line)\n352:             .and_then(|segs| segs.first())\n353:             .map(|seg| seg.visual_line)\n354:             .unwrap_or(logical_line)\n355:     }\n356: \n357:     /// Get logical line from visual line\n358:     pub fn visual_line_to_logical(&self, visual_line: usize) -> usize {\n359:         for (&logical_line, segments) in &self.lines {\n360:             for seg in segments {\n361:                 if seg.visual_line == visual_line {\n362:                     return logical_line;\n363:                 }\n364:             }\n365: [Security](#)\n\n[](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/tauri-apps/wry/refs/heads/dev/.github/splash.png)\n\n[](https://crates.io/crates/wry)\n[](https://docs.rs/wry/) [](https://opencollective.com/tauri) [](https://discord.gg/SpmNs4S) [](https://tauri.app) [](https://good-labs.github.io/greater-good-affirmation) [](https://opencollective.com/tauri)\n\nWry is a cross-platform WebView rendering library. The webview requires a running event loop and a window type that implements [ `HasWindowHandle` ],\nor a gtk container widget if you need to support X11 and Wayland. You can use a windowing library like [`tao`](https://docs.rs/tao) or [`winit`](https://docs.rs/winit) . ### Examples\n\n[]()\n\nThis example leverages the [ `HasWindowHandle` ] and supports Windows, macOS, iOS, Android and Linux (X11 Only). See the following example using [`winit`](https://docs.rs/winit) :\n\n```\n# [ derive ( Default ) ] \n struct App { \n  window : Option < Window > , \n  webview : Option < wry :: WebView > , \n } \n\n impl ApplicationHandler for App { \n  fn resumed ( & mut self , event_loop : & ActiveEventLoop ) { \n    let window = event_loop .\ncreate_window ( Window :: default_attributes ( ) ) . unwrap ( ) ; \n    let webview = WebViewBuilder :: new ( ) \n      . with_url ( \"https://tauri.app\" ) \n      . build ( & window ) \n      . unwrap ( ) ; \n\n    self . window = Some ( window ) ; \n    self . webview = Some ( webview ) ; \n  } \n\n  fn window_event ( & mut self , _event_loop : & ActiveEventLoop , _window_id : WindowId , event : WindowEvent ) { } \n } \n\n let event_loop = EventLoop :: new ( ) . unwrap ( ) ; \n let mut app = App :: default ( ) ; \nevent_loop . run_app ( & mut app ) . unwrap ( ) ;\n```\n\nIf you also want to support Wayland too, then we recommend you use [ `WebViewBuilderExtUnix::new_gtk` ] on Linux. See the following example using [`tao`](https://docs.rs/tao) :\n\n```\nlet event_loop = EventLoop :: new ( ) ; \n let window = WindowBuilder :: new ( ) . build ( & event_loop ) . unwrap ( ) ; \n\n let builder = WebViewBuilder :: new ( ) .\nwith_url ( \"https://tauri.app\" ) ; \n\n # [ cfg ( not ( target_os =  \"linux\" ) ) ] \n let webview = builder . build ( & window ) . unwrap ( ) ; \n # [ cfg ( target_os =  \"linux\" ) ] \n let webview = builder . build_gtk ( window . gtk_window ( ) ) . unwrap ( ) ;\n```\n\n### Child webviews\n\n[]()\n\nYou can use [ `WebView::new_as_child` ] or [ `WebViewBuilder::new_as_child` ] to create the webview as a child inside another window. This is supported on\nmacOS, Windows and Linux (X11 Only). ```\n# [ derive ( Default ) ] \n struct App { \n  window : Option < Window > , \n  webview : Option < wry :: WebView > , \n } \n\n impl ApplicationHandler for App { \n  fn resumed ( & mut self , event_loop : & ActiveEventLoop ) { \n    let window = event_loop . create_window ( Window :: default_attributes ( ) ) . unwrap ( ) ; \n    let webview = WebViewBuilder :: new ( ) \n      . with_url ( \"https://tauri.app\" ) \n      . with_bounds ( Rect { \n        position : LogicalPosition :: new ( 100 , 100 ) .\ninto ( ) , \n        size : LogicalSize :: new ( 200 , 200 ) . into ( ) , \n      } ) \n      . build_as_child ( & window ) \n      . unwrap ( ) ; \n\n    self . window = Some ( window ) ; \n    self . webview = Some ( webview ) ; \n  } \n\n  fn window_event ( & mut self , _event_loop : & ActiveEventLoop , _window_id : WindowId , event : WindowEvent ) { } \n } \n\n let event_loop = EventLoop :: new ( ) . unwrap ( ) ; \n let mut app = App :: default ( ) ; \nevent_loop . run_app ( & mut app ) . unwrap ( ) ;\n```\n\nIf you want to support X11 and Wayland at the same time, we recommend using\n[ `WebViewExtUnix::new_gtk` ] or [ `WebViewBuilderExtUnix::new_gtk` ] with [ `gtk::Fixed` ]. ```\nlet event_loop = EventLoop :: new ( ) ; \n let window = WindowBuilder :: new ( ) . build ( & event_loop ) . unwrap ( ) ; \n\n let builder = WebViewBuilder :: new ( ) \n  . with_url ( \"https://tauri.app\" ) \n  . with_bounds ( Rect { \n    position : LogicalPosition :: new ( 100 , 100 ) .\ninto ( ) , \n    size : LogicalSize :: new ( 200 , 200 ) . into ( ) , \n  } ) ; \n\n # [ cfg ( not ( target_os =  \"linux\" ) ) ] \n let webview = builder . build_as_child ( & window ) . unwrap ( ) ; \n # [ cfg ( target_os =  \"linux\" ) ] \n let webview = { \n  # use gtk :: prelude :: * ; \n  let vbox = window . default_vbox ( ) . unwrap ( ) ; // tao adds a gtk::Box by default \n  let fixed = gtk :: Fixed :: new ( ) ; \n  fixed . show_all ( ) ; \n  vbox . pack_start ( & fixed , true , true , 0 ) ; \n  builder . build_gtk ( & fixed ) . unwrap ( ) \n } ;\n```\n\n### Platform Considerations\n\n[]()\n\nHere is the underlying web engine each platform uses, and some dependencies you might need to install.\n#### Linux\n\n[]()\n\n[WebKitGTK](https://webkitgtk.org/) is used to provide webviews on Linux which requires GTK,\nso if the windowing library doesn't support GTK (as in [`winit`](https://docs.rs/winit) )\nyou'll need to call [ `gtk::init` ] before creating the webview and then call [ `gtk::main_iteration_do` ] alongside\nyour windowing library event loop. ```\n# [ derive ( Default ) ] \n struct App { \n  webview_window : Option < ( Window , WebView ) > , \n } \n\n impl ApplicationHandler for App { \n  fn resumed ( & mut self , event_loop : & ActiveEventLoop ) { \n    let window = event_loop . create_window ( Window :: default_attributes ( ) ) . unwrap ( ) ; \n    let webview = WebViewBuilder :: new ( ) \n      . with_url ( \"https://tauri.app\" ) \n      . build ( & window ) \n      . unwrap ( ) ; \n\n    self .\n ... \n+ `WRY_ANDROID_PACKAGE` : which is the reversed domain name of your android project and the app name in snake\\_case, for example, `com.wry.example.wry_app`\n    + `WRY_ANDROID_LIBRARY` : for example, if your cargo project has a lib name `wry_app` , it will generate `libwry_app.so` so you set this env var to `wry_app`\n    + `WRY_ANDROID_KOTLIN_FILES_OUT_DIR` : for example, `path/to/app/src/main/kotlin/com/wry/example`\n2. Your main Android Activity needs to inherit `AppCompatActivity` , preferably it should use the generated `WryActivity` or inherit it. 3. Your Rust app needs to call `wry::android_setup` function to setup the necessary logic to be able to create webviews later on. 4. Your Rust app needs to call `wry::android_binding!` macro to setup the JNI functions that will be called by `WryActivity` and various other places. It is recommended to use the [`tao`](https://docs.rs/tao/latest/tao/) crate as it provides maximum compatibility with `wry` .\n```\n# [ cfg ( target_os =  \"android\" ) ] \n { \n  tao :: android_binding! ( \n      com_example , \n      wry_app , \n      WryActivity , \n      wry :: android_setup , // pass the wry::android_setup function to tao which will be invoked when the event loop is created \n      _start_app\n  ) ; \n  wry :: android_binding! ( com_example , ttt ) ; \n }\n```\n\nIf this feels overwhelming, you can just use the preconfigured template from [`cargo-mobile2`](https://github.com/tauri-apps/cargo-mobile2) . For more information, check out [MOBILE.md](https://github.com/tauri-apps/wry/blob/dev/MOBILE.md) . ### Feature flags\n\n[]()\n\nWry uses a set of feature flags to toggle several advanced features. * `os-webview` (default): Enables the default WebView framework on the platform. This must be enabled\n  for the crate to work. This feature was added in preparation of other ports like cef and servo.\n ... \n* `tracing` : enables [`tracing`](https://docs.rs/tracing) for `evaluate_script` , `ipc_handler` , and `custom_protocols` . ### Partners\n\n[]()\n\n|[](https://crabnebula.dev) |\n| --- |\n\nFor the complete list of sponsors please visit our [website](https://tauri.app) and [Open Collective](https://opencollective.com/tauri) . ### License\n\n[]()\n\nApache-2.0/MIT\n\n## About\n\nCross-platform WebView library in Rust for Tauri. ### Resources\n\n[Readme]()\n\n### License\n\n### Code of conduct\n\n[Code of conduct]()\n\n### Security policy\n\n[Security policy]()\n\n### Uh oh!       "# Servo aims to empower developers with a lightweight, high-performance alternative for embedding web technologies in applications.\n\n[Get Involved](/contributing/) [Donate](https://opencollective.com/servo)\n\nServo is a web browser rendering engine written in Rust, with WebGL and WebGPU support, and adaptable to desktop, mobile, and embedded applications.\n\nEmbeddable\n\nServo provides a **WebView API** so other applications can use it to embed web content.\n\nMemory-safe\n\nServo takes advantage of the memory safety features of the **Rust programming language** , resulting in fewer vulnerabilities related to memory and concurrency.\n\nModular\n\nBuilt with a modular architecture and powered by widely-used Rust crates, Servo makes it **easier to customize and adapt** a high-performance browser engine to your needs.\n\nParallel\n\nServo uses concurrency and parallelism for **faster and more energy-efficient rendering of web content** on multi-core devices.\n\nCross platform\n\nServo has **multi-platform support** , including Windows, macOS, Linux, Android, and OpenHarmony. In addition, Servo can be ported and adapted to **embedded devices** .\n\nIndependent\n\nServo is a project managed with **open governance under Linux Foundation Europe** through our Technical Steering Committee.\n\n## Latest on the blog\n\n2025-12-15\n\n[November in Servo: monthly releases, context menus, parallel CSS parsing, and more!](/blog/2025/12/15/november-in-servo/)\n\nAll of these updates are in Servo 0.0.3.\n\n2025-11-21\n\n[Servo Sponsorship Tiers](/blog/2025/11/21/sponsorship-tiers/)\n\nThe Servo project has defined sponsorship tiers for organizations and individuals donating money to the project.\n\n2025-11-14\n\n[October in Servo: better for the web, better for embedders, better for you](/blog/2025/11/14/october-in-servo/)\n\nBig changes to our webview API, improved macOS and Android builds, and now we’ve shipped AbortController, AbortSignal, and XPath.\n\n2025-10-24\n\n[This month in Servo: experimental mode, Trusted Types, strokeText(), and more!](/blog/2025/10/24/this-month-in-servo/)\n\nClick the experimental mode button (☢) to enable bleeding-edge web platform features.\n\n[View older posts](/blog/)\n\n## Acknowledgements\n\nThe Servo project is dependent on outside funding. [References](#references)\n\n---\n\n## Overview\n\n### Current State\n\nThe editor has:\n- Syntax highlighting for Markdown (tree-sitter-md)\n- Split view infrastructure (`EditorArea`, `LayoutNode`, `SplitContainer`)\n- winit-based event loop\n\nNo rendered preview exists—users see only raw Markdown with syntax highlighting.\n\n### Goals\n\n1. **Live preview** - Render Markdown as formatted HTML in a preview pane\n2. **Synchronized scrolling** - Scroll position linked between source and preview\n3. **Split integration** - Preview appears as a pane alongside source editor\n4. **Theme-aware** - Preview respects editor theme colors\n5. **Live updates** - Preview updates as user types (debounced)\n\n### Non-Goals\n\n- Image upload/embedding\n- LaTeX/math rendering (future)\n- Mermaid diagram rendering (future)\n- Export to PDF\n- WYSIWYG editing in preview\n\n---\n\n## Approach Comparison\n\n| Aspect | Webview (wry) | Native Rendering |\n|--------|---------------|------------------|\n| **Effort** | M (3-5 days) | L (1-2 weeks) |\n| **Rendering quality** | Excellent (full CSS) | Basic (styled text) |\n| **Scroll sync** | Easy (JS callbacks) | Complex (line mapping) |\n| **Dependencies** | +wry (~WebKit/Edge) | +pulldown-cmark only |\n| **Binary size** | Minimal (uses OS webview) | No change |\n| **Maintenance** | Low (leverage HTML/CSS) | High (custom renderer) |\n| **Platform support** | macOS/Windows/Linux | Same |\n\n### Recommendation: Webview\n\nThe webview approach is **simpler and more maintainable**:\n- Markdown → HTML is a solved problem (`pulldown-cmark`)\n- CSS handles all styling (headers, code blocks, lists)\n- JS handles scroll sync with 10 lines of code\n- No need to build a custom formatted text renderer\n\n---\n\n## Recommended: Webview Approach\n\n### Architecture\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                        Markdown Preview with Webview                         │\n│                                                                              │\n│  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐    │\n│  │                         EditorArea Layout                            │    │\n│  │  ┌────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────┐               │    │\n│  │  │    Source Editor       │    Webview Preview      │               │    │\n│  │  │   (existing)           │   (wry WebView)         │               │    │\n│  │  │                        │                         │               │    │\n│  │  │  # Heading             │  <h1>Heading</h1>       │               │    │\n│  │  │  Some **bold** text    │  Some <b>bold</b> text  │               │    │\n│  │  │                        │                         │               │    │\n│  │  └────────────────────────┴─────────────────────────┘               │    │\n│  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘    │\n│                                                                              │\n│  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐    │\n│  │                           Data Flow                                  │    │\n│  │                                                                      │    │\n│  │   Document ──► pulldown-cmark ──► HTML String ──► WebView.eval()    │    │\n│  │                                                                      │    │\n│  │   Scroll Sync:                                                       │    │\n│  │   Source scroll ──► JS: window.scrollTo(lineY) ──► Preview scrolls  │    │\n│  │   Preview scroll ──► IPC callback ──► Source scrolls                │    │\n│  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘    │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### Dependencies\n\n```toml\n# Cargo.toml additions\n[dependencies]\nwry = \"0.50\"           # Cross-platform webview (uses OS webview)\npulldown-cmark = \"0.12\" # Markdown → HTML\n```\n\n**Note:** `wry` uses the OS-provided webview (WebKit on macOS/Linux, WebView2 on Windows), so binary size increase is minimal.\n\n### Module Structure\n\n```\nsrc/\n├── markdown/                    # NEW MODULE\n│   ├── mod.rs                   # Public exports\n│   ├── renderer.rs              # Markdown → HTML via pulldown-cmark\n│   └── preview.rs               # PreviewPane with wry WebView\n├── model/\n│   └── editor_area.rs           # + LayoutNode::Preview variant\n├── update/\n│   └── preview.rs               # NEW: Preview message handler\n└── messages.rs                  # + PreviewMsg enum\n```\n\n### Core Implementation\n\n#### Markdown to HTML\n\n```rust\n// src/markdown/renderer.rs\n\nuse pulldown_cmark::{html, Options, Parser};\n\n/// Convert markdown to HTML with source line markers\npub fn markdown_to_html(markdown: &str, theme: &PreviewTheme) -> String {\n    let options = Options::ENABLE_TABLES\n        | Options::ENABLE_FOOTNOTES\n        | Options::ENABLE_STRIKETHROUGH\n        | Options::ENABLE_TASKLISTS;\n\n    let parser = Parser::new_ext(markdown, options);\n\n    // Wrap with line markers for scroll sync\n    let mut html_output = String::new();\n    html::push_html(&mut html_output, parser);\n\n    // Wrap in styled HTML document\n    format!(\n        r#\"<!DOCTYPE html>\n<html>\n<head>\n    <meta charset=\"utf-8\">\n    <style>{}</style>\n</head>\n<body>\n    <div id=\"content\">{}</div>\n    <script>{}</script>\n</body>\n</html>\"#,\n        generate_css(theme),\n        add_line_markers(&html_output, markdown),\n        SCROLL_SYNC_JS\n    )\n}\n\nfn generate_css(theme: &PreviewTheme) -> String {\n    format!(r#\"\n        body {{\n            font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, \"Segoe UI\", Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif;\n            font-size: 14px;\n            line-height: 1.6;\n            color: {};\n            background: {};\n            padding: 20px;\n            max-width: 800px;\n            margin: 0 auto;\n        }}\n        h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6 {{\n            color: {};\n            border-bottom: 1px solid {};\n            padding-bottom: 0.3em;\n        }}\n        code {{\n            background: {};\n            padding: 0.2em 0.4em;\n            border-radius: 3px;\n            font-family: \"SF Mono\", Consolas, monospace;\n        }}\n        pre {{\n            background: {};\n            padding: 16px;\n            border-radius: 6px;\n            overflow-x: auto;\n        }}\n        pre code {{\n            background: none;\n            padding: 0;\n        }}\n        blockquote {{\n            border-left: 4px solid {};\n            margin: 0;\n            padding-left: 16px;\n            color: {};\n        }}\n        a {{\n            color: {};\n        }}\n        [data-line] {{\n            scroll-margin-top: 20px;\n        }}\n    \"#,\n        theme.text,\n        theme.background,\n        theme.heading,\n        theme.border,\n        theme.code_background,\n        theme.code_background,\n        theme.accent,\n        theme.muted,\n        theme.link,\n    )\n}\n\n/// Add data-line attributes to elements for scroll sync\nfn add_line_markers(html: &str, markdown: &str) -> String {\n    // Simple approach: wrap each block-level element with line number\n    // More sophisticated: use pulldown-cmark's offset tracking\n    html.to_string() // TODO: Add line markers\n}\n\nconst SCROLL_SYNC_JS: &str = r#\"\n    // Receive scroll position from editor\n    window.scrollToLine = function(line) {\n        const el = document.querySelector(`[data-line=\"${line}\"]`);\n        if (el) {\n            el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });\n        }\n    };\n\n    // Report scroll position back to editor\n    let lastReportedLine = -1;\n    window.addEventListener('scroll', () => {\n        const elements = document.querySelectorAll('[data-line]');\n        for (const el of elements) {\n            const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();\n            if (rect.top >= 0) {\n                const line = parseInt(el.dataset.line, 10);\n                if (line !== lastReportedLine) {\n                    lastReportedLine = line;\n                    window.ipc.postMessage(JSON.stringify({ type: 'scroll', line }));\n                }\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n    });\n\"#;\n```\n\n#### Preview Pane with Webview\n\n```rust\n// src/markdown/preview.rs\n\nuse wry::{WebView, WebViewBuilder, Rect};\nuse crate::model::editor_area::DocumentId;\n\n/// A markdown preview pane using an embedded webview\npub struct PreviewPane {\n    /// The webview instance\n    webview: WebView,\n\n    /// Document being previewed\n    pub document_id: DocumentId,\n\n    /// Whether scroll sync is enabled\n    pub sync_scroll: bool,\n\n    /// Last rendered revision (for change detection)\n    last_revision: u64,\n}\n\nimpl PreviewPane {\n    /// Create a new preview pane as a child of the window\n    pub fn new(\n        window: &impl raw_window_handle::HasWindowHandle,\n        document_id: DocumentId,\n        bounds: Rect,\n        ipc_handler: impl Fn(String) + 'static,\n    ) -> Result<Self, wry::Error> {\n        let webview = WebViewBuilder::new()\n            .with_bounds(bounds)\n            .with_ipc_handler(move |msg| {\n                ipc_handler(msg.body().to_string());\n            })\n            .with_devtools(cfg!(debug_assertions))\n            .build_as_child(window)?;\n\n        Ok(Self {\n            webview,\n            document_id,\n            sync_scroll: true,\n            last_revision: 0,\n        })\n    }\n\n    /// Update preview content\n    pub fn update_content(&mut self, html: &str, revision: u64) {\n        if revision != self.last_revision {\n            // Use data URL to load content (avoids CORS issues)\n            let encoded = base64::encode(html);\n            let data_url = format!(\"data:text/html;base64,{}\", encoded);\n            let _ = self.webview.load_url(&data_url);\n            self.last_revision = revision;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Scroll preview to match source line\n    pub fn scroll_to_line(&self, line: usize) {\n        if self.sync_scroll {\n            let js = format!(\"window.scrollToLine({})\", line);\n            let _ = self.webview.evaluate_script(&js);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Update bounds when layout changes\n    pub fn set_bounds(&mut self, bounds: Rect) {\n        let _ = self.webview.set_bounds(bounds);\n    }\n}\n```\n\n#### Preview Theme\n\n```rust\n// src/markdown/mod.rs\n\n/// Theme colors for markdown preview\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct PreviewTheme {\n    pub background: String,\n    pub text: String,\n    pub heading: String,\n    pub link: String,\n    pub code_background: String,\n    pub border: String,\n    pub accent: String,\n    pub muted: String,\n}\n\nimpl PreviewTheme {\n    /// Generate from editor theme\n    pub fn from_editor_theme(theme: &crate::theme::Theme) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            background: theme.editor.background.to_css(),\n            text: theme.editor.text.to_css(),\n            heading: theme.syntax.keyword.to_css(),\n            link: theme.syntax.string.to_css(),\n            code_background: theme.editor.gutter_bg.to_css(),\n            border: theme.editor.line_number.to_css(),\n            accent: theme.syntax.function.to_css(),\n            muted: theme.editor.line_number.to_css(),\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### Messages\n\n```rust\n// src/messages.rs additions\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub enum PreviewMsg {\n    /// Toggle preview for current document\n    Toggle,\n\n    /// Open preview to the side\n    Open,\n\n    /// Close preview\n    Close,\n\n    /// Update preview content (after document edit)\n    Refresh,\n\n    /// Scroll preview to line (from source scroll)\n    ScrollToLine(usize),\n\n    /// Scroll source to line (from preview scroll via IPC)\n    SyncFromPreview(usize),\n\n    /// Toggle scroll synchronization\n    ToggleSync,\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Alternative: Native Rendering\n\nIf adding a webview dependency is undesirable, a simpler native approach:\n\n### Styled Text Rendering\n\n```rust\n// Render markdown as styled lines using existing TextPainter\n\npub struct MarkdownLine {\n    pub segments: Vec<StyledSegment>,\n    pub source_line: usize,\n}\n\npub struct StyledSegment {\n    pub text: String,\n    pub style: MarkdownStyle,\n}\n\npub enum MarkdownStyle {\n    Normal,\n    Heading { level: u8 },\n    Bold,\n    Italic,\n    Code,\n    Link,\n    ListItem { indent: usize },\n    Blockquote,\n}\n```\n\nThis approach uses the existing `TextPainter` but with additional style handling. However, it requires:\n- Custom font scaling for headings\n- Custom rendering for code blocks\n- Manual line wrapping\n- More complex scroll sync mapping\n\n**Recommendation:** Start with webview; consider native if webview proves problematic.\n\n---\n\n## Implementation Plan\n\n### Phase 1: Core Infrastructure\n\n**Effort:** S (1-2 days)\n\n- [ ] Add `wry` and `pulldown-cmark` dependencies\n- [ ] Create `src/markdown/mod.rs` module structure\n- [ ] Implement `markdown_to_html()` with basic styling\n- [ ] Create `PreviewTheme` from editor theme\n\n**Test:** Call `markdown_to_html()` and verify output.\n\n### Phase 2: Webview Integration\n\n**Effort:** M (2-3 days)\n\n- [ ] Implement `PreviewPane` with wry WebView\n- [ ] Add `LayoutNode::Preview` variant to layout system\n- [ ] Create `update/preview.rs` message handler\n- [ ] Wire `PreviewMsg::Toggle` to Cmd+Shift+V\n- [ ] Render preview pane in correct layout position\n\n**Test:** Cmd+Shift+V opens preview pane with rendered markdown.\n\n### Phase 3: Content Updates\n\n**Effort:** S (1-2 days)\n\n- [ ] Trigger preview refresh on document changes (debounced 150ms)\n- [ ] Track document revision to avoid redundant updates\n- [ ] Handle theme changes (re-render with new CSS)\n\n**Test:** Type in source, preview updates after short delay.\n\n### Phase 4: Scroll Synchronization\n\n**Effort:** M (2-3 days)\n\n- [ ] Add `data-line` attributes to HTML elements\n- [ ] Implement `scrollToLine()` JS function\n- [ ] Hook source scroll to call `preview.scroll_to_line()`\n- [ ] Implement IPC handler for preview → source sync\n- [ ] Add toggle for scroll sync\n\n**Test:** Scroll in source, preview follows. Scroll in preview, source follows.\n\n### Phase 5: Polish\n\n**Effort:** S (1 day)\n\n- [ ] Add loading indicator during render\n- [ ] Handle edge cases (empty file, very large file)\n- [ ] Close preview when switching to non-markdown file\n- [ ] Add to command palette\n\n**Test:** Full workflow feels responsive and polished.\n\n---\n\n## Keybindings\n\n| Action | Mac | Windows/Linux | Command |\n|--------|-----|---------------|---------|\n| Toggle preview | `Cmd+Shift+V` | `Ctrl+Shift+V` | `MarkdownTogglePreview` |\n| Open to side | `Cmd+K V` | `Ctrl+K V` | `MarkdownOpenPreviewToSide` |\n\n```yaml\n# keymap.yaml additions\n- key: \"cmd+shift+v\"\n  command: MarkdownTogglePreview\n  when: [\"language:markdown\"]\n\n- key: \"cmd+k v\"\n  command: MarkdownOpenPreviewToSide\n  when: [\"language:markdown\"]\n```\n\n---\n\n## Testing Strategy\n\n### Unit Tests\n\n```rust\n#[test]\nfn test_markdown_to_html_basic() {\n    let md = \"# Hello\\n\\nWorld\";\n    let html = markdown_to_html(md, &PreviewTheme::default());\n\n    assert!(html.contains(\"<h1>Hello</h1>\"));\n    assert!(html.contains(\"<p>World</p>\"));\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn test_markdown_to_html_code_block() {\n    let md = \"```rust\\nfn main() {}\\n```\";\n    let html = markdown_to_html(md, &PreviewTheme::default());\n\n    assert!(html.contains(\"<pre>\"));\n    assert!(html.contains(\"<code\"));\n    assert!(html.contains(\"fn main()\"));\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn test_preview_theme_from_editor_theme() {\n    let editor_theme = Theme::default();\n    let preview_theme =
