changes\n2. **Fold/unfold operations** - Toggle individual regions\n3. **Gutter indicators** - Show fold controls in gutter (▶/▼)\n4. **Viewport adjustment** - Hidden lines don't render\n5. **Cursor handling** - Cursor in folded region expands it\n\n### Non-Goals (This Phase → See folding-advanced.md)\n\n- Syntax-based detection (functions, classes)\n- Region markers (`#region`/`#endregion`)\n- Fold persistence across sessions\n- Fold level commands (fold to level N)\n- Manual/custom folds\n\n### Why Indentation First?\n\n1. **Works for all languages** - No language-specific logic needed\n2. **Simple algorithm** - Just track indent level changes\n3. **Low risk** - Doesn't depend on syntax parsing correctness\n4. **Quick win** - Delivers 80% of folding value with 20% of complexity\n\n---\n\n## Architecture\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                       Indentation-Based Folding                              │\n│                                                                              │\n│  Document:                                                                   │\n│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│  │  0: fn main() {           ◄─ indent=0, starts fold (ends line 5)    │   │\n│  │  1:     let x = 1;        ◄─ indent=4                               │   │\n│  │  2:     if true {         ◄─ indent=4, starts fold (ends line 4)    │   │\n│  │  3:         do_thing();   ◄─ indent=8                               │   │\n│  │  4:     }                 ◄─ indent=4, ends fold                    │   │\n│  │  5: }                     ◄─ indent=0, ends fold                    │   │\n│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n│                                                                              │\n│  FoldingState.regions:                                                       │\n│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│  │  [FoldRegion { start: 0, end: 5, level: 0, collapsed: false }]      │   │\n│  │  [FoldRegion { start: 2, end: 4, level: 1, collapsed: false }]      │   │\n│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n│                                                                              │\n│  Rendering (if line 0-5 collapsed):                                          │\n│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│  │  ▶ 1 │ fn main() { ⋯ 5 lines                                        │   │\n│  │    7 │ // next code                                                  │   │\n│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n│                                                                              │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### Module Structure\n\n```\nsrc/\n├── folding/                     # NEW MODULE\n│   ├── mod.rs                   # Public exports\n│   ├── region.rs                # FoldRegion, FoldingState\n│   ├── indent.rs                # Indentation-based detection\n│   └── gutter.rs                # Fold indicator rendering\n├── model/\n│   └── editor.rs                # + folding_state: FoldingState\n├── update/\n│   └── folding.rs               # NEW: Fold message handler\n└── view/\n    └── mod.rs                   # + render fold indicators, skip hidden lines\n```\n\n---\n\n## Data Structures\n\n### FoldRegion\n\n```rust\n// src/folding/region.rs\n\n/// A single foldable region\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct FoldRegion {\n    /// Starting line (inclusive, the line with fold indicator)\n    pub start_line: usize,\n\n    /// Ending line (inclusive)\n    pub end_line: usize,\n\n    /// Nesting level (0 = outermost)\n    pub level: usize,\n\n    /// Current state\n    pub collapsed: bool,\n}\n\nimpl FoldRegion {\n    /// Number of lines hidden when collapsed\n    pub fn hidden_line_count(&self) -> usize {\n        if self.collapsed {\n            self.end_line - self.start_line\n        } else {\n            0\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Check if a line is inside this region (not the start line)\n    pub fn contains_line(&self, line: usize) -> bool {\n        line > self.start_line && line <= self.end_line\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### FoldingState\n\n```rust\n// src/folding/region.rs\n\n/// Folding state for a document\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]\npub struct FoldingState {\n    /// All fold regions (sorted by start_line)\n    pub regions: Vec<FoldRegion>,\n\n    /// Document revision when computed\n    pub revision: u64,\n\n    /// Whether state needs recomputation\n    pub dirty: bool,\n}\n\nimpl FoldingState {\n    /// Check if a line is hidden (inside a collapsed fold)\n    pub fn is_hidden(&self, line: usize) -> bool {\n        self.regions.iter().any(|r| r.collapsed && r.contains_line(line))\n    }\n\n    /// Get fold region starting at a line (if any)\n    pub fn region_at(&self, line: usize) -> Option<&FoldRegion> {\n        self.regions.iter().find(|r| r.start_line == line)\n    }\n\n    /// Get mutable fold region starting at a line\n    pub fn region_at_mut(&mut self, line: usize) -> Option<&mut FoldRegion> {\n        self.regions.iter_mut().find(|r| r.start_line == line)\n    }\n\n    /// Toggle fold at line (if a fold starts there)\n    pub fn toggle(&mut self, line: usize) {\n        if let Some(region) = self.region_at_mut(line) {\n            region.collapsed = !region.collapsed;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Expand any fold containing this line\n    pub fn expand_containing(&mut self, line: usize) {\n        for region in &mut self.regions {\n            if region.collapsed && region.contains_line(line) {\n                region.collapsed = false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Collapse all folds\n    pub fn collapse_all(&mut self) {\n        for region in &mut self.regions {\n            region.collapsed = true;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Expand all folds\n    pub fn expand_all(&mut self) {\n        for region in &mut self.regions {\n            region.collapsed = false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Map document line to visual line (accounting for collapsed folds)\n    pub fn doc_to_visual(&self, doc_line: usize) -> usize {\n        let mut visual = 0;\n        for line in 0..doc_line {\n            if !self.is_hidden(line) {\n                visual += 1;\n            }\n        }\n        visual\n    }\n\n    /// Map visual line to document line\n    pub fn visual_to_doc(&self, visual_line: usize) -> usize {\n        let mut visual = 0;\n        let mut doc = 0;\n\n        while visual < visual_line {\n            if !self.is_hidden(doc) {\n                visual += 1;\n            }\n            doc += 1;\n        }\n\n        // Skip any hidden lines at target\n        while self.is_hidden(doc) {\n            doc += 1;\n        }\n\n        doc\n    }\n\n    /// Total visible line count\n    pub fn visible_line_count(&self, total_lines: usize) -> usize {\n        (0..total_lines).filter(|&l| !self.is_hidden(l)).count()\n    }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Fold Detection\n\n### Indentation-Based Algorithm\n\n```rust\n// src/folding/indent.rs\n\nuse crate::model::Document;\nuse super::region::FoldRegion;\n\n/// Minimum indent change to create a fold (usually 1 level = tab_width spaces)\nconst MIN_FOLD_LINES: usize = 1;\n\n/// Detect fold regions based on indentation\npub fn detect_indent_folds(document: &Document, tab_width: usize) -> Vec<FoldRegion> {\n    let mut regions = Vec::new();\n    let mut stack: Vec<(usize, usize)> = vec![]; // (start_line, indent_level)\n    let line_count = document.line_count();\n\n    for line_idx in 0..line_count {\n        let line_text = document.get_line(line_idx).unwrap_or_default();\n        let trimmed = line_text.trim_end();\n\n        // Skip empty lines for indent calculation\n        if trimmed.is_empty() {\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        let indent = measure_indent(trimmed, tab_width);\n\n        // Close any regions with higher indent\n        while let Some(&(start, level)) = stack.last() {\n            if indent <= level {\n                // Region ends at previous non-empty line\n                let end_line = find_last_content_line(document, start, line_idx - 1);\n                if end_line > start + MIN_FOLD_LINES {\n                    regions.push(FoldRegion {\n                        start_line: start,\n                        end_line,\n                        level: stack.len() - 1,\n                        collapsed: false,\n                    });\n                }\n                stack.pop();\n            } else {\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Check if this line starts a new fold (ends with fold-starting char)\n        if should_start_fold(trimmed) {\n            stack.push((line_idx, indent));\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Close remaining open regions\n    for (start, _) in stack.into_iter().rev() {\n        let end_line = find_last_content_line(document, start, line_count - 1);\n        if end_line > start + MIN_FOLD_LINES {\n            regions.push(FoldRegion {\n                start_line: start,\n                end_line,\n                level: 0,\n                collapsed: false,\n            });\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Sort by start line for efficient lookup\n    regions.sort_by_key(|r| r.start_line);\n    regions\n}\n\n/// Measure indentation level (handles tabs and spaces)\nfn measure_indent(line: &str, tab_width: usize) -> usize {\n    let mut indent = 0;\n    for ch in line.chars() {\n        match ch {\n            ' ' => indent += 1,\n            '\\t' => indent += tab_width - (indent % tab_width),\n            _ => break,\n        }\n    }\n    indent\n}\n\n/// Check if line should start a fold region\nfn should_start_fold(line: &str) -> bool {\n    // Lines ending with these characters typically start blocks\n    let fold_starters = ['{', ':', '[', '('];\n\n    line.chars()\n        .rev()\n        .find(|c| !c.is_whitespace())\n        .map(|c| fold_starters.contains(&c))\n        .unwrap_or(false)\n}\n\n/// Find last line with content between start and end\nfn find_last_content_line(document: &Document, start: usize, end: usize) -> usize {\n    for line in (start..=end).rev() {\n        if let Some(text) = document.get_line(line) {\n            if !text.trim().is_empty() {\n                return line;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    end\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Rendering\n\n### Fold Indicators in Gutter\n\n```rust\n// src/folding/gutter.rs\n\nuse crate::view::Frame;\nuse super::region::FoldingState;\n\n/// Render fold indicators in the gutter\npub fn render_fold_indicators(\n    frame: &mut Frame,\n    folding: &FoldingState,\n    visible_start: usize,\n    visible_count: usize,\n    gutter_x: usize,\n    gutter_width: usize,\n    line_height: usize,\n    y_offset: usize,\n    theme: &FoldIndicatorTheme,\n    painter: &mut TextPainter,\n) {\n    let indicator_x = gutter_x + gutter_width - 12; // Right side of gutter\n\n    for visual_line in 0..visible_count {\n        let doc_line = folding.visual_to_doc(visible_start + visual_line);\n        let y = y_offset + visual_line * line_height;\n\n        if let Some(region) = folding.region_at(doc_line) {\n            let icon = if region.collapsed { \"▶\" } else { \"▼\" };\n            painter.draw_text(\n                frame,\n                indicator_x,\n                y,\n                icon,\n                theme.indicator_color,\n                None,\n            );\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Render collapsed fold placeholder\npub fn render_fold_placeholder(\n    frame: &mut Frame,\n    region: &FoldRegion,\n    x: usize,\n    y: usize,\n    theme: &FoldIndicatorTheme,\n    painter: &mut TextPainter,\n) {\n    let hidden_count = region.end_line - region.start_line;\n    let placeholder = format!(\" ⋯ {} lines\", hidden_count);\n\n    painter.draw_text(\n        frame,\n        x,\n        y,\n        &placeholder,\n        theme.placeholder_color,\n        Some(theme.placeholder_bg),\n    );\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct FoldIndicatorTheme {\n    pub indicator_color: u32,\n    pub placeholder_color: u32,\n    pub placeholder_bg: u32,\n}\n```\n\n### Skip Hidden Lines in Render\n\n```rust\n// In view/mod.rs - modify render_document()\n\nfn render_document(/* ... */) {\n    // ...existing setup...\n\n    let folding = &editor_state.folding_state;\n    let mut visual_line = 0;\n\n    for doc_line in viewport.top_line..viewport.bottom_line() {\n        // Skip hidden lines\n        if folding.is_hidden(doc_line) {\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        let y = y_offset + visual_line * line_height;\n\n        // Render line content\n        render_line(frame, document, doc_line, x, y, /* ... */);\n\n        // If this line starts a collapsed fold, add placeholder\n        if let Some(region) = folding.region_at(doc_line) {\n            if region.collapsed {\n                let line_end_x = /* calculate end of line text */;\n                render_fold_placeholder(frame, region, line_end_x, y, theme, painter);\n            }\n        }\n\n        visual_line += 1;\n    }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Keybindings\n\n| Action | Mac | Windows/Linux | Command |\n|--------|-----|---------------|---------|\n| Toggle fold at cursor | `Cmd+Option+[` | `Ctrl+Shift+[` | `ToggleFold` |\n| Fold at cursor | `Cmd+Option+[` | `Ctrl+Shift+[` | `Fold` |\n| Unfold at cursor | `Cmd+Option+]` | `Ctrl+Shift+]` | `Unfold` |\n| Fold all | `Cmd+K Cmd+0` | `Ctrl+K Ctrl+0` | `FoldAll` |\n| Unfold all | `Cmd+K Cmd+J` | `Ctrl+K Ctrl+J` | `UnfoldAll` |\n\n```yaml\n# keymap.yaml additions\n- key: \"cmd+option+[\"\n  command: ToggleFold\n\n- key: \"cmd+option+]\"\n  command: Unfold\n\n- key: \"cmd+k cmd+0\"\n  command: FoldAll\n\n- key: \"cmd+k cmd+j\"\n  command: UnfoldAll\n```\n\n---\n\n## Messages\n\n```rust\n// src/messages.rs additions\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub enum FoldMsg {\n    /// Toggle fold at current cursor line\n    Toggle,\n\n    /// Fold region at cursor (if any)\n    Fold,\n\n    /// Unfold region at cursor (if any)\n    Unfold,\n\n    /// Fold all regions\n    FoldAll,\n\n    /// Unfold all regions\n    UnfoldAll,\n\n    /// Recompute fold regions (after document change)\n    Recompute,\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Implementation Plan\n\n### Phase 1: Core Data Structures\n\n**Effort:** S (1 day)\n\n- [ ] Create `src/folding/mod.rs` module structure\n- [ ] Implement `FoldRegion` and `FoldingState`\n- [ ] Add `folding_state: FoldingState` to `EditorState`\n- [ ] Add `FoldIndicatorTheme` to theme system\n\n**Test:** Create `FoldingState`, add regions, verify `is_hidden()`.\n\n### Phase 2: Fold Detection\n\n**Effort:** S (1-2 days)\n\n- [ ] Implement `measure_indent()` function\n- [ ] Implement `detect_indent_folds()` algorithm\n- [ ] Trigger detection on document load\n- [ ] Trigger re-detection on document change (debounced)\n\n**Test:** Parse Python/YAML file, verify correct regions detected.\n\n### Phase 3: Toggle Operations\n\n**Effort:** S (1 day)\n\n- [ ] Add `FoldMsg` to messages.rs\n- [ ] Implement `update_fold()` handler\n- [ ] Wire up `toggle()`, `fold()`, `unfold()`\n- [ ] Wire up `fold_all()`, `unfold_all()`\n- [ ] Add keybindings\n\n**Test:** Toggle fold via keyboard, verify state changes.\n\n### Phase 4: Rendering\n\n**Effort:** M (2-3 days)\n\n- [ ] Implement `render_fold_indicators()` in gutter\n- [ ] Modify `render_document()` to skip hidden lines\n- [ ] Implement `render_fold_placeholder()`\n- [ ] Update viewport calculations for visual lines\n- [ ] Handle scrolling with collapsed folds\n\n**Test:** Collapse fold, verify hidden lines don't render, placeholder shown.\n\n### Phase 5: Cursor Integration\n\n**Effort:** S (1 day)\n\n- [ ] When cursor moves into hidden line, expand containing fold\n- [ ] When clicking gutter indicator, toggle fold\n- [ ] Update cursor position after fold/unfold\n\n**Test:** Arrow down into collapsed region, verify it expands.\n\n---\n\n## Testing Strategy\n\n### Unit Tests\n\n```rust\n#[test]\nfn test_measure_indent() {\n    assert_eq!(measure_indent(\"hello\", 4), 0);\n    assert_eq!(measure_indent(\"    hello\", 4), 4);\n    assert_eq!(measure_indent(\"\\thello\", 4), 4);\n    assert_eq!(measure_indent(\"  \\thello\", 4), 4); // 2 spaces + tab to 4\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn test_detect_folds_python() {\n    let doc = Document::with_text(\n        \"def foo():\\n    x = 1\\n    y = 2\\n\\nz = 3\"\n    );\n    let regions = detect_indent_folds(&doc, 4);\n\n    assert_eq!(regions.len(), 1);\n    assert_eq!(regions[0].start_line, 0);\n    assert_eq!(regions[0].end_line, 2);\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn test_is_hidden() {\n    let mut state = FoldingState::default();\n    state.regions.push(FoldRegion {\n        start_line: 5,\n        end_line: 10,\n        level: 0,\n        collapsed: true,\n    });\n\n    assert!(!state.is_hidden(5));  // Start line visible\n    assert!(state.is_hidden(6));   // Inside = hidden\n    assert!(state.is_hidden(10));  // End line hidden\n    assert!(!state.is_hidden(11)); // After = visible\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn test_visual_line_mapping() {\n    let mut state = FoldingState::default();\n    state.regions.push(FoldRegion {\n        start_line: 2,\n        end_line: 5,\n        level: 0,\n        collapsed: true,\n    });\n\n    // Lines: 0, 1, 2, [3,4,5 hidden], 6, 7\n    // Visual: 0, 1, 2, 3, 4\n    assert_eq!(state.doc_to_visual(0), 0);\n    assert_eq!(state.doc_to_visual(2), 2);\n    assert_eq!(state.doc_to_visual(6), 3);\n    assert_eq!(state.doc_to_visual(7), 4);\n\n    assert_eq!(state.visual_to_doc(0), 0);\n    assert_eq!(state.visual_to_doc(2), 2);\n    assert_eq!(state.visual_to_doc(3), 6);\n}\n```\n\n### Manual Testing\n\n- [ ] Fold indicator appears for indented blocks\n- [ ] Click indicator toggles fold\n- [ ] Keyboard toggle works\n- [ ] Fold all/unfold all work\n- [ ] Scrolling works with collapsed folds\n- [ ] Cursor navigation expands folds\n- [ ] Line numbers remain correct\n- [ ] Works with Python, YAML, Rust, JavaScript\n\n---\n\n## References\n\n- [VS Code Folding](https://code.visualstudio.com/docs/editor/codebasics#_folding)\n- [folding-advanced.md](folding-advanced.md) - Next phase: syntax-based folding\n"
}

These features require more complex infrastructure and language-specific knowledge.\n\n### Prerequisites\n\nBefore implementing this phase:\n- ✅ Basic indentation-based folding working\n- ✅ Fold regions, toggle, and rendering complete\n- ✅ Visual line mapping for collapsed folds\n\n### Goals (This Phase)\n\n1. **Syntax-based detection** - Use tree-sitter to find functions, classes, etc.\n2. **Region markers** - Support `#region`/`#endregion` comments\n3. **Fold level commands** - Fold to level 1, 2, 3, etc.\n4. **Fold persistence** - Remember fold state across sessions\n5. **Manual folds** - Create custom fold from selection\n\n---\n\n## Features\n\n### 1. Syntax-Based Detection\n\nUse tree-sitter parse tree to detect language constructs:\n\n```rust\n// Rust: functions, impls, structs, enums, mods, match arms\nfn detect_rust_folds(tree: &Tree) -> Vec<FoldRegion> {\n    let foldable = [\"function_item\", \"impl_item\", \"struct_item\",\n                    \"enum_item\", \"mod_item\", \"match_expression\"];\n    // Walk tree, find nodes...\n}\n\n// JavaScript: functions, classes, if/for/while blocks\nfn detect_js_folds(tree: &Tree) -> Vec<FoldRegion> {\n    let foldable = [\"function_declaration\", \"class_declaration\",\n                    \"method_definition\", \"if_statement\", \"for_statement\"];\n    // Walk tree, find nodes...\n}\n```\n\n**Integration:** Hook into existing syntax highlighting worker to compute folds after parse.\n\n### 2. Region Markers\n\nSupport explicit fold regions via comments:\n\n```rust\n// #region Helper Functions\nfn helper_one() { }\nfn helper_two() { }\n// #endregion\n\n// Also support:\n// <!-- region Name --> / <!-- endregion --> (HTML)\n// # region Name / # endregion (Python)\n```\n\n```rust\nfn detect_region_markers(document: &Document) -> Vec<FoldRegion> {\n    let patterns = [\n        (r\"//\\s*#region\\b\", r\"//\\s*#endregion\\b\"),   // C-style\n        (r\"<!--\\s*region\\b\", r\"<!--\\s*endregion\"),   // HTML\n        (r\"#\\s*region\\b\", r\"#\\s*endregion\\b\"),       // Python/Ruby\n    ];\n    // Match and pair markers...\n}\n```\n\n### 3. Fold Level Commands\n\nFold to a specific nesting depth:\n\n| Command | Behavior |\n|---------|----------|\n| Fold Level 1 | Fold only top-level regions |\n| Fold Level 2 | Fold level 1 + level 2 regions |\n| Fold Level 3 | Fold level 1, 2, and 3 |\n\n```rust\npub fn fold_to_level(&mut self, max_level: usize) {\n    for region in &mut self.regions {\n        region.collapsed = region.level < max_level;\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 4. Fold Persistence\n\nSave fold state per file:\n\n```json\n// ~/.config/token-editor/fold-state.json\n{\n    \"/path/to/file.rs\": {\n        \"version\": 1,\n        \"collapsed_lines\": [10, 45, 120],\n        \"manual_folds\": [\n            { \"start\": 50, \"end\": 55 }\n        ],\n        \"timestamp\": \"2025-12-20T10:00:00Z\"\n    }\n}\n```\n\n**Restore logic:**\n1. Load fold state on file open\n2. Match collapsed lines to current fold regions\n3. Apply collapsed state\n4. Save on file close or explicit save\n\n### 5. Manual Folds\n\nCreate fold from selection:\n\n```rust\npub fn create_manual_fold(&mut self, start_line: usize, end_line: usize) {\n    if end_line > start_line {\n        self.regions.push(FoldRegion {\n            start_line,\n            end_line,\n            level: 0,\n            collapsed: true,\n            source: FoldSource::Manual,\n        });\n        self.regions.sort_by_key(|r| r.start_line);\n    }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Data Structure Extensions\n\n```rust\n/// Extended FoldRegion with source tracking\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct FoldRegion {\n    pub start_line: usize,\n    pub end_line: usize,\n    pub level: usize,\n    pub collapsed: bool,\n    pub source: FoldSource,          // NEW\n    pub summary: Option<String>,     // NEW: e.g., \"fn main()\" \n}\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub enum FoldSource {\n    Indentation,\n    Syntax,\n    RegionMarker,\n    Manual,\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Keybindings\n\n| Action | Mac | Windows/Linux | Command |\n|--------|-----|---------------|---------|\n| Fold Level 1 | `Cmd+K Cmd+1` | `Ctrl+K Ctrl+1` | `FoldLevel(1)` |\n| Fold Level 2 | `Cmd+K Cmd+2` | `Ctrl+K Ctrl+2` | `FoldLevel(2)` |\n| Fold Level 3 | `Cmd+K Cmd+3` | `Ctrl+K Ctrl+3` | `FoldLevel(3)` |\n| Fold Selection | `Cmd+K Cmd+[` | `Ctrl+K Ctrl+[` | `FoldSelection` |\n\n---\n\n## Implementation Plan\n\n### Phase 1: Syntax-Based Detection\n\n**Effort:** M (3-4 days)\n\n- [ ] Add `FoldSource` enum to `FoldRegion`\n- [ ] Implement `detect_syntax_folds()` for each language\n- [ ] Hook into syntax worker to compute folds after parse\n- [ ] Merge syntax folds with indentation folds (prefer syntax)\n\n### Phase 2: Region Markers\n\n**Effort:** S (1-2 days)\n\n- [ ] Implement `detect_region_markers()` with regex patterns\n- [ ] Support C-style, HTML, and Python/Ruby comment markers\n- [ ] Add to fold detection pipeline\n\n### Phase 3: Fold Level Commands\n\n**Effort:** S (1 day)\n\n- [ ] Implement `fold_to_level()` method\n- [ ] Add `FoldLevel(n)` command and keybindings\n- [ ] Add to command palette\n\n### Phase 4: Fold Persistence\n\n**Effort:** M (2-3 days)\n\n- [ ] Design fold state JSON schema\n- [ ] Implement save/load functions\n- [ ] Save fold state on file close\n- [ ] Restore fold state on file open\n- [ ] Handle stale state (file changed)\n\n### Phase 5: Manual Folds\n\n**Effort:** S (1 day)\n\n- [ ] Implement `create_manual_fold()` from selection\n- [ ] Add `FoldSelection` command\n- [ ] Persist manual folds separately\n\n---\n\n## Dependencies\n\n- Requires [folding-basic.md](folding-basic.md) to be complete\n- Reuses existing tree-sitter infrastructure from syntax highlighting\n\n---\n\n## References\n\n- [VS Code Folding Regions](https://code.visualstudio.com/docs/editor/codebasics#_folding)\n- [JetBrains Custom Folding](https://www.jetbrains.com/help/idea/working-with-source-code.html#folding_comments)\n- {
  "path": "~/code/rust-editor/docs/feature/gesture-bindings.md",
  "content": "# Double-Tap Modifier Keybindings\n\nSupport repeated modifiers in key specifications (`alt+alt+up`)\n\n> **Status:** 📋 Planned\n> **Priority:** P2 (Important)\n> **Effort:** S (1-2 days)\n> **Created:** 2025-12-19\n> **Updated:** 2025-12-20\n> **Milestone:** 3 - Keybinding Enhancements\n\n---\n\n## Overview\n\n### Current State\n\nThe editor currently has a hardcoded implementation for `Option+Option+Arrow` (double-tap Option, then arrow key) to add cursors above/below. This works but:\n\n- Cannot be reconfigured or disabled\n- Not expressed in keymap.yaml\n- Requires special handling that bypasses the keymap system\n\n### Goal\n\nAllow keybindings like `alt+alt+up` in keymap.yaml where repeating a modifier means \"double-tap that modifier\":\n\n```yaml\n# keymap.yaml\n- key: alt+alt+up\n  command: AddCursorAbove\n\n- key: alt+alt+down\n  command: AddCursorBelow\n```\n\n### Non-Goals\n\n- Configurable timing window (hardcode 300ms)\n- Triple-tap or more\n- Modifier-only bindings (must end with a key)\n- Complex gesture sequences\n\n---\n\n## Design\n\n### Core Insight\n\nThe key insight is simple: when parsing a key specification like `alt+alt+up`, if we see the same modifier twice, it means \"that modifier was double-tapped.\"\n\nAt match time, we check if the modifier is currently held AND was double-tapped (based on timing).\n\n### Keystroke Extension\n\n```rust\n// src/keymap/types.rs\n\n/// A parsed keystroke from config\npub struct Keystroke {\n    pub key: KeyCode,\n    pub mods: Modifiers,\n    pub double_tap: Option<ModifierKey>,  // NEW: Which modifier must be double-tapped\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub enum ModifierKey {\n    Alt,\n    Ctrl,\n    Shift,\n    Cmd,\n}\n```\n\n### Gesture State (Already Exists)\n\nThe existing code already tracks double-tap state:\n\n```rust\n// src/runtime/app.rs (existing)\npub struct App {\n    last_option_press: Option<Instant>,\n    option_double_tapped: bool,\n    // ...\n}\n```\n\nWe just need to:\n1. Generalize to track all modifiers\n2. Expose this state to the keymap matching\n\n### Generalized Gesture Tracking\n\n```rust\n// src/runtime/app.rs\n\nconst DOUBLE_TAP_WINDOW_MS: u64 = 300;\n\n#[derive(Default)]\nstruct ModifierGesture {\n    last_press: Option<Instant>,\n    double_tapped: bool,\n}\n\nimpl ModifierGesture {\n    fn on_press(&mut self) {\n        let now = Instant::now();\n        if let Some(last) = self.last_press {\n            if now.duration_since(last) < Duration::from_millis(DOUBLE_TAP_WINDOW_MS) {\n                self.double_tapped = true;\n            }\n        }\n        self.last_press = Some(now);\n    }\n\n    fn on_release(&mut self) {\n        self.double_tapped = false;\n    }\n}\n\npub struct App {\n    alt_gesture: ModifierGesture,\n    ctrl_gesture: ModifierGesture,\n    shift_gesture: ModifierGesture,\n    cmd_gesture: ModifierGesture,\n    // ...\n}\n\nimpl App {\n    /// Get current double-tap states for keymap matching\n    pub fn gesture_state(&self) -> GestureState {\n        GestureState {\n            alt_double_tap: self.alt_gesture.double_tapped,\n            ctrl_double_tap: self.ctrl_gesture.double_tapped,\n            shift_double_tap: self.shift_gesture.double_tapped,\n            cmd_double_tap: self.cmd_gesture.double_tapped,\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Passed to keymap for matching\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Default)]\npub struct GestureState {\n    pub alt_double_tap: bool,\n    pub ctrl_double_tap: bool,\n    pub shift_double_tap: bool,\n    pub cmd_double_tap: bool,\n}\n```\n\n### Config Parsing\n\n```rust\n// src/keymap/config.rs\n\nfn parse_key_spec(spec: &str) -> Result<Keystroke, ParseError> {\n    let parts: Vec<&str> = spec.split('+').collect();\n\n    let mut mods = Modifiers::default();\n    let mut double_tap: Option<ModifierKey> = None;\n    let mut seen_modifiers: Vec<&str> = Vec::new();\n\n    // Parse all but last part as modifiers\n    for part in &parts[..parts.len() - 1] {\n        let lower = part.to_lowercase();\n\n        // Check for repeated modifier (double-tap)\n        if seen_modifiers.contains(&lower.as_str()) {\n            double_tap = Some(match lower.as_str() {\n                \"alt\" | \"option\" => ModifierKey::Alt,\n                \"ctrl\" | \"control\" => ModifierKey::Ctrl,\n                \"shift\" => ModifierKey::Shift,\n                \"cmd\" | \"meta\" | \"super\" => ModifierKey::Cmd,\n                _ => return Err(ParseError::InvalidModifier(lower)),\n            });\n        } else {\n            seen_modifiers.push(part);\n            match lower.as_str() {\n                \"alt\" | \"option\" => mods.alt = true,\n                \"ctrl\" | \"control\" => mods.ctrl = true,\n                \"shift\" => mods.shift = true,\n                \"cmd\" | \"meta\" | \"super\" => mods.cmd = true,\n                _ => return Err(ParseError::InvalidModifier(lower)),\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Last part is the key\n    let key = parse_key_code(parts.last().unwrap())?;\n\n    Ok(Keystroke { key, mods, double_tap })\n}\n```\n\n### Keymap Matching\n\n```rust\n// src/keymap/keymap.rs\n\nimpl Keymap {\n    pub fn lookup(\n        &self,\n        keystroke: &Keystroke,\n        context: &KeyContext,\n        gesture: &GestureState,\n    ) -> Option<&Command> {\n        for binding in &self.bindings {\n            if self.matches(binding, keystroke, context, gesture) {\n                return Some(&binding.command);\n            }\n        }\n        None\n    }\n\n    fn matches(\n        &self,\n        binding: &Keybinding,\n        keystroke: &Keystroke,\n        context: &KeyContext,\n        gesture: &GestureState,\n    ) -> bool {\n        // Check key and modifiers\n        if binding.keystroke.key != keystroke.key {\n            return false;\n        }\n        if binding.keystroke.mods != keystroke.mods {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        // Check double-tap requirement\n        if let Some(mod_key) = binding.keystroke.double_tap {\n            let is_double_tapped = match mod_key {\n                ModifierKey::Alt => gesture.alt_double_tap,\n                ModifierKey::Ctrl => gesture.ctrl_double_tap,\n                ModifierKey::Shift => gesture.shift_double_tap,\n                ModifierKey::Cmd => gesture.cmd_double_tap,\n            };\n            if !is_double_tapped {\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Check context conditions\n        self.check_conditions(&binding.when, context)\n    }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Default Bindings\n\n```yaml\n# keymap.yaml - move hardcoded behavior to config\n\n# Double-tap Option + Arrow for multi-cursor\n- key: alt+alt+up\n  command: AddCursorAbove\n\n- key: alt+alt+down\n  command: AddCursorBelow\n```\n\n---\n\n## Implementation Plan\n\n### Phase 1: Generalize Gesture Tracking\n\n**Effort:** S (half day)\n\n- [ ] Create `ModifierGesture` struct\n- [ ] Add gesture tracking for Alt, Ctrl, Shift, Cmd in `App`\n- [ ] Create `GestureState` struct for passing to keymap\n- [ ] Update keyboard event handler to track all modifiers\n\n**Test:** Log gesture state, verify double-tap detection works.\n\n### Phase 2: Extend Keystroke Parsing\n\n**Effort:** S (half day)\n\n- [ ] Add `double_tap: Option<ModifierKey>` to `Keystroke`\n- [ ] Update `parse_key_spec()` to detect repeated modifiers\n- [ ] Add unit tests for parsing `alt+alt+up`\n\n**Test:** Parse \"alt+alt+up\", verify `double_tap = Some(Alt)`.\n\n### Phase 3: Update Keymap Matching\n\n**Effort:** S (half day)\n\n- [ ] Pass `GestureState` to `Keymap::lookup()`\n- [ ] Add double-tap check in `matches()`\n- [ ] Update all call sites to pass gesture state\n\n**Test:** Binding with `alt+alt+up` only matches when Alt double-tapped.\n\n### Phase 4: Remove Hardcoded Logic\n\n**Effort:** S (half day)\n\n- [ ] Remove `skip_keymap` check in `input.rs`\n- [ ] Remove hardcoded Arrow handling for double-tap\n- [ ] Add default bindings to `keymap.yaml`\n- [ ] Verify existing behavior preserved\n\n**Test:** Double-tap Option + Arrow still works, now via keymap.\n\n---\n\n## Testing\n\n### Unit Tests\n\n```rust\n#[test]\nfn test_parse_double_tap() {\n    let ks = parse_key_spec(\"alt+alt+up\").unwrap();\n    assert_eq!(ks.key, KeyCode::ArrowUp);\n    assert!(ks.mods.alt);\n    assert_eq!(ks.double_tap, Some(ModifierKey::Alt));\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn test_parse_no_double_tap() {\n    let ks = parse_key_spec(\"alt+up\").unwrap();\n    assert_eq!(ks.key, KeyCode::ArrowUp);\n    assert!(ks.mods.alt);\n    assert_eq!(ks.double_tap, None);\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn test_match_requires_double_tap() {\n    let binding = Keybinding {\n        keystroke: Keystroke {\n            key: KeyCode::ArrowUp,\n            mods: Modifiers { alt: true, ..Default::default() },\n            double_tap: Some(ModifierKey::Alt),\n        },\n        command: Command::AddCursorAbove,\n        when: vec![],\n    };\n\n    let input = Keystroke {\n        key: KeyCode::ArrowUp,\n        mods: Modifiers { alt: true, ..Default::default() },\n        double_tap: None,\n    };\n\n    // Without double-tap state, should NOT match\n    let gesture_no = GestureState::default();\n    assert!(!keymap.matches(&binding, &input, &context, &gesture_no));\n\n    // With double-tap state, SHOULD match\n    let gesture_yes = GestureState { alt_double_tap: true, ..Default::default() };\n    assert!(keymap.matches(&binding, &input, &context, &gesture_yes));\n}\n```\n\n### Manual Testing\n\n- [ ] Double-tap Option + Up/Down adds cursors (existing behavior)\n- [ ] Single Option + Up/Down does NOT add cursors\n- [ ] Can remap to `ctrl+ctrl+up` in user keymap\n- [ ] Can disable with `command: Unbound`\n- [ ] Timing window feels right (~300ms)\n\n---\n\n## Migration\n\nExisting users will get the same behavior automatically via the default bindings. No breaking changes.\n\n---\n\n## Files to Modify\n\n| File | Changes |\n|------|---------|\n| `src/runtime/app.rs` | Add `ModifierGesture` for all modifiers, expose `gesture_state()` |\n| `src/keymap/types.rs` | Add `double_tap: Option<ModifierKey>` to `Keystroke` |\n| `src/keymap/config.rs` | Parse `alt+alt+up` syntax |\n| `src/keymap/keymap.rs` | Check gesture state in `matches()` |\n| `src/runtime/input.rs` | Remove hardcoded double-tap handling |\n| `keymap.yaml` | Add `alt+alt+up/down` default bindings |\n\n---\n\n## References\n\n- [Current implementation](../../src/runtime/app.rs#L404-421) - Existing gesture detection\n- [Keymapping System](../archived/KEYMAPPING_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md) - Keymap architecture\n"

#### 1. Markdown Preview - Rewritten
**[docs/feature/markdown-preview.md](~/code/rust-editor/docs/feature/markdown-preview.md)**

- Scroll sync via simple JS callbacks (10 lines)
- Theme integration by generating CSS from editor theme
- Effort reduced from L → M (3-5 days)

**Phase 2:** [docs/feature/folding-advanced.md](~/code/rust-editor/docs/feature/folding-advanced.md)
